### [1] [AT2025agpz in Rubin commissioning data: distinguishing a luminous interacting supernova from nuclear transients in compact galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05318)
*C. R. Angus,M. Quilt,H. F. Stevance,M. Nicholl,S. J. Smartt,P. Wiseman,A. Möller,C. T. Murphey,P. J. Pessi,K. Auchettl,M. Dennefeld,M. Dominik,C. Frohmaier,P. Francis,M. Gromadzki,A. Lawrence,G. Leloudas,C. Lidman,D. Magill,I. Mandel,B. Martin,S. Mattila,G. Narayan,F. Onori,S. R. Oates,H. M. L. Perkins,L. Rauf,A. Rest,S. Romagnoli,R. Roy,S. Schulze,X. Sheng,K. W. Smith,K. de Soto,P. M. Veres,M. E. Verrico,A. Wasserman,R. Williams,Ł. Wyrzykowski,D. R. Young*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了AT2025agpz，一个位于z=0.147、与矮星系核重合的明亮瞬变事件，由ATLAS发现并由Rubin天文台委托观测的Euclid Deep Field South数据密切监测。该事件静止系上升时间77.9天，峰值光度6.3×10^43 erg/s，早期幂律上升指数n≈3.33，光谱呈现窄Balmer发射线和电子散射翼特征，表明其为相互作用驱动的明亮超新星，而非核瞬变事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rubin天文台的LSST巡天将发现大量罕见的长寿命光学瞬变，其中许多过于暗淡或数量过多而无法进行全面的光谱后续观测。因此，刻画那些处于已建立瞬变类别边界之间的天体，对于改善光度分类至关重要。

Method: 利用Rubin委托观测数据（深度和节奏优异）结合DECam互补成像，精确约束爆炸历元并拟合早期幂律上升（n=3.33）；通过后续光谱揭示缓慢的光谱演化，使用高分辨率X-shooter光谱解析Hα电子散射宽翼和多发射线成分；对宿主星系进行SED建模以确定其低质量、中等恒星形成特征。

Result: AT2025agpz在观测参数空间上与最近识别的Ambiguous Nuclear Transient群体重叠，但其颜色演化、宿主环境和发射线形态更倾向于相互作用驱动的明亮超新星。事件展示了明亮相互作用超新星与核瞬变之间日益增长的观测重叠。

Conclusion: 该事件凸显了Rubin时代瞬变分类面临的挑战和机遇，表明随着巡天数据的深入，不同类型瞬变之间的边界变得更加模糊，需要综合多维度观测信息来区分相互作用超新星与核瞬变。

Abstract: The Vera C. Rubin Observatory's Legacy Survey of Space and Time will discover unprecedented numbers of rare, long-lived optical transients, many of which will be too faint or too numerous for comprehensive spectroscopic follow-up. Characterising objects that span the boundaries between established transient classes is therefore essential for improving photometric classification. We present AT2025agpz, a luminous transient at $z=0.147$, discovered around peak by ATLAS but extensively monitored during Rubin commissioning observations of the Euclid Deep Field South. The transient is coincident with the nucleus of a faint dwarf galaxy, exhibits a rest-frame rise time of $77.9\pm1.3$ d, and reaches a peak bolometric luminosity of $6.3\times10^{43}$ erg s$^{-1}$. The exceptional depth and cadence of the Rubin commissioning data, combined with complementary DECam imaging, tightly constrain the explosion epoch and yield an early-time power-law rise of $n=3.33^{+0.33}_{-0.28}$. Follow-up spectroscopy reveals remarkably slow spectral evolution dominated by narrow Balmer emission, while high-resolution X-shooter observations resolve broad electron-scattering wings and multiple H$α$ emission components characteristic of interaction-powered supernovae. Spectral energy distribution modelling indicates a low-mass, moderately star-forming host galaxy. Although AT2025agpz occupies observational parameter space shared by the recently identified Ambiguous Nuclear Transient population, its colour evolution, host environment, and emission-line morphology favour an interaction-powered luminous supernova. More broadly, this event highlights the growing observational overlap between luminous interacting supernovae and nuclear transients, illustrating both the challenges and opportunities for transient classification in the Rubin era.

</details>


### [2] [Search for an all-sky and a Galactic Ridge diffuse neutrino emission with the first 2 years of KM3NeT/ARCA data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06163)
*The KM3NeT Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了利用KM3NeT/ARCA探测器早期配置（6、8、19、21个探测单元，总运行时间640天）对弥散天体物理中微子通量的全天区及银河脊区域的搜寻结果。全天分析在单幂律假设下拟合出单味天体物理中微子通量参数φ₀=3.0×10⁻¹⁸ GeV⁻¹cm⁻²s⁻¹sr⁻¹，谱指数γ=3.00；银河脊分析当前灵敏度不足以约束通量参数。两种分析均给出了上限并与最新测量结果进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测弥散天体物理中微子通量是理解宇宙射线起源和高能天体物理过程的关键。本文旨在利用KM3NeT/ARCA探测器早期运行数据，对全天区及银河脊区域进行中微子通量的搜索与测量，验证探测器性能并为未来更高灵敏度测量奠定基础。

Method: 利用KM3NeT/ARCA探测器的第一代配置（6、8、19和21个活跃探测单元）所收集的数据，总有效观测时间为640天。采用单幂律假设对全天区弥散天体物理中微子通量进行参数拟合（贝叶斯框架，给出68%可信区间），同时对银河脊区域进行针对性分析，并推导两种分析的上限。

Result: 全天分析得到单味天体物理中微子通量φ₀¹ᶠ=3.0⁺²·¹₋₂·₀×10⁻¹⁸ GeV⁻¹cm⁻²s⁻¹sr⁻¹，谱指数γ=3.00⁺⁰·³⁰₋₀·₃₅（68%可信水平）。银河脊区域的拟合在当前灵敏度下无法对通量参数给出约束。两种分析均给出了上限并与最新测量结果进行了比较。

Conclusion: 当前数据集的分析虽未产生统计学上显著的结果，但所开发的分析方法为未来KM3NeT探测器（更高灵敏度和更完整配置）的中微子测量提供了坚实基础。全天区结果与现有测量一致，银河脊探测能力有待探测器规模扩大后进一步提升。

Abstract: We report on the search performed for a diffuse astrophysical neutrino flux from the full sky and from a specific region of the Galactic plane, the Galactic Ridge. The study uses the dataset collected with the first KM3NeT/ARCA configurations with 6, 8, 19, and 21 active detection units, corresponding to a total livetime of 640 days. For the all-sky analysis, the fitted single-flavour astrophysical neutrino flux parameters, under the single power-law assumption, are $φ_0^{1f} = 3.0^{+2.1}_{-2.0} \times 10^{-18}$ GeV$^{-1}$ cm$^{-2}$ s$^{-1}$ sr$^{-1}$ with spectral index $γ= 3.00^{+0.30}_{-0.35}$ at 68% credible level. The Galactic Ridge fit does not yield constraints for the flux parameters with the current sensitivity. For both analyses upper limits are derived and compared with state-of-the-art measurements. While the analysis of these datasets has not yielded statistically significant results, the developed methods provide a solid basis for future measurements with the KM3NeT detector.

</details>


### [3] [Reconstructing Core-Collapse Supernova Gravitational-Wave Signals with Transdimensional Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05456)
*Hayden Chapman,Jade Powell,Nir Guttman,Yi Shuen Christine Lee,Paul D. Lasky*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究使用跨维度贝叶斯推理框架tBilby重建核心坍缩超新星（CCSNe）的引力波信号，在合成Advanced LIGO噪声中实现了最高85%的重建重叠度，并证明了即使重建质量较低时仍能提取原中子星的演化信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCSNe是未来引力波探测器的重要信号源，但其信号具有随机性、复杂特征和宽频带等特点，给信号重建带来挑战。信号的随机性尤其需要与形态无关的重建技术，以便在观测后推断新生原中子星的性质、旋转和未知的爆炸机制。

Method: 使用跨维度贝叶斯推理框架tBilby对CCSNe引力波信号进行形态无关重建；在合成Advanced LIGO探测器噪声中注入模拟信号，覆盖一系列信噪比；使用两种小波类型（sine Gaussians和chirplets）进行信号重建。

Result: 注入信号与重建信号之间的重叠度最高可达85%。即使重叠值较低，所捕获的时频结构仍足以对演化中原中子星的尺寸做出推断。

Conclusion: tBilby被确立为引力波天文学中突发源（burst sources）信号重建的强大工具。

Abstract: Core-collapse supernovae (CCSNe) are promising future sources of gravitational waves for current and next-generation observatories. Reconstructing CCSN gravitational-wave signals is challenging as they contain stochastic elements, have multiple complex features, and cover a wide frequency band. The stochasticity of the signal in particular motivates the need for morphology-independent reconstruction techniques which, once observed, will enable us to infer properties of the newly born proto-neutron star, the rotation, and the unknown CCSN explosion mechanism. In this work, we investigate the reconstruction of gravitational-wave signals from CCSNe using the transdimensional Bayesian inference framework tBilby. We demonstrate the method using simulated signals in synthetic Advanced LIGO detector noise at a range of signal-to-noise ratios. We reconstruct the signals using two types of wavelets: sine Gaussians and chirplets. We calculate overlaps between injected and reconstructed signals of up to 85%. We find that even when reconstruction overlap values are low, enough of the time-frequency structure of the dominant mode is captured to still make statements about the size of the evolving proto-neutron star. These capabilities establish tBilby as a powerful tool for gravitational-wave astronomy with burst sources.

</details>


### [4] [A Roadmap for Transient Hunters: Mapping Stellar Mass and Star Formation Rate Anisotropies in the Local Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05531)
*Ye-Hao Cheng,Yuan-Pei Yang,Ye Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究利用大型星系目录绘制了不同光度距离阈值和天球角分辨率下的恒星质量与恒星形成率（SFR）的天球分布图，用于刻画近邻河外暂现源的各向异性空间分布。研究发现恒星质量与SFR的天球分布在主要各向异性结构上相似，随光度距离阈值增大而趋于各向同性，角功率谱和涨落迅速下降；并定性验证了核坍缩超新星与SFR天球分布在大尺度结构上的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近邻宇宙中的暂现源在天球分布上呈现显著的各向异性，不同于宇宙学距离上的天体。为了提高近邻暂现源的搜寻效率，需要针对这种各向异性设计合适的巡天策略，因此需要先绘制并理解恒星质量和SFR的天空分布特征。

Method: 利用大型星系目录，在不同光度距离阈值和不同天球网格角分辨率下，分别映射恒星质量和SFR的天球分布；计算给定角分辨率下的角功率谱和涨落以量化各向异性程度；最后定性比较核坍缩超新星与SFR天球分布的一致性。

Result: 恒星质量和SFR的主要各向异性结构在天球分布上相似；随着光度距离阈值的增大，天球分布趋于各向同性，角功率谱和涨落快速下降；核坍缩超新星的天球分布与SFR天球分布在大尺度结构上表现出一致的模式。

Conclusion: 所绘制的恒星质量和SFR天球分布图可作为未来河外暂现源巡天的重要参考，有助于针对近邻宇宙的各向异性分布优化巡天策略，提升暂现源搜寻效率。

Abstract: Over the past few decades, an increasing number of transients in nearby galaxies have been discovered through various survey projects. Unlike astrophysical phenomena at cosmological distances, transients in the local universe exhibit a pronounced anisotropy in their sky distribution. Consequently, adopting an appropriate survey strategy is essential to improve the efficiency of transient searches in the local universe. In this work, we utilized a large galaxy catalog to map the sky distributions of stellar mass and star formation rate (SFR) across different luminosity distance thresholds and angular resolutions of the grid on the celestial sphere. These maps can further serve to characterize the anisotropic spatial distribution of nearby extragalactic transients. For different angular resolutions of the celestial sphere, we find that the sky distributions of stellar mass of galaxies are similar to those of the SFR in the main anisotropic structures. As the luminosity distance threshold increases, the anisotropic structures of the sky distributions become more isotropic. We calculate the angular power spectra and fluctuations of the sky distribution of stellar mass and SFR at a given angular resolution and find that the angular power spectra and fluctuations decrease rapidly as the luminosity distance threshold increases. Finally, by qualitatively comparing the sky distribution of core-collapse supernovae with our SFR sky distribution, we find that the two exhibit consistent patterns in several prominent structures. The mapped sky distributions of stellar mass and SFR can serve as valuable references for future surveys in searching for extragalactic transients.

</details>


### [5] [No evidence for a supermassive black hole binary in GSN 069](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05534)
*Yuhe Zeng,Zhen Pan,Bin Liu,Cong Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何利用准周期爆发（QPEs）的计时信息来探测更遥远的超大质量黑洞伴星。作者提出了两种互补的诊断方法：一是通过EMRI宿主SMBH绕双SMBH质心运动引起的渡越时间调制，二是通过恒星轨道器的存活约束排除伴星参数空间，并以GSN 069为例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QPEs源于恒星质量天体（SMO）绕超大质量黑洞（SMBH）运动并反复穿越吸积盘时产生的软X射线爆发。这种爆发计时可以追踪EMRI轨道运动，作者想知道能否利用这些计时信息来探测更远处的SMBH伴星（即SMBHB系统），从而揭示隐藏在邻近星系核中的不可见SMBH伴星。

Method: 作者发展了两种互补的诊断手段：（1）EMRI宿主SMBH绕双SMBH系统的质心运动会在观测QPE到达时间中引入光行时间调制，特别表现为奇偶次爆发到达时间的同相调制；（2）如果QPE源包含存活的恒星轨道器，外部SMBH不能通过von Zeipel-Lidov-Kozai（ZLK）机制的偏心率激发将SMO驱动到潮汐瓦解，以此约束外部SMBH的参数空间。

Result: 以GSN 069为例，在QPE计时中没有检测到同相调制（即没有发现SMBHB的证据），并据此给出了伴星SMBH被排除的参数空间。

Conclusion: QPE计时和恒星存活提供了约束邻近星系核中隐藏SMBH伴星的互补途径，为探测SMBHB系统提供了新的时域探针。

Abstract: Quasi-periodic eruptions (QPEs) are recurrent soft X-ray flares from galactic nuclei and provide a new time-domain probe of stellar-mass objects (SMOs) orbiting supermassive black holes (SMBHs). In an extreme-mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) system interacting with an accretion disk, QPEs are produced when the SMO repeatedly crosses an accretion disk, so that the eruption times trace the orbital motion of the EMRI. We investigate whether such timing information can be used to probe a more distant SMBH companion. We develop two complementary diagnostics: (1) the motion of the EMRI host SMBH around the SMBH-binary (SMBHB) center of mass induces a light-travel-time modulation in the observed QPE arrival times, specifically an \emph{in-phase} modulation in arrival times of even and odd eruptions; (2) if the QPE source contains a surviving stellar orbiter, the external SMBH must not drive the SMO into tidal disruption through eccentricity excitation by the von Zeipel--Lidov--Kozai (ZLK) mechanism. Using GSN 069 as an example, we find \emph{no} in-phase modulation in the QPE timing (i.e., no evidence for a SMBHB) and constrain the excluded parameter space of the companion SMBH. These results demonstrate that QPE timing and stellar survival offer complementary routes for constraining otherwise hidden SMBH companions in nearby galactic nuclei.

</details>


### [6] [Influence of effective mass of the relativistic mean field theory on core collapse supernovae and compact objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05582)
*Kohsuke Sumiyoshi,Hajime Togashi,Shun Furusawa,Shuying Li,Hong Shen,Ken'ichiro Nakazato,Hideyuki Suzuki*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在相对论平均场(RMF)框架下研究有效质量对坍缩驱动超新星中心核性质及致密天体形成的影响，通过具有不同有效质量但相同饱和性质的TM1e和TM1m状态方程表，结合引力坍缩、弹回及原中子星冷却的数值模拟，发现大有效质量EOS导致致密原中子星及早坍缩为黑洞，并产生高能中微子长脉冲和短暴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效质量对超新星核心的影响以往仅在非相对论框架下研究，而相对论框架中有效质量的角色与之不同，因此需要澄清其在RMF框架下的作用，以更准确理解超新星爆炸机制和致密天体形成过程。

Method: 采用RMF理论中参数化TM1e和TM1m两组具有不同有效质量但相同饱和性质的EOS表；对质量恒星的引力坍缩、弹回以及原中子星冷却过程进行数值模拟，对比中微子发射和物质演化差异。

Result: 有效质量选择同时影响EOS的刚度（通过压力）和热力学行为（通过温度）。大有效质量EOS导致原中子星更致密、密度和温度更高时更早坍缩为黑洞，并产生来自冷却过程的长时间高能中微子发射和黑洞形成时的短促中微子暴。

Conclusion: 在RMF框架下，有效质量是决定超新星核心演化、原中子星冷却路径及黑洞形成时机和中微子信号特征的重要物理量，对致密天体形成和可观测的中微子信号有显著影响。

Abstract: We study the influence of the effective mass in the relativistic mean field (RMF) theory on the properties of the central core of collapse-driven supernovae and the formation of compact objects. Influence of the effective mass has been so far studied within the non-relativistic frameworks. In order to clarify the role of the effective mass in the relativistic frameworks, which is different from non-relativistic ones, we adopt the set of equation of state (EOS) tables using the parameterizations TM1e and TM1m, which have different effective masses but with the same saturation properties, in the RMF theory. We show that choices of the effective mass in supernova matter affect both the stiffness of the EOS through pressure and the thermodynamical behavior through temperature under the RMF frameworks. We explore differences in matter evolution with neutrino emissions by performing a set of numerical simulations of the gravitational collapse and bounce of massive stars and the cooling of the proto-neutron stars. The EOS with large effective mass leads to compact proto-neutron stars and early collapse to black holes with high densities and temperatures due to the softness. It leads to high energy neutrinos in long emission from the proto-neutron star cooling and in short burst from the black hole formation.

</details>


### [7] [Kinematically Resolving the Fe K Complex in Her X-1: The Accretion Disk and Ionized Wind Across X-ray Eclipses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05719)
*Koh Sakamoto,Teruaki Enoto,Peter Kosec,Takeshi GO Tsuru,Takuto Narita,Daijiro Hitotsuyanagi,Laura Brenneman,Ruediger Staubert,Jon M. Miller,Andrew Fabian,Daniele Rogantini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用XRISM/Resolve的高能量分辨率（5 eV）光谱，对Her X-1在2024年9月三次X射线食期间的铁K复合体进行了详细分析，解开了中性铁荧光线和高度电离发射线等所有主要成分。中性Fe Kα线在食中未被显著探测，表明其起源于中子星附近；而高度电离的Fe XXV和Fe XXVI线在食中仍可见，表明其具有延展起源。通过光致电离建模（SPEX pion模型）和通量比诊断，约束了团块盘风的几何内边界和物理参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Her X-1作为典型的X射线双星系统，其铁K复合体包含了从冷物质到热电离气体的多种发射成分。利用XRISM/Resolve前所未有的5 eV高能量分辨率，通过观测X射线食相位，可以沿视线分离不同空间尺度的发射区，从而厘清铁K复合体各成分的起源区域，并为研究吸积盘风的结构、几何边界和物质输运提供关键约束。

Method: 利用XRISM/Resolve光谱仪对Her X-1进行三次X射线食的时序观测，覆盖进入食、食中和离开食的相位。通过5 eV高分辨率解缠铁K复合体的各发射成分（中性Fe Kα/Kβ荧光线和电离Fe XXV Heα、Fe XXVI Lyα线）。对中性Fe Kα线进行系统速度和轨道运动修正后分析残余速度偏移。采用SPEX的pion光致电离模型拟合谱线，并通过谱线通量比诊断约束盘风的几何内边界。

Abstract: We present XRISM/Resolve spectroscopy of Her X-1 across three X-ray eclipses observed in September 2024, resolving its iron K complex through the ingress, mid-eclipse, and egress phases. The neutral Fe K$α$ emission is not significantly detected during mid-eclipse, indicating a compact origin near the neutron star. At ingress and egress, the line centroid exhibits red- and blue-shifts of $\sim 200$ km s$^{-1}$ after correcting for the systemic velocity and the neutron star's orbital motion. This residual shift corresponds to Keplerian rotation at a characteristic radius of $r_{\rm disk} \sim 6.6\times10^{6}$ km, suggesting an association with the outer accretion disk. In contrast, the highly ionized Fe XXV He$α$ and Fe XXVI Ly$α$ lines remain visible during eclipses, indicating an extended origin. Photoionization modeling (SPEX pion model) yields $\log_{10}(ξ/{\rm erg\,cm\,s^{-1}}) \sim 3.4$ and $N_{\rm H} \sim 3.1\times10^{22}$ cm$^{-2}$ consistent with the ionized disk wind of Her X-1. Flux-ratio diagnostics constrain the geometric inner boundary of the clumpy disk wind to $R_{\rm in} = 3^{+5}_{-2} \times 10^{10}$ cm ($1σ$), consistent with the Compton-heated thermal winds. The inferred mass outflow rate is $\dot{M}_{\rm out} \approx 3.2 \times 10^{-9}\,M_{\odot}$ yr$^{-1}$ (half the supplied mass), consistent with absorption line measurements of the disk wind obtained out of eclipse.

In contrast, we find only modest constraints on the BH spins. The improved low-frequency sensitivity also extends the redshift reach of the detector network, enabling events injected at $z_{\rm true}\simeq19.8$ to be confidently identified as originating beyond $z\simeq18.5$ at $90\%$ credibility, compared to a maximum lower-bound redshift of $z\simeq17.5$ for the $10\,\rm Hz$ configuration. Improved detector sensitivity below $10\, \rm Hz$ also reduces the sky-localisation uncertainties, which are essential for cosmological cross-correlation.

Abstract: The gravitational collapse of massive stars at the end of their life leads to powerful supernova explosions that produce compact objects, regulate the dynamics of host galaxies, and contribute to cosmic chemical evolution. In the presence of fast rotation and strong magnetic fields, such explosions can reach extreme energies, explaining sources such as hypernovae and long gamma-ray bursts.

Differences in stiffness, composition, and finite-temperature behavior of the EoS affect the collapse, bounce, and jet-launching phases.
  Using the Aenus-Alcar code, which includes relativistic magnetohydrodynamics, two-moment neutrino transport, neutrino-matter interactions, and general-relativistic corrections, we perform axisymmetric simulations with different EoSs. Our results show that magnetorotational core-collapse supernova signatures depend not only on the cold stiffness of the EoS, but also on its thermal and compositional properties, highlighting the importance of combining gravitational-wave and neutrino observations to constrain dense matter physics and the explosion mechanism.

Abstract: The inspiral and merger of two supermassive black holes (SMBHs) releases immense energy in low-frequency gravitational waves (GWs). Recent pulsar timing array (PTA) observations of the stochastic nHz GW background (GWB) are consistent with a SMBH binary origin. GW data from PTAs and from the upcoming Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) can probe late-stage binary evolution where electromagnetic constraints are scarce. However, the complexity of the relevant astrophysics necessitates a well optimized approach. I argue that a key physical quantity to constrain with PTAs is the orbital semi-major axis at which binary inspiral transitions from the astrophysical to the GW-dominated regime ($a_{\rm GW}$). This quantity should be treated as a free parameter in analysis of the GWB. Using this premise, I present a simple analytic framework for modeling SMBH binary inspiral in an "inside-out" fashion. At orbital separations slightly larger than $a_{\rm GW}$, a power-law scaling for the astrophysical inspiral timescale is assumed, while the outermost phase (prior to the PTA regime) is simply modeled via a delay time. I show that the GWB spectral shape and amplitude are most sensitive to $a_{\rm GW}$ (normalized to $10^9 M_{\odot}$, equal-mass binaries and expressed in gravitational units), with weaker dependence on the inner power-law index and the outer delay time. The GWB is largely insensitive to the mass and mass-ratio scaling of $a_{\rm GW}$ and to the boundary between the inner and outer astrophysical inspiral regimes. I compare with models for gas- and stellar-driven binary inspiral and discuss implications for LISA and for GW source parameter inference.

Abstract: We compute and test analytic models for the plunging region dynamics, thermodynamics, and magnetic fields against dedicated 3D global general relativistic magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) simulations of thin accretion discs around black holes across the spin range, using the code {\tt ATHENAK}. We find that the dynamics of the plunging fluid closely resembles that of a gravity-dominated geodesic plunge, with the best agreement at low spins. Additionally, we find good agreement between the thermodynamic framework and the simulated quantities across the spin range. Finally, we develop a new model for the magnetic fields in the plunging region that assumes a fixed geodesic inflow, into which the magnetic fields are frozen. Overall, our simulations are in good concordance with this model, albeit with some discrepancies that suggest a degree of non-ideal MHD dissipation. In addition, we investigate how the MHD stresses in the plunging region depend on the black hole spin, interpreting our results through the lens of our flux-freezing model. We find that the magnitude of the stress increases as the black hole spin is increased in the prograde direction. This question is of particular importance for observers who wish to determine the black hole spin from X-ray measurements of the inner accretion disc, since a low-stress, high-spin solution is degenerate with a high-stress, low-spin solution. The spin-stress relationship that we report is approximately orthogonal to the contour of degenerate spin-stress pairings, indicating that the degeneracy is not fundamental. We show this explicitly for the case of M33 X-7.

Abstract: Recent X-ray and soft gamma-ray spectro-polarimetric observations of black hole X-ray binaries, such as Cygnus X-1, reveal discrepancies that challenge current models of accretion and high-energy emission. Abstract: We introduce a notion of completeness for two-dimensional second-gradient elastic continua and propose a microstructural route toward its synthesis. Starting from the Principle of Virtual Work, we derive the constitutive relations, equilibrium equations, and admissible boundary interactions for a broad class of fibrous second-gradient continua whose stored energies depend on fiber stretch, stretch gradients, and curvature. The theory is then applied to several continua motivated by pantographic microstructures. Classical pantographic sheets are shown to be incomplete, while bi-pantographic fabrics enlarge the class of components of the second gradient detected by the energy but remain incomplete. Finally, we formulate a tri-pantographic continuum associated with a proposed three-family architecture and prove that it is complete. The examples illustrate how microstructural architecture influences both the completeness properties of an effective continuum and the pointwise form of its higher-order boundary interactions.

This refutes the retract conjecture of Hovey-Palmieri. Our construction also allows us to determine the cardinality of the Bousfield lattice to be $2^{2^{\aleph_0}}$, answering a question of Dwyer-Palmieri.

Abstract: The Intersection Euler Characteristic Profile (Intersection ECP) of $k$ colored point clouds $X_1, \ldots, X_k \subset \mathbb{R}^d$ is the Euler characteristic $χ(\bigcap_{i=1}^k \mathcal{U}(X_i; t_i))$ of the overlap of their ball unions---an integer-valued, multiparameter invariant of their topological interaction across scales. Its organizing framework is the Euler calculus on constructible functions: the profile is equally the Euler integral $\int \prod_{i=1}^k \mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{U}(X_i; t_i)} \, dχ$ of the product of the $k$ data-dependent offsets, and this identity---our Intersection Theorem---is a commuting square interchanging geometric intersection and algebraic product. The invariant is rigid-motion invariant, scale-equivariant, and $L^1$-stable, and it is canonical: among pointwise-Euler interaction profiles it is the one forced by separation and normalization, the top floor of a spectrum of descriptors graded by how many clouds meet. For $n$ points a single sorted Alpha-complex sweep computes it in $O(n^{\lceil d/2 \rceil} \log n)$ time with no persistence reduction, worst-case optimal in even dimensions. Where the Euler characteristic cancels, a relative-homology refinement resolves the finer interaction and is stable in the two-parameter interleaving distance. Finally, for increasingly dense samples the profile and its refinement are consistent, recovering the (relative) homology and Euler characteristic of the underlying shapes---in the inverse limit for compact sets, and, under positive reach, persistently and with explicit sample complexity.

</details>


### [22] [Whitehead's theorem for minimal finite models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06176)

TL;DR: 本文研究了Whitehead定理在最小有限模型中的有效性。作者证明该定理在此设定下不成立，对每个n≥2构造了S^n∨S^{n-1}∨S^{n-1}的两个(2n+4)点最小有限模型，它们之间存在弱同伦等价但非同伦等价，从而否定了Barmak提出的问题。此外，作者给出了一个近极值分类定理，证明了在不超过2n+3个点的连通有限空间中，若第n阶同调在域上非零，其序复形同伦等价于S^n或S^n∨S^k。最后证明了在自然上同调刚性条件下，最小有限模型间的弱同伦等价必为同伦等价的正向Whitehead型结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Whitehead定理断言：连通CW复形之间的弱同伦等价必为同伦等价。但有限空间（有限T0拓扑空间）作为代数拓扑中的离散化研究对象，其上的Whitehead定理是否仍然成立是一个自然且重要的问题。Barmak曾提出该问题，本文旨在探讨最小有限模型（在弱同伦意义下具有极少点数的有限空间）中Whitehead定理的有效性边界。

Method: 本文采用构造性与分类性相结合的方法。首先，针对每个n≥2，构造S^n∨S^{n-1}∨S^{n-1}的两个(2n+4)点最小有限模型，通过分析其同伦不变量证明二者弱同伦等价但非同伦等价，从而给出Whitehead定理失败的反例。其次，利用近极值分类定理——不超过2n+3个点的连通有限空间若在域上第n阶同调非零，其序复形同伦等价于S^n或S^n∨S^k——来确保所构造例子的最小性。最后，引入自然的上同调刚性假设，证明该假设下最小有限模型间的弱同伦等价必是同伦等价。

Result: （1）对每个n≥2，构造了S^n∨S^{n-1}∨S^{n-1}的两个(2n+4)点最小有限模型，之间存在弱同伦等价但非同伦等价，否定了Barmak的问题；（2）证明了近极值分类定理：若连通有限空间至多有2n+3个点且第n阶同调（域系数）非零，则其序复形同伦等价于S^n或S^n∨S^k（1≤k≤n）；（3）证明了在自然上同调刚性条件下，Whitehead型正向结果成立。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了Whitehead定理在最小有限模型中的失效性：一方面通过具体反例证明其一般不成立；另一方面通过上同调刚性假设给出了其成立的充分条件。这一结果不仅回答了Barmak的问题，还为有限空间理论中弱同伦等价与同伦等价的差距提供了精确的刻画。

Abstract: We investigate the extent to which Whitehead's theorem remains valid for minimal finite models. We show that it fails in this setting, answering negatively a question posed by Barmak. More precisely, for every $n\geq 2$, we construct a weak homotopy equivalence between two $(2n+4)$-point minimal finite models of $S^n\vee S^{n-1}\vee S^{n-1}$ which are not homotopy equivalent. The minimality of these examples follows from a near-extremal classification theorem: if a connected finite space has at most $2n+3$ points and nonzero $n$th homology over a field, then its order complex is homotopy equivalent either to $S^n$ or to $S^n\vee S^k$ for some $1\leq k\leq n$. Finally, we prove a positive Whitehead-type result: under a natural cohomological rigidity hypothesis, every weak homotopy equivalence between minimal finite models is a homotopy equivalence.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了外部同伦理论的稳定框架，解决了proper同伦理论中稳定理论发展的范畴性障碍，并通过Brown表示定理展示了该框架在proper上同调理论中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: We develop a stable framework for exterior homotopy theory and apply it to proper homotopy theory. Starting from a known simplicial model structure on the category of exterior spaces we then pass to the proper, cellular model category of retractive exterior spaces over the ray, and establish the associated stable homotopy theory of exterior spectra.
  The resulting framework provides exterior analogues of several classical constructions in stable homotopy theory and sheds new light on proper homotopy theory, where the development of a satisfactory stable theory has been hindered by the lack of suitable categorical properties. As an application, we establish a Brown representability theorem for reduced exterior cohomology theories. This yields representability results for proper cohomology theories arising from the exterior setting, including cohomology theories with compact supports associated with classical generalized cohomology theories.

</details>

### [24] [Search for resonant production of lepton-enriched semivisible jets in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05323)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究利用CMS探测器在LHC上收集的138 fb⁻¹质子-质子碰撞数据（√s=13 TeV），搜索由强耦合暗区产生的富含轻子的半可见喷注（SVJ）的共振产生过程。研究涵盖两种信号场景：所有轻子味富集的SVJℓ和τ轻子为主的SVJτ。采用图神经网络与全连接神经网络的双机器学习策略提升信号灵敏度。结果显示，在95%置信水平下，SVJℓ场景中介子质量最高被排除至4.7 TeV，SVJτ场景中1.8至3.5 TeV区间被排除，这是对富含轻子半可见喷注的首个实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强耦合暗区通过重Z'媒介子产生的富含轻子的半可见喷注信号，填补此前类似搜索仅关注不含轻子或仅含单一轻子类型信号的空白，提供首个对这类信号的实验约束。

Method: 基于CMS探测器138 fb⁻¹数据，针对SVJℓ和SVJτ两种信号场景，聚焦横动量缺失与含非孤立轻子的喷注重合末态。采用双机器学习策略：图神经网络（GNN）用于喷注鉴别，全连接神经网络（FCN）融合喷注级与事件级信息以增强信号灵敏度并优化背景估计。信号模型假设Z'媒介子与标准模型夸克耦合（基准耦合常数0.25），暗强子发生瞬发衰变。

Result: 在95%置信水平下，SVJℓ场景中Z'媒介子质量被排除至4.7 TeV；SVJτ场景中1.8至3.5 TeV质量区间被排除。

Conclusion: 该分析为富含轻子的半可见喷注提供了首个实验约束，展示了双机器学习方法在此类搜索中的有效性，并为后续探索强耦合暗区参数空间奠定了基础。

Abstract: This search targets the resonant production of lepton-enriched semivisible jets (SVJs) from a strongly coupled dark sector, using 138 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data collected with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV. Two scenarios are investigated: jets enriched in all lepton flavors (SVJ$\ell$ signature) and jets predominantly enriched in tau leptons (SVJ$τ$ signature). The analysis focuses on final states in which the missing transverse momentum is aligned with jets containing nonisolated leptons. A dual machine-learning strategy is employed, using a graph neural network for jet identification and a fully connected neural network that combines jet- and event-level information to enhance signal sensitivity and background estimation. The signal models assume a heavy Z' mediator with a benchmark coupling of 0.25 to standard model quarks, together with prompt decays of unstable dark hadrons. In the SVJ$\ell$ scenario, mediator masses up to 4.7 TeV are excluded at 95% confidence level, while masses between 1.8 and 3.5 TeV are excluded in the SVJ$τ$ scenario. These results provide the first experimental constraints on lepton-enriched semivisible jets.

</details>

Method: SPD作为通用探测器，通过测量单自旋和双自旋不对称性来实现物理目标，采用多种互补探针（粲偶素、开粲、瞬发光子产生过程），并在NICA运行初期通过降低亮度和碰撞能量的方式开展极化与非极化的物理研究。

Abstract: The Spin Physics Detector (SPD) is a universal detector at the NICA collider under commissioning at JINR, Dubna. The SPD is intended to study the spin structure of the proton and deuteron and other spin-related phenomena using a unique possibility to operate with polarized proton and deuteron beams at a collision energies up to 27~GeV and a luminosity up to 10$^{32}$cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$. As the primary goal, the experiment aims to provide access to the gluon TMD PDFs in the proton and deuteron, as well as the gluon transversity distribution and tensor PDFs in the deuteron, via the measurements of relevant single and double spin asymmetries using different complementary probes such as charmonia, open charm, and prompt photon production processes. Other polarized and unpolarized physics studies are possible, especially at the first stage of NICA operation with reduced luminosity and collision energy of the proton and ion beams. The physics program of the SPD and the design of the SPD setup will be presented.

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的约8998万个J/ψ事件，搜索了电荷轻子味破坏衰变η'→eμ，未发现显著信号，在90%置信水平下将其衰变分支比上限设定为6.3×10⁻⁷，比此前最优结果改善了近三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻子味破坏过程在标准模型中是被抑制的，因此任何此类衰变的观测都将构成超出标准模型的新物理信号。该研究旨在搜索η'→eμ这一带电轻子味破坏衰变，以寻找新物理的迹象。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在质心能量√s=3.097 GeV处的e⁺e⁻对撞数据，通过J/ψ→γη'过程产生η'介子，然后搜索η'→eμ衰变道。

Result: 未观察到显著信号，在90%置信水平下设定η'→eμ衰变分支比的上限为6.3×10⁻⁷，比之前最好的实验结果改善了约三个数量级。

Conclusion: 在目前的数据统计量下，未发现η'→eμ轻子味破坏衰变的证据，但所设定的上限显著优于此前的实验结果，为约束相关新物理模型提供了更严格的实验限制。

Abstract: Based on $(8998\pm40)\times10^6$ $J/ψ$ events collected in $e^+e^-$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 3.097$ GeV with the BESIII detector, we present a search for the charged lepton flavour violating decay $η'\to eμ$ with $J/ψ\toγη'$. No significant signal is observed, and an upper limit on its decay branching fraction is set to be $6.3\times10^{-7}$ at the 90% confidence level, improving the previous best result by nearly three orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [27] [Quantum noise reduction schemes for KAGRA post-O5 upgrade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06229)
*Yuhang Zhao,Marc Eisenmann,Michael Page,Zong-Hong Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文针对KAGRA探测器post-O5阶段，系统比较了五种量子噪声抑制方案（FIS、FC、AFC、FDBS和EPR），发现在所有频率上FC方案优于AFC和FDBS；当低频噪声以经典噪声为主时FIS方案具有最大BNS探测距离，而当低频噪声以量子噪声为主时FC方案最优；优化滤波腔参数可将BNS探测率提升至少23%，且EPR方案在探测重双星系统时表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子噪声是当前引力波探测器的主要限制噪声源之一，虽然压缩真空已被常规采用，但如何在不同探测器配置（特别是存在悬挂噪声等多级摆噪声）下最优地实现频率依赖压缩以进一步降低量子噪声，仍是一个需要系统比较和优化的问题。

Method: 在KAGRA post-O5背景下，将引力波信号灵敏度分解为噪声预算，考虑多级摆悬挂噪声等经典噪声源，系统比较了五种量子噪声抑制方案：频率无关压缩（FIS）、滤波腔频率依赖压缩（FC）、振幅滤波腔（AFC）、频率依赖分束器（FDBS）以及EPR方案，并对滤波腔参数进行优化以及评估其对臂功率变化和腔内损耗的鲁棒性。

Result: FC方案在所有频率上优于AFC和FDBS方案；当低频噪声以经典噪声为主时FIS给出最大BNS范围，当低频噪声以量子噪声为主时FC方案最优；优化滤波腔参数可显著提升BNS范围，使用85米滤波腔相比FIS方案至少提升23%的探测率；优化后的滤波腔通过调节失谐量可完全补偿臂功率从半值到全值的变化以及不同腔内损耗条件；EPR方案在探测重双星系统时表现最佳。

Conclusion: 在KAGRA post-O5的量子噪声抑制设计中，滤波腔（FC）方案是频率依赖压缩的最优选择，且通过参数优化可显著提升探测性能；EPR方案则更适合重双星系统的探测。滤波腔参数对臂功率和损耗条件具有良好的补偿能力。

Abstract: Quantum noise, arising from the quantisation of electromagnetic field, has been a limiting noise source for current gravitational wave (GW) detectors. Squeezed vacuum modifies quantum fluctuations and has been routinely employed. To reduce quantum noise, the current solution is to combine squeezed vacuum with a detuned over-coupled optical cavity (filter cavity) to achieve frequency-dependent squeezing (FDS). The sensitivity to GW signals can be decomposed into a noise budget. Depending on the detector configuration, the contribution from noise sources other than quantum noise can be significant. In particular, suspension noise from multi-stage pendulums is a key factor in quantum-noise reduction design. In the context of KAGRA post-O5, we have compared quantum noise reduction schemes, frequency-independent squeezing (FIS), FDS with a filter cavity (FC), FDS with an amplitude filter cavity (AFC), FDS with a frequency-dependent beam splitter (FDBS) and EPR scheme. The FC scheme was found to outperform the AFC and FDBS schemes at all frequencies. It was found that FIS scheme gives the largest Binary Neutron Star (BNS) range when low frequency noise is dominated by classical noise, while the FC scheme gives the largest BNS range when low-frequency noise becomes dominated by quantum noise. Optimised filter cavity parameters could substantially improve the BNS range. This would allow at least 23% increase in the detection rate for an 85 m filter cavity, compared with using FIS scheme. Once a filter cavity is constructed with optimised parameters, refining its detuning can fully compensate for the variations in arm power (from half to full design value) and for different intra-cavity loss conditions. The EPR scheme performs best for the detection of heavy binary systems.

</details>

### [28] [Generic spherical unitary dual for Chevalley groups over local fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05767)
*Dan Ciubotaru*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了等参数分次仿射Hecke代数的酉球形参数（generic spherical parameter）是酉的，当且仅当其归一化交织形式在反射表示及其第二对称幂的不可约分量上为正。该结论统一了不同数域上Chevalley群的球形酉对偶，并给出了一个简洁的组合刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解分次仿射Hecke代数的酉表示是理解p-adic群和实约化群酉对偶的核心步骤。本文旨在建立球形参数酉性的一个统一判据，并揭示其对不同基域（实数域、非阿基米德局部域、复数域）上Chevalley群球形酉对偶的独立性的深层影响。

Method: 证明结合了多重技术：反射表示的符号公式（signature formula）、Yu的一致双墙操作（uniform two-wall operation）、单laced情形的Jantzen递推、以及根偏序集分析（root-poset analysis）。在B_n和C_n型中进一步证明只需反射表示V及Sym^2(V)的无迹对角分量。还给出了第二个更短的证明（允许任意Weyl群类型）。

Result: 球形参数酉性等价于交织形式在V和Sym^2(V)的不可约分量上的正性。通过Barbasch-Vogan的petite K-type技巧，推论为：实数和非阿基米德局部域上Chevalley群的通用球形酉对偶与基域无关，对单laced群和复辛群在复数域上亦然。该对偶具有统一描述：基本Weyl房中的仿射Weyl群排列中2的幂个alcove的无交并的Weyl共轭，指数为短余根子系统的Dynkin图的匹配数。

Conclusion: 本文给出了分次仿射Hecke代数球形酉参数的一个简洁而普适的判据，并由此统一了不同数域上Chevalley群的球形酉对偶，揭示了其结构的组合本质（匹配数）与基域无关性。

Abstract: We prove that a generic spherical parameter for the graded affine Hecke algebra with equal parameters is unitary if and only if its normalized intertwining forms are positive on the reflection representation and on the irreducible constituents of its second symmetric power. The proof combines a signature formula for the reflection representation, Yu's uniform two-wall operation, a simply-laced Jantzen recursion, and a root-poset analysis. In types B_n and C_n, we prove more sharply that the reflection representation V and the traceless-diagonal constituent of Sym^2(V) suffice. We also give a shorter second proof when arbitrary Weyl-group types are allowed. The consequence of the first proof (via Barbasch-Vogan petite K-types) is that the generic spherical unitary dual of a Chevalley group over the real numbers or a nonarchimedean local field, and for simply-laced groups and complex symplectic groups also over the complex numbers, is independent of the field. Moreover, the answer has a simple uniform description as the Weyl conjugates of a disjoint union of a power of 2 alcoves in the affine Weyl group arrangement in the fundamental Weyl chamber, where the exponent is the matching number of the Dynkin diagram of the subsystem of short coroots.

</details>

### [29] [VLAff: Vision-Language-Affordance Model for Unified Actionable Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05215)
*Jihoon Oh,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLAff框架，利用第一人称人类视频提取与人体无关的对象中心可操作性价比（视觉、抓取和轨迹），构建大规模EgoAffordance数据集（204K片段），并训练统一的视觉语言模型基础模型，实现零样本机器人操作和性价比引导的机器人学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类视频学习机器人操作技能具有可扩展性，但人机实体形态不匹配是核心挑战。现有方法缺乏显式编码"在哪里交互、如何抓取、如何移动"的可操作性价比框架，因此需要一种与实体无关的动作约束提取方法。

Method: 1) 利用第一人称人类视频，结合3D结构运动恢复（SfM）和手部网格重建提取三类可操作性价比（视觉、抓取、轨迹）；2) 构建EgoAffordance大规模数据集（204K片段，5.6M视觉性价比和11.6M抓取及轨迹性价比）；3) 提出VLAff，基于大型视觉语言模型（VLM）的统一基础模型，学习跨模态相关性；4) 将生成的热力图、抓取姿态和轨迹结合3D场景信息转换为可直接执行的机器人动作。

Result: VLAff在视觉可操作性价比预测上达到最先进性能，并在真实机器人应用（零样本操作和性价比引导的机器人学习）中表现有效。

Conclusion: 通过大规模人类视频数据提取与实体无关的可操作性价比，并利用VLM统一建模多种性价比，是弥合人机实体差距、实现可扩展机器人学习的有效路径。

Abstract: Learning manipulation skills from human videos is promising for scalable robot learning. However, the embodiment mismatch between humans and robots makes this challenging. One promising solution is to learn object-centric actionable affordances that are embodiment-agnostic. In this work, we propose a framework that leverages egocentric human videos with state-of-the-art 3D Structure-from-Motion and hand mesh reconstruction to extract actionable affordances such as visual, grasp, and trajectory affordances that explicitly encode where to interact, how to grasp, and how to move. We construct EgoAffordance, a large-scale dataset comprising 204K episodes with 5.6M visual affordances and 11.6M grasp and trajectory affordances. Building on this, we introduce VLAff, a large vision-language model-based unified foundation model that learns cross-modal correlations across all actionable affordances. Given a visual observation and instruction, VLAff generates visual affordance heatmaps, grasp poses, and trajectories, which are then converted into directly executable actions by utilizing 3D scene information. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that VLAff not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on visual affordance prediction, but can also be effectively applied to real robot applications such as zero-shot manipulation and affordance-guided robot learning.

</details>


### [30] [A System for Train Condition Monitoring and Structural Health Assessment of Rail Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05221)
*Maximilian Posner,Martin Dazer,Daniela Lauer,Robert Winkler-Höhn,Mathilde Laporte,Tobias Herrmann,Martin Köppel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将结构传感器技术与AI数据分析相结合的新型实时车辆状态监测与碰撞检测框架，旨在解决主线路铁路中全自动运行（GoA4）部署有限的问题。该框架涵盖三大应用：碰撞/结构损伤/碾压事件自动检测、基于状态维护、以及长期数据分析支持车辆设计优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GoA4全自动运行已在地铁系统中成熟应用，但在主线路铁路中的部署仍十分有限，主要原因是严格的安全要求和开放运营环境的复杂性。现有的基于相机、雷达和激光雷达的感知系统虽能有效检测物体，但在可靠识别撞击、碰撞和碾压事件方面能力不足，亟需新的技术手段来弥补这一安全缺口。

Method: 本文提出的框架将结构传感器技术与基于AI的数据分析相结合，实现实时车辆状态监测与碰撞检测。系统围绕三个关键应用场景构建：(1)自动检测碰撞、结构损伤及碾压事件；(2)通过连续监测实现基于状态的维护；(3)利用长期数据分析支持车辆设计优化。

Result: 研究结果证明了所提出方法的可行性，并展示了其在增强运营安全、实现预测性维护策略以及支持主线路铁路向全自动运行过渡方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究为克服主线路铁路全自动运行中的安全监测瓶颈提供了可行的技术路径，通过结构传感与AI融合的方式弥补了传统感知系统在碰撞与碾压事件检测方面的不足，有望推动主线路铁路迈向更高自动化等级。

Abstract: The ongoing digitalization of rail systems and the increasing use of artificial intelligence (AI) are fundamentally transforming the design, operation, and maintenance of rail vehicles. While fully automated operation at Grade of Automation 4 (GoA4) is well established in metro systems, its deployment in mainline rail remains limited. This is primarily due to stringent safety requirements and the complexity of open operational environments. Current perception systems based on cameras, radar, and lidar are effective in detecting objects but provide limited capability for reliably identifying impacts, collisions, and driving-over events. This paper presents a novel approach for real-time vehicle condition monitoring and impact detection that integrates structural sensor technologies with AI-based data analysis. The proposed framework addresses three key applications: (1) automated detection of impacts, structural damage, and driving-over events, (2) condition-based maintenance enabled by continuous monitoring, and (3) long-term data analytics to support vehicle design optimization. The results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach and highlight its potential to enhance operational safety, enable predictive maintenance strategies, and support the transition toward fully automated operation in mainline rail systems

</details>

*Duc M. Nguyen,Saad A. Ghani,Andrew Marshall,Allison Andreyev,Gregory J. Stein,Xuesu Xiao*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在与人类、宠物和日常物品共享的家庭环境中运行时，容易遭受软件崩溃、硬件退化或不可预测交互等故障。虽然机器人专家努力减少故障，但部分故障不可避免，因此必须减轻其潜在后果以确保安全可靠的部署。现有方法缺乏对故障影响的系统量化和评估手段。

Method: 本文提出一种新颖的安全公式，同时评估故障期间机器人与其周围实体之间发生有影响交互的概率以及这些交互结果的严重性。通过量化故障对不同实体的影响，使机器人能够做出兼顾安全性与任务效率的知情规划决策。此外，文章还提出了FailBench，一个基于MuJoCo的仿真框架，用于在多种故障模式（包括感知问题和执行器故障）下研究机器人-环境交互。

Result: 所提出的安全公式与FailBench仿真框架共同为开发更安全、更稳健的运动规划和策略学习提供了基础，可在真实家庭环境中使用。

Conclusion: 本文的安全公式和FailBench工具共同为构建更安全、更鲁棒的家庭服务机器人运动规划与学习策略奠定了基础，支撑真实世界中的安全可靠部署。

Abstract: Service robots operate in household environments shared with humans, pets, and everyday objects, where they are highly susceptible to failures such as software crashes, hardware degradation, or unpredictable interactions. While roboticists strive to minimize failures, some remain inevitable, making it critical to mitigate their potential consequences for safe and reliable deployment. This paper introduces a novel safety formulation that evaluates both the probability of impactful interactions between robots and surrounding entities during failures, and the severity of their outcomes. By quantifying the impact of failures on different entities, our approach enables robots to make informed planning decisions that balance safety with task efficiency. To support systematic evaluation, we also present FailBench, a MuJoCo-based simulation framework for studying robot-environment interactions under diverse failure modes, including sensing issues and actuator malfunctions. Together, our safety formulation and FailBench provide a foundation for developing safer and more robust motion plans and learned policies in real-world household environments.

</details>

*Md Ether Deowan,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向动态水下环境的UUV纯观测自主导航框架，融合持久占用地图构建、全局间隙感知规划与风险感知局部控制。通过行为树蒸馏和不确定性校准机制提升安全性与训练稳定性，在NVIDIA Isaac Sim高保真仿真中通过多种子评估协议验证了其在动态条件下的鲁棒性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下无人潜航器（UUV）在动态、部分可观测环境中导航面临巨大挑战：传统方法依赖显式环境模型导致对动态障碍鲁棒性不足，而纯端到端RL方法缺乏长时域结构先验，难以在复杂动态场景下保证安全性与泛化能力。现有工作缺乏统一的混合规划-学习架构以及可复现的评估方法。

Method: 提出观测唯一的自主导航框架，包含四个核心组件：(1) 仅利用声呐和深度图像构建持久占用地图；(2) 间隙约束全局规划器提供长时域结构；(3) 强化学习策略处理短程跟踪与反应式避障；(4) 从传感器数据学习紧凑潜状态表示以编码环境结构、障碍动力学和不确定性。此外引入带分阶段监督的行为树（BT）蒸馏以提升安全性和训练稳定性，并通过不确定性校准蒸馏机制动态调整教师指导的权重，重点强化高不确定性区域的训练。

Result: 在高保真GPU加速仿真环境（NVIDIA Isaac Sim）中建立多种子可复现评估协议，与BT-only和标准RL基线进行对比，结果表明该框架在动态条件下表现出更优的鲁棒性和安全性，验证了混合规划学习架构的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的混合规划-学习架构的通用流程及可复现的方法论，为部分可观测条件下鲁棒的UUV自主导航提供了有效解决方案，同时为后续研究建立了标准化的评估基准。

Abstract: This paper presents an observation-only autonomy framework for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles (UUVs) navigation in dynamic underwater environments that integrates persistent occupancy mapping, global clearance-aware planning, and risk-aware local control. The proposed pipeline constructs occupancy maps solely from onboard sonar and depth image observations, adapts a clearance-constrained global planner (GP) to provide long-horizon structure, and integrates a reinforcement learning (RL) policy to handle short-range tracking and reactive avoidance. To further support decision-making under partial observability, the system learns a compact latent state representation from onboard sensor data, encoding environmental structure, obstacle dynamics, and uncertainty. Behavior tree (BT) distillation with staged supervision is introduced to improve safety and training stability, while an uncertainty-calibrated distillation mechanism reweights teacher guidance using online latent-model uncertainty, emphasizing uncertain regimes during learning, with time-to-collision (TTC) and clearance cues remaining explicit in planning and local policy features. To demonstrate the efficacy of the framework, a reproducible multi-seed evaluation protocol is established in high-fidelity GPU-accelerated simulation using NVIDIA Isaac Sim, and performance is benchmarked against BT-only and standard RL baselines. The results obtained demonstrate improved robustness and safety under dynamic conditions, thus providing a general pipeline with a unified hybrid planning learning architecture and a reproducible methodology for robust UUV autonomy under partial observability.

</details>


### [33] [World-to-Wrist: Task-Conditioned Future Wrist Modeling for Fine-Grained Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05369)
*Yuhao Pan,Haosong Peng,Zhengshen Zhang,Zhengyang Yan,Yalun Dai,Fushuo Huo,Chujie Wang,Tianyu Qi,Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: W2-VLA是一种用于精细机器人操作的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过任务条件下的未来腕部建模来区分主视角和腕部视角在操作中的不同作用，并引入W2-CoT合成管线提供辅助监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型往往将主视角和腕部视角视为并行视觉输入，忽略了它们在机器人操作中的不同角色。精细操作实际上受益于在全局任务情境下预测腕部局部交互如何演化。

Method: W2-VLA使用一组潜在建模token作为视觉语言模型与腕部预测器之间的紧凑接口。预测器基于该接口和观察到的腕部历史预测未来腕部潜在表示，并将其转化为未来感知上下文用于动作预测。同时引入W2-CoT合成管线，生成描述操作进展、物理转换线索和腕部局部证据的结构化注释，提供辅助监督。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，该方法在单臂和双臂场景下均提升了精细及接触敏感操作能力，同时保持80Hz以上的动作生成速率。

Conclusion: 通过区分主视角和腕视角的独特作用，并利用任务条件化的未来腕部建模，W2-VLA有效提升了VLA模型在精细操作任务中的表现，且效率不受影响。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often treat main-view and wrist-view observations as parallel visual inputs, overlooking their distinct roles in robot manipulation. Fine-grained manipulation, however, benefits from anticipating how wrist-local interactions may evolve under the global task context. To address this limitation, we present World-to-Wrist VLA (W2-VLA), a VLA model for fine-grained robot manipulation with task-conditioned future wrist modeling. Given current multi-view observations and a task instruction, W2-VLA contextualizes a set of latent modeling tokens as a compact interface between the vision-language model and the wrist predictor. Conditioned on this interface and the observed wrist history, the predictor forecasts future wrist latents, which are transformed into future-aware context for action prediction. In addition, we introduce W2-CoT, a synthesis pipeline that produces structured annotations describing manipulation progress, physical transition cues, and wrist-local evidence. These annotations provide auxiliary supervision that shapes the task-conditioned latent interface. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved fine-grained and contact-sensitive manipulation across both single-arm and bimanual settings, while maintaining action-generation rates above 80 Hz.

</details>


### [34] [Sliding Sensors: Configurable Confidence in State Estimation for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05410)
*Ella Walsh,Spencer Teetaert,Eric Diller,Timothy D. Barfoot,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机械可重新配置的传感方案，通过在连续体机器人内部纵向滑动传感器来重塑状态估计的不确定性分布，从而在任务相关位置获得足够的置信度，并降低全身形变估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人在不确定环境中运行，状态估计的精度在空间上呈非均匀分布，不同位置因测量可用性不同而置信度各异。全局估计精度并非总是首要目标，更重要的是在任务关键位置获得足够的置信度。现有固定传感器无法灵活调整估计不确定性分布。

Method: 提出机械可重新配置的传感概念，设计了一种允许传感器在连续体机器人内部纵向移动的硬件方案。通过随时间前后滑动传感器，动态改变测量位置以重塑状态估计的不确定性分布。

Result: 概念硬件设计证明了传感器纵向平移的可行性；实验表明通过改变传感器位置可以重新配置状态估计置信度，相比单个固定尖端传感器，滑动传感器可降低全身形状估计误差。

Conclusion: 机械可重新配置传感能够有效重塑连续体机器人状态估计的不确定性，通过在时间上移动传感器位置，可在任务相关区域达到更高置信度，同时改善全局形状估计精度。

Abstract: Continuum robots often operate in uncertain environments, where accurate state estimation is essential for safe interactions. Estimate uncertainty is inherently spatially non-uniform: confidence varies depending on where measurements are available. Global estimation accuracy is not always the top priority, but rather achieving sufficient confidence at task-relevant locations along the robot. This extended abstract introduces mechanically reconfigurable sensing enabling uncertainty-shaping in state estimation for continuum robots. We present a concept hardware design demonstrating the feasibility of longitudinal translation of a sensor within a continuum robot. We demonstrate that state estimation confidence can be reconfigured by varying the sensor location, and show a reduction of full-body shape estimation errors when sliding the sensor back and forth over time, compared to a single fixed tip sensor.

</details>


### [35] [ARGUS: Aligning Robot Scene Geometry Under Shifting Views with Large 3D Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05579)
*Rishik Sathua,Haonan Chen,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ARGUS，一个基于大规模3D视觉模型的观测预处理管线，将任意相机视角的图像观测对齐到统一规范视角后再输入视觉运动策略，从而解耦场景几何与视角的纠缠，提升策略在视角多样化数据集上的学习效率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视觉运动策略在处理机器人操作任务时表现优异，但常将场景几何与相机视角纠缠在一起，学习的是物体在图像中的位置而非任务空间中的位置。这种纠缠限制了策略从视角多样化数据集（如DROID、BridgeV2）中学习的能力，也难以泛化到训练数据之外的新视角。

Method: ARGUS是一个观测预处理管线，利用大规模3D视觉模型将来自任意相机视角的图像观测对齐到一个规范视角，然后再将对齐后的观测传递给下游视觉运动策略。实验涵盖了从固定多相机配置到高度变化相机位置的多种视角多样性训练数据集。

Result: 在有限视角和视角多样化两种训练模式下，ARGUS均持续优于先前方法。在效率对比中，ARGUS利用简化的观测空间，比先前方法快4-6倍收敛到高成功率，展现出从视角多样化数据中高效学习的能力。

Conclusion: 利用大规模3D视觉模型将图像观测对齐到规范视角，能够降低视觉运动策略的学习负担，从而实现在大规模、视角多样化机器人数据集上更高效的学习。

Abstract: Large-scale visuomotor policies have demonstrated impressive performance across a wide range of robot manipulation tasks. However, despite this success, manipulation polices often entangle scene geometry with the corresponding viewpoint, learning where objects lie in an image rather than where it lies in the task space. This entanglement inherently limits the corresponding policy's ability to learn from viewpoint-diverse datasets (ex. DROID, BridgeV2) and generalize beyond the viewpoints captured in their training data. In this work, we present ARGUS, an observation pre-processing pipeline that uses large-scale 3D vision models to align image observations from arbitrary camera viewpoints into a canonical viewpoint before passing it to downstream visuomotor policies. Experiments across training datasets with varying levels of viewpoint diversity, from fixed multi-view camera configurations to highly varied camera placements, show that our method consistently outperforms prior approaches across both limited-view and view-diverse training regimes. In efficiency comparisons, ARGUS demonstrates an ability to learn from view-diverse data, converging to high success rates 4-6x faster than previous methods by leveraging a simplified observation space. Overall, our findings show that leveraging large-scale 3D vision models reduces the learning burden on visuomotor policies, enabling more efficient learning from large-scale, viewpoint-diverse robot datasets.

</details>


### [36] [PathCover: A Fast Convex Decomposition along a Path via Randomized Iterative Space Partitioning (RISP) on Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05586)
*Kunal S. Narkhede,Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Guoquan Huang,Ioannis Poulakakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PathCover框架，基于新随机算法RISP，直接从原始点云数据以期望线性时间构建凸多面体，生成重叠无障碍走廊以约束MPC和轨迹优化，在合成与真实LiDAR数据集上实现比SOTA方法一个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人导航需要快速生成无障碍区域用于轨迹规划，但现有走廊生成器难以满足实时、传感器频率级别的计算约束，成为系统性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出PathCover框架，核心是RISP（一种新型随机算法），从原始点云直接构造凸多面体，期望线性时间复杂度并满足温和的概率排除条件；生成重叠的无障碍凸多面体序列，用于约束下游MPC和轨迹优化。

Result: 理论上保证算法有限步内终止并沿任意无障碍参考路径持续进展；在合成及真实LiDAR基准上实现比SOTA方法一个数量级的加速，同时保持相当的走廊体积；通过高保真四旋翼仿真和四足机器人物理部署（实时LiDAR感知）验证了完整管线。

Conclusion: PathCover成功解决了走廊生成的计算瓶颈，兼顾效率与走廊质量，并在仿真和真实机器人（四足机器人）上得到验证，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation requires the rapid generation of obstacle-free regions for trajectory planning. However, existing corridor generators struggle to meet real-time, sensor-rate computational constraints. To resolve this bottleneck, we introduce PathCover, a framework driven by RISP; a novel randomized algorithm that constructs convex polytopes directly from raw point cloud data in expected linear time under a mild probabilistic elimination condition. PathCover generates sequences of overlapping, obstacle-free polytopes that safely constrain downstream MPC and trajectory optimization. We mathematically guarantee that the algorithm terminates in finite steps while ensuring continuous progress along any obstacle-free reference path. Extensive benchmarks on synthetic and real-world LiDAR datasets demonstrate an order-of-magnitude speedup over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining comparable corridor volumes. The complete pipeline is validated via high-fidelity quadrotor simulations and physical deployment on a quadrupedal robot navigating constrained environments using live LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [37] [Search-Aided Joint Agent-Environment Reinforcement Learning for Robust Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding with Rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05588)
*He Jiang,Jingtian Yan,Yulun Zhang,Yimin Tang,Tanishq Duhan,Rishi Veerapaneni,Guillaume Sartoretti,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SJRL框架，解决具有鲁棒安全约束和原地旋转约束的终身多智能体路径规划（LMAPF-R2）问题，通过将因果PIBT搜索规划器与神经网络策略相结合，并联合优化智能体与环境策略，在密集地图和混合现实仓储环境中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMAPF学习方法大多采用过度简化的运动学假设，忽略了实际自动化仓储系统中关键的运动约束（如安全约束和原地旋转约束），这些约束在受限空间中会显著增加协调难度，导致学习方法的实际应用性能不佳。

Method: 提出Search-Aided Joint Reinforcement Learning (SJRL)框架：1) 使用Causal PIBT（单步搜索规划器）增强神经策略，解决智能体间冲突并传播意图；2) 提出统一的强化学习公式，联合优化智能体和环境策略，其中环境策略学习图边权重，通过反向Dijkstra搜索提供全局移动引导。

Result: 实验表明SJRL在多个高密度地图上相较于强搜索基线Causal-PIBT有显著提升；同时在包含8个物理机器人和248个虚拟机器人的混合现实仓储环境中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将搜索增强与联合强化学习相结合，SJRL能够有效应对LMAPF-R2中运动约束带来的协调难题，证明了学习与搜索方法融合在实际仓储场景中的可行性和优势。

Abstract: Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding (LMAPF) requires repeatedly planning collision-free paths for agents that continuously receive new goals upon reaching their current ones. While many learning-based planners have been proposed for LMAPF, most rely on oversimplified kinematic assumptions that may overlook motion constraints critical to real-world performance. In this work, we study a more realistic LMAPF model derived from many real-world automated warehouse systems, termed LMAPF-R2, which incorporates robust safety constraints and in-place rotation constraints. These constraints substantially increase coordination difficulty, particularly in highly constrained spaces. To address these challenges, we propose Search-Aided Joint Reinforcement Learning (SJRL). We first augment neural policies with Causal PIBT, a single-step search-based planner that resolves agents' collisions and propagates their intentions. We then introduce a unified RL formulation that jointly optimizes agent and environment policies, where the environment policy learns graph edge costs to provide global movement guidance via backward Dijkstra search. Experiments demonstrate that SJRL achieves significant improvements over the strong search-based planner, Causal-PIBT, across multiple high-density maps. We further validate SJRL in a challenging mixed-reality warehouse environment with 8 physical robots and 248 virtual robots.

</details>


### [38] [KILVO: Kinematic-Inertial-LiDAR-Visual Odometry with Robust Multimodal Adaptation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05647)
*Jixin Gao,Fucheng Liu,Teng Zhang,Fusheng Zha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出KILVO，一种面向人形机器人的运动学-惯性-LiDAR-视觉里程计框架，通过异步-序列混合误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波器(ESIKF)融合关节编码器、IMU、LiDAR和相机数据，实现高精度、高效率、高输出率且强鲁棒的位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在复杂真实环境中需要高精度、高频率且鲁棒的状态估计，但现有融合方法未能充分利用人形机器人常备的多模态传感器（关节编码器、IMU、LiDAR、相机），且在传感器退化或失效时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 采用异步-序列混合误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波器(ESIKF)：惯性数据用于预测；腿部运动学以高频率异步处理并提供本体感觉约束；外感受更新按序进行——先通过LiDAR点云配准提供几何先验，再通过光度误差更新视觉分量。框架设计了多模态自适应机制以应对传感器故障，并包含一个无需额外传感器的紧凑接触估计模块。

Result: 在公开数据集和多个真实人形机器人、多种步态模式及场景上的广泛实验表明，KILVO在精度、效率和输出频率方面具有高度竞争力，并对传感器退化与故障表现出强鲁棒性，优于现有最先进的融合方法。代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: KILVO通过充分挖掘人形机器人多模态传感器信息并采用精心设计的异步序列融合架构，显著提升了状态估计的精度、效率与鲁棒性，比现有SOTA融合方法更适合人形机器人应用。

Abstract: This article presents a kinematic-inertial-LiDAR-visual odometry for humanoid robots, called KILVO. Tailored to the platform features, requirements, and real-world complexity, it fully utilizes the sensors commonly equipped on humanoid robots, including joint encoders, IMU, LiDAR, and camera, within an asynchronous-sequential hybrid error-state iterated Kalman filter (ESIKF). Specifically, inertial data are used for prediction, leg kinematics are processed asynchronously at a high rate and provide proprioceptive constraints, while exteroception is updated sequentially, first by registering LiDAR points for geometric priors and then by updating the visual component via photometric errors. Moreover, the framework is elaborately designed with multimodal adaptation for resilience to sensor failures. A compact contact estimation module is also developed, sharing information with state estimation without additional sensors. Extensive experiments on public datasets and in the real world across multiple humanoid robots, gait patterns, and scenarios demonstrate that KILVO achieves highly competitive accuracy, efficiency, and output rates, with strong robustness against sensor degradation and failures, making it more suitable for humanoid robots than state-of-the-art fusion methods. Our code and datasets are released on GitHub.

</details>

Result: 在真实AgiBot基准上，JoyAI-RA在已见任务和未见变体上均表现良好。任务分数随人类第一视角预训练数据量增加而持续提升，且在最大规模下仍未显示饱和迹象，说明丰富但弱标注的人类经验可转化为可迁移的训练信号。

Conclusion: 人类视频不仅是弱辅助数据源，而是操控能力扩展的主要轴，可通过双重动作对齐将其转化为可迁移的训练信号，实现跨异构数据的知识共享而非负迁移。

Abstract: Robot data is scarce, so generalist policies need to learn from heterogeneous sources, including human egocentric video, simulation, and real robots, which differ in supervision and embodiment, with action labels missing or mutually incompatible. Human egocentric data scale best but sit farthest from robot data, and naive pooling causes negative transfer rather than knowledge sharing. We propose JoyAI-RA 0.5, a generalist Vision-Language-World-Action (VLWA) framework that couples physical world-dynamics priors with visual semantics and scales manipulation learning across such data via dual action alignment. Implicit action alignment infers latent actions from visual transitions, enabling action-free human, simulation, and robot data to guide a latent-action-conditioned world model in learning physical dynamics. Explicit alignment grounds reliable human and robot trajectories in a unified physical action space through a canonical action representation and camera-frame chunk-relative end-effector actions. An inner-outer-loop reinforcement stage then pairs efficient task adaptation with foundation-policy improvement. On a real-world AgiBot benchmark, JoyAI-RA performs strongly on both seen tasks and unseen variations. The task score improves consistently as the volume of human egocentric pretraining data increases and shows no sign of plateauing at our largest scale. This suggests that abundant but weakly labeled human experience can be converted into a transferable training signal, making human video not merely a weak auxiliary source but a primary axis along which manipulation capability can be scaled. Project page can be found at https://joyai-ra-05.github.io/.

Abstract: Nonvisual classification of ground condition based on a multimodal sensing approach was investigated for an amoeba-inspired autonomous walking robot. To classify ground condition without image sensing and processing, we implemented artificial proprioception by integrating a three-axis accelerometer, eight foot pressure sensors, and reservoir computing (RC). Even when large fluctuations in the sensor outputs are caused by dynamic motions of a four-legged robot in walking, our system can classify the ground condition, flat or rough, with high accuracy. We demonstrate on-site switching of walking gait depending on ground condition in the robot. We also discuss the contribution of each sensor to ground condition classification.

### [41] [Hijacking Robots with a Piece of Paper: A Systematic Study of Physical Prompt Injection in VLM-Controlled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05715)
*S. M . Bhagya P. Samarakoon,M. A. Viraj J. Muthugala,W. K. R. Sachinthana,Mohan Rajesh Elara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了针对VLM控制的分拣机器人的物理提示注入攻击。通过四分类法构建20个攻击提示的基准，在3种场景布局和3种命令公式下对3个前沿VLM（GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen3-VL-32B）进行了5,670次试验，攻击成功率分别为27.0%、29.4%和5.0%。研究发现成功妥协几乎总是有意识的（99.9%），不同模型采用不同的防御机制。三种简单防御（提示级防御75-100%有效、两阶段验证85-100%、预处理文本掩蔽100%）能显著降低风险，但存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）越来越多地作为机器人系统中的规划器部署，其感知与指令遵循的紧耦合引入了新的攻击面：放置在机器人视野内的对抗性文本可充当对VLM推理栈的间接提示注入。理解这一漏洞的严重性和潜在防御措施对于部署安全至关重要。

Method: 作者提出了一种四类别分类法（间接标牌、任务重定义、权威冒充、冲突注入），构建了包含20个攻击提示的基准。在3种物理场景布局和3种命令公式（目的地特异性与规则明确性不同）下进行系统评估。对3个前沿VLM（GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen3-VL-32B）进行5,670次试验，并分析推理轨迹。评估三种防御策略：基于提示的防御、两阶段验证和预处理文本掩蔽。

Result: 攻击成功率为27.0%（GPT-4o）、29.4%（Gemini 2.5 Flash）和5.0%（Qwen3-VL-32B）。权威冒充和否定类攻击可在所有三个模型中转移。推理轨迹分析表明99.9%的妥协是有意识的，Gemini主要通过显式拒绝防御而GPT-4o通过感知不注意防御。防御效果：提示级防御75-100%有效（模型依赖）、两阶段验证85-100%、预处理文本掩蔽100%。防御在基准中保留了一般任务能力，但可能损害需要读取场景内标签的任务。

Conclusion: VLM控制的操作系统对人类可读的物理标牌存在实际漏洞，简单防御能大幅降低风险。然而，防御选择涉及权衡（如文本掩蔽会损害需要读取场景标签的任务），需要根据具体应用场景进行审慎选择。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as planners in robotic systems, where they translate natural-language commands into executable actions grounded in visual scene understanding. This tight coupling between perception and instruction-following introduces a new attack surface: adversarial text placed within the robot's visual field can act as an indirect prompt injection into the VLM's reasoning stack. We present a systematic study of physical prompt injection attacks against VLM-controlled sorting, introducing a four-category taxonomy, indirect signage, task redefinition, authority impersonation, and conflict injection, instantiated as a benchmark of 20 attack prompts evaluated across three physical scene layouts and three command formulations that vary in destination specificity and rule explicitness. Across 5,670 trials on three frontier VLMs (GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen3-VL-32B), attacks succeed at 27.0%, 29.4%, and 5.0% respectively, with authority-impersonating and negation attacks transferring across all three models. Analysis of reasoning traces reveals that successful compromise is nearly always conscious (99.9% acknowledgment rate), and that models defend through structurally different mechanisms, explicit rejection for Gemini, perceptual inattention for GPT-4o. We evaluate three simple mitigations: prompt-based defense (75-100% effective, model-dependent), two-stage verification (85-100%), and pre-processing text masking (100%). Our findings show that VLM-controlled manipulation is meaningfully vulnerable to human-readable physical signage, and that simple defenses substantially reduce risk, though defense choice involves trade-offs. The defenses preserve general task capabilities in our benchmark, but they may impair tasks that require reading in-scene labels.

</details>

*Yu Chen,Gong Chen,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ATP控制框架，结合可扩展的肌肉骨骼仿真、在线转矩修正与能量罐被动性保证，实现上肢外骨骼的安全、舒适且泛化的解剖学辅助；仿真与真实实验验证了转矩跟踪的准确性与被动性，EMG实验显示目标肌肉活动最高降低48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解剖学辅助主要集中于下肢外骨骼，下肢周期性、负重运动对转矩精度要求较低；将此类辅助扩展到复杂、非周期的上肢运动仍具挑战，需要在上肢外骨骼中实现安全且泛化的解剖学辅助。

Method: 提出Anatomical Torque with Passivity-Based Control (ATP)框架，包含三部分：(1) 可扩展的肌肉骨骼仿真框架，训练统一强化学习肌肉控制器，跨上肢动作泛化并生成解剖参考转矩而不需复杂生物力学计算；(2) 在线转矩修正方案，自适应不同动作、抑制肌腱引起的尖峰，并引入学习异常分数确保安全舒适；(3) 基于交互转矩的控制通过缆绳驱动柔性外骨骼提供辅助，不限制运动到预设轨迹，能量罐保证被动性并给出转矩跟踪与系统被动性的理论保证。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明：控制器能准确跟踪长时间运动序列并泛化到实时人体动作；在保持被动性的同时实现精确转矩跟踪，能量罐补充后可恢复跟踪。EMG研究（5名参与者）显示，与重力补偿和开环辅助相比，静态与动态任务中目标肌肉活动减少，动态多关节任务中相比无外骨骼运动最多降低48%。

Conclusion: ATP框架成功将解剖学辅助从下肢拓展到复杂上肢运动，在保持系统被动性和安全性的前提下实现精确转矩跟踪与肌肉活动显著降低，为可泛化的上肢外骨骼辅助提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Providing assistance across diverse movements is a central objective of exoskeletons, and anatomical knowledge can enable responsive support that generalizes across tasks. However, anatomical assistance has mainly been studied for lower-limb exoskeletons, where periodic, weight-bearing motions impose lower demands on torque precision. Extending such assistance to complex, nonperiodic upper-limb movements remains challenging. This paper proposes Anatomical Torque with Passivity-Based Control (ATP) for safe upper-limb exoskeleton assistance. First, a scalable musculoskeletal simulation framework trains a unified reinforcement-learning muscle controller that generalizes across upper-limb movements and generates anatomical reference torques without complex biomechanical computations. Second, an online torque-refinement scheme adapts the reference to diverse movements, suppresses tendon-induced spikes, and incorporates a learned anomaly score for safe and comfortable assistance. Third, an interaction torque controller delivers assistance through a cable-driven compliant exoskeleton without constraining motion to predefined trajectories, while an energy tank preserves passivity with theoretical guarantees on torque tracking and system passivity. Simulations and real-world experiments show accurate tracking of long-duration motion sequences and generalization to real-time human movements. The controller achieves accurate torque tracking while preserving passivity and resumes tracking after energy-tank replenishment. An EMG study with five participants further shows reduced target-muscle activity during static and dynamic tasks compared with gravity compensation and open-loop assistance, with reductions of up to 48% relative to movement without the exoskeleton in a dynamic multi-joint task.

</details>

*Zhengying Zhu,Ruilin Zhang,Runze Hu,Chenxi Xiao*
