TL;DR: 本文提出了一种近传感器计算框架，将频谱泊松求解器实现为全流式硬件流水线，用于视觉触觉传感器的接触几何重建。该方案以347mW的功耗、166MHz工作频率实现确定性低延迟（0.211ms），重建精度达峰值接触深度的0.17%，并将机器人保护性反射回路的响应时间从169.9ms缩短至28.3ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于主机的视觉触觉传感器处理方式存在功耗高、数据传输延迟和调度延迟不确定的问题，限制了机器人系统的感知与响应速度。作者旨在解决这些瓶颈，实现快速、高效且确定性的触觉几何重建。

Method: 实现了一个近传感器计算框架，核心是一个频谱泊松求解器，设计为完全流式的硬件流水线。该流水线无数据依赖分支、无需迭代收敛，因此提供确定性延迟。基于重建结果在芯片上完成决策，直接闭环控制机器人执行器。

Result: 计算核心逻辑估测功耗为347mW；在166MHz下，首个深度值在接收首个输入像素后35,107个周期（即0.211ms固定延迟）产生；15种接触几何下重建深度与双精度参考值的偏差为峰值接触深度的0.17%；机器人保护性反射闭环耗时28.3±4.9ms，而等效基于主机的闭环为169.9±27.8ms。

Conclusion: 近传感器重建能够以高精度、低功耗和确定性延迟的方式提供触觉几何信息，其时间尺度满足快速机器人接触响应的需求，展示了在机器人触觉系统中部署边缘计算的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Visuotactile sensors reconstruct dense contact geometry from measured surface gradients, but host-based processing increases power consumption and introduces data-transfer delays and variable scheduling latency, limiting the sensing and response speed of robotic systems. To address these limitations, we implement a near-sensor computing framework that includes a spectral Poisson solver as a fully streaming hardware pipeline. The computational core logic has an estimated power consumption of 347 mW and achieves high throughput without data-dependent branching or iterative convergence, thereby providing deterministic latency. Operating at 166 MHz, the pipeline produces the first depth value of each 128x128 frame 35,107 cycles after receiving the first input pixel, corresponding to a fixed latency of 0.211 ms. Across 15 contact geometries, the reconstructed depths differ from a double-precision reference by 0.17 % of the peak contact depth. On-chip decisions based on these reconstructions close a robot protective reflex loop in 28.3 +/- 4.9 ms, compared with 169.9 +/- 27.8 ms for an equivalent host-based loop using the same actuator. These results demonstrate that near-sensor reconstruction can provide accurate, energy-efficient, and deterministic tactile geometry on timescales suitable for rapid robotic contact responses.

</details>


### [44] [In-Context VLA: Endowing Vision-Language-Action Models with Language via In-Context Post-Training and Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05738)
*Jiarui Yang,Wen Huang,Jiale Zhang,Maowei Hu,Hang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种提升Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型语言理解能力的框架，通过上下文后训练和智能体工具接口，使策略能够"消费"而非"生成"语言，在仿真和真实机器人操控任务中超越CoT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型普遍采用行为克隆训练，注入自由文本链式思维(CoT)会降低低级控制性能：推理缺乏依据、延迟破坏闭环时序，且推理与动作token的优化目标冲突，导致策略学会"叙述"而非"行动"。因此VLA需要的不是生成语言的能力，而是消费（理解）语言的能力。

Method: 提出框架包含两个核心组件：(i) 上下文后训练——将感知证据作为结构化上下文注入，仅在动作上进行监督；(ii) 智能体工具使用接口——策略主动查询开放词汇检测器、单目深度和视觉语言模型以获取任务相关信息。数据引擎生成多样化、释义化且基于证据的空间描述，使策略学会理解未见过的语言表达。

Result: 在RoboCasa-GR1、SimplerEnv、LIBERO三个仿真基准和8个真实机器人操控任务中，该方法在匹配配置下相比CoT方法在性能和效率上均达到SOTA效果。

Conclusion: VLA模型应专注于"理解"语言而非"生成"语言，通过注入结构化感知上下文和主动工具查询的方式，可以显著提升操控策略的表现和效率，证明了该方向的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become the dominant recipe for generalist manipulation, yet they are almost universally trained by behavior cloning: a policy imitates expert action chunks conditioned on a static image and a fixed instruction. A natural remedy is to inject explicit reasoning through textual chain-of-thought (CoT). We show, both empirically and analytically, that free-form textual CoT degrades low-level control: the reasoning it produces is ungrounded, its latency breaks closed-loop timing, and, crucially, the reasoning and action tokens are optimized against conflicting objectives so that the policy learns to narrate rather than to act. We argue that what a VLA needs is not the ability to generate language, but the ability to consume grounded language. To this end we introduce \textbf{\ourmethod{}}, a framework that endows a VLA with language competence through (i) in-context post-training, in which perceptual evidence is injected as structured context and the model is supervised only on actions, and (ii) an agentic tool-use interface, in which the policy queries open-vocabulary detectors, monocular depth, and a vision--language model to actively acquire task-relevant information. Rather than emitting a single templated caption, our data engine produces diverse, paraphrased, and evidence-conditioned spatial descriptions, so that the policy learns to interpret language it has never seen verbatim. Across the RoboCasa-GR1, SimplerEnv, and LIBERO simulation benchmarks, together with 8 real-world robot manipulation tasks, our method consistently achieves SOTA results in both performance and efficiency when compared with CoT-based approaches under matched configurations.

</details>


### [45] [Acoustic-driven millimetric helical robot: ultrasonic synergistic manipulation in confined fluidic environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05746)
*Hanlin Wang,Xin Wang,Xinwei Wei,Jiaxu Liu,Le Wang,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种协同多声场驱动方法，结合声辐射力与声流作用，实现了毫米级螺旋机器人在受限生物环境中的可控运动（平面导航、斜面爬升、垂直运动），并通过体外猪静脉血管实验验证了其在生物相关约束下的单向和往复运动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声场驱动的微纳物体操控具有非接触、无创的优势，但将其扩展到毫米级机器人时，在受限生物环境中推进效率不足，限制了应用。需要一种新方法来解决毫米级机器人在生物受限环境中的推进效率问题。

Method: 采用协同多声场方法，利用声辐射力与声流（acoustic streaming flows）的协同作用，为毫米级螺旋机器人提供推进力。通过多物理场仿真捕捉毫米级螺旋机器人在组合声场下的动力学特性，并进行实验验证其运动能力（平面导航、倾斜爬升、垂直运动）。此外，进行半自主导航实验和体外猪静脉血管测试。

Result: 实验验证了毫米级螺旋机器人在协同声场驱动下具备平面导航、斜面爬升和垂直运动能力；半自主导航实验证实超声协同效应显著提升了操控性；体外猪静脉血管实验表明协同声场能在生物学相关受限条件下支持单向和往复运动。

Conclusion: 该研究为将声学微操控扩展到毫米尺度提供了机理层面的见解，支持需要多功能可控机器人运动能力的生物医学应用。

Abstract: Acoustic field-driven manipulation provides a non-contact and non-invasive strategy for controlling microscale and nanoscale objects, yet its extension to millimeter-scale robots was limited by insufficient propulsion efficiency in confined biological environments. Here, a coordinated multi-acoustic-field approach is introduced, which harnesses the synergistic action of acoustic radiation forces and acoustic streaming flows to enable controlled locomotion of millimeter-scale helical robots and enhance propulsion. Multiphysics simulations captured the dynamics of millimeter-scale helical robots under combined acoustic fields, and experimental validation demonstrated their locomotion capabilities, including planar navigation, inclined climbing, and vertical motion. Semi-autonomous navigation experiments further confirmed that ultrasonic synergy substantially improved maneuverability. In vitro tests in porcine venous vessels demonstrated that coordinated acoustic fields supported both unidirectional and reciprocating motion under biologically relevant confinement. These findings provide mechanistic insight into scaling acoustic micromanipulation to the millimetre regime and support biomedical applications requiring versatile and controllable robotic mobility.

</details>


### [46] [XEWorld: Can Action-Conditioned World Models Generalize to Unseen Robot Embodiments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05799)
*Yixiang Chen,Jiabing Yang,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Keji He,Peiyan Li,Kai Wang,Ziheng He,Xiangnan Wu,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出XEWorld跨具身测试平台，系统揭示当前世界模型本质上是2D视觉模式匹配器，其泛化受视觉相似性而非物理运动学相似性驱动，导致无法在未见机器人上实现零样本渲染，即使通过少样本适应也会灾难性遗忘已见具身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型仅在训练机器人上进行评估，无法区分模型是否真正捕获了物理动力学，还是仅仅记住了视觉模式。作者希望回答一个关键问题：模型能否忠实渲染一个从未见过的机器人。

Method: 构建XEWorld受控跨具身测试平台，在物理上相同的场景中隔离具身因素，通过评估未见机器人来测试世界模型的跨具身泛化能力。通过系统性分析揭示模型架构瓶颈，并验证零样本和少样本两种场景下的泛化表现。

Result: 发现当前模型主导机制是2D视觉模式匹配，泛化由视觉相似性而非动力学相似性驱动；模型难以将抽象数值关节动作转化为连贯视觉轨迹，无法从静态初始观测预测动态视觉变化；零样本渲染未见具身需要像素空间动作和显式时空对齐等强接地线索；少样本适应会导致已见具身的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 真正的跨具身泛化要求架构创新，将视觉外观与底层物理动力学解耦，否则无法将学习到的物理动力学应用于新的视觉外观。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are promising learned simulators for robotic manipulation, yet evaluating them exclusively on training robots fails to reveal whether they capture physical dynamics or merely memorize visual patterns. To answer whether a model can faithfully render a robot it has never seen, we introduce XEWorld, a controlled cross-embodiment testbed for world models that isolates embodiments by evaluating held-out robots within physically identical scenes. Our systematic analysis uncovers a shared architectural bottleneck: current models act primarily as 2D visual pattern matchers whose generalization is governed by visual similarity rather than physical kinematic similarity. Driven by this limitation, they struggle to translate abstract numeric joint actions into coherent visual trajectories, and fail to predict dynamic visual changes from static initial observations. Consequently, successfully rendering an unseen embodiment zero-shot strictly requires heavily grounded cues, specifically pixel-space actions and explicit spatial-temporal alignment. Even when bypassing this zero-shot barrier via few-shot adaptation, the forced appearance recovery triggers catastrophic forgetting of seen embodiments. Together, these failures expose a critical inability to apply learned physical dynamics to novel visual appearances, highlighting that achieving true cross-embodiment generalization requires architectural innovations that decouple visual appearance from underlying physical dynamics.

</details>


### [47] [Coordinated Multi-Robot Disassembly for Makespan Optimization of Large-Scale Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05830)
*Niklas Hargus,Andreas Orthey,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CoMuDi的多机器人拆卸任务与运动规划方法。CoMuDi通过复合任务创建和时间约束传播来协调多机器人团队，并结合空间-时间RRT*（ST-RRT*）规划器来最小化每个任务的到达时间，从而整体优化makespan。实验表明，CoMuDi结合ST-RRT*比RRT*具有更高的成功率，并能可靠求解多达49个零件、9个机器人的大型装配体拆卸任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人拆卸任务规划需要机器人在受限工作空间中协同运动，同时要避免机器人之间的碰撞。这是一个既具有挑战性又实际的问题，需要一种能够同时处理任务分配和运动规划的协调方法。

Method: CoMuDi方法分为几个关键步骤：1) 输入团队机器人、物体装配体和依赖图；2) 基于这些信息创建pick、place和exit动作的复合任务；3) 通过传播时间约束，确保每个机器人能尽早开始和结束任务并避免与邻近机器人碰撞；4) 集成ST-RRT*规划器，确保单个任务最小化到达时间，从而帮助最小化整体makespan。

Result: CoMuDi结合ST-RRT*相比RRT*在不同时间预算下具有更高的成功率，同时能最小化makespan。在多达49个零件和9个机器人的六个装配体场景中，CoMuDi返回的机器人路径表现出低空闲时间，展示了其在大型装配体上的可靠求解能力。

Conclusion: CoMuDi能够可靠地解决大规模多机器人拆卸规划问题，通过任务协调和时间约束传播实现高效协作，结合ST-RRT*可进一步提升成功率和时间效率，在大型装配体场景中展现出低空闲时间和稳定的求解能力。

Abstract: Multi-robot task and motion planning for disassembly tasks requires robots to operate in confined workspaces while coordinating their motions with other robots. To tackle this problem, we propose a planning method called coordinated multi-robot disassembly (CoMuDi). CoMuDi coordinates a team of robots for disassembly tasks. The input is a team of robots, an assembly of objects, and a dependency graph. Based on this information, we create compound tasks for pick, place, and exit motions. By propagating temporal constraints, we ensure that each robot can start and end their tasks as early as possible while avoiding collisions with nearby robots. By integrating the space-time RRT* planner (ST-RRT*) into CoMuDi, we ensure that individual tasks minimize arrival time and thereby help us minimize overall makespan. We compare the performance of CoMuDi using both ST-RRT* and RRT* planners with varying time bounds, demonstrating that the combination of CoMuDi and ST-RRT* leads to a higher success rate while minimizing makespan. Finally, we evaluate CoMuDi on six assemblies with up to 49 pieces and up to 9 robots. In those scenarios, we show that CoMuDi returns robot paths that exhibit low idle times, thereby demonstrating that CoMuDi can reliably solve large-scale assemblies.

</details>


### [48] [SkillMemo: Expert-guided Skill Memory Framework for Compositional Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05970)
*Changyuan Wang,Chubin Zhang,Zhenyu Wu,Runhao Li,Angyuan Ma,Ke Chao,Yinan Liang,Xiuwei Xu,Ziwei Wang,Yansong Tang,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-Based Memory (SkillMemo)框架，通过隐式分解长程演示为潜在原子技能，并将技能级特征存入动态情景记忆库，以解决具身视触觉模型在数据稀缺条件下组合泛化不足的问题。该框架在模拟基准和真实世界操作任务中一致提升了DP和VLA骨干模型的性能，达到SOTA并超越π_{0.5}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视触觉模型（如DP和VLA）虽然在下游操作基准上表现良好，但其潜力受限于大规模具身轨迹数据的稀缺性，导致在分布外场景中组合泛化能力不足，难以捕获可复用的技能结构。

Method: SkillMemo框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)架构的专家引导轨迹分割模块，通过学习到的门控系数隐式将长程轨迹划分为不同的技能原语；(2) 技能级情景记忆架构，将紧凑的技能表征存储为可检索的键值对。推理时，记忆库检索最相关的技能原语并与模型当前的策略门控分布融合，提供上下文先验以精化动作预测。

Result: 在模拟基准和真实世界操作任务上的大量实验表明，SkillMemo一致提升了DP和VLA两类骨干模型的性能，达到最先进水平，超越π_{0.5}，并在未见任务配置中展现出强大的组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: SkillMemo通过隐式技能分解和情景记忆检索机制，有效缓解了具身操作模型中大规模轨迹数据稀缺带来的组合泛化瓶颈，为提升可复用技能结构的捕获能力提供了通用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Embodied visuomotor models, including Diffusion Policy (DP) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, have demonstrated promising performance on robotic manipulation benchmarks. However, their potential remains fundamentally constrained by the scarcity of large-scale embodied trajectory datasets, leading to insufficient compositional generalization in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios with limited capability to capture reusable skill structures. To address this limitation, we propose Skill-Based Memory (SkillMemo) framework that implicitly decomposes long-horizon demonstrations into latent atomic skills and integrates skill-level features into a dynamic episodic memory bank for solving compositional tasks. Specifically, we first introduce an expert-guided trajectory segmentation module built upon a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture, which implicitly partitions trajectories into distinct skill primitives represented by learned gating coefficients. We further design a skill-level episodic memory architecture that stores compact skill representations as retrievable key-value pairs. During inference, the memory bank retrieves the most relevant skill primitives which are subsequently fused with the model's current gating distribution, providing a robust contextual prior to refine action predictions. Extensive experiments on the simulation benchmark and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that SkillMemo consistently enhances both DP and VLA backbones, achieving state-of-the-art performance and outperforming $π_{0.5}$, while exhibiting strong compositional generalization to unseen task configurations.

</details>


### [49] [TRACE: Learned Proprioceptive Odometry for Legged Robots under Unreliable Contact Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05975)
*Taehyeon Kong,Woojin Kim,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE（Tokenized Robust Attention for Contact-Aware Estimation），一种端到端学习的腿式机器人本体感测里程计估计器，通过足部感知的交叉注意力模块自适应加权IMU和腿部运动学token，在不依赖人工接触/滑移阈值的情况下，在不可靠接触条件下实现相对位移、旋转和机体速度的鲁棒估计。结合物理启发的辅助损失、策略随机化和部分真实世界微调，在多种地形上相比经典滤波、混合和纯学习基线均降低位置漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在不可靠接触条件（如滑移、打滑、接触丢失）下，传统基于接触检测/阈值的方法容易失效，而现有学习方法对接触状态的建模依赖人工定义的阈值，且存在策略过拟合和sim-to-real迁移困难的问题，亟需一种不依赖人工接触假设且具有跨域鲁棒性的里程计估计方法。

Method: 提出端到端学习的TRACE框架：(1) 将IMU和逐腿运动学测量编码为token序列；(2) 引入足部感知的交叉注意力模块，自适应加权IMU与腿部token，无需人工接触或滑移阈值；(3) 采用直接监督结合两个物理启发的辅助损失（促进运动学一致性和腿部信息的可靠利用）；(4) 训练策略包含策略随机化以降低策略特定过拟合，并通过部分真实世界微调时序编码器和预测头来改善sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在多种室内外地形上的实验中，TRACE相比经典的基于滤波的方法、混合方法和纯学习方法基线，一致性地降低了位置漂移。消融研究进一步验证了所提出的训练目标、策略随机化和真实世界微调在不可靠接触和sim-to-real失配场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: TRACE通过token化表示与交叉注意力机制，为腿式机器人在不可靠接触条件下的本体感测里程计提供了一种无需人工接触阈值的鲁棒解决方案，且通过针对性的训练策略和微调方案有效缓解了sim-to-real差距问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we present TRACE (Tokenized Robust Attention for Contact-Aware Estimation), an end-to-end learned proprioceptive odometry estimator for legged robots under unreliable contact conditions. The proposed estimator directly predicts relative displacement, relative rotation, and body-frame velocity from a recent history of onboard inertial and joint measurements. To improve robustness under unreliable contact conditions, we introduce a foot-aware cross-attention module that adaptively weights IMU and leg-wise kinematic tokens without relying on manually defined contact or slip thresholds. The estimator is trained with direct supervision and two physics-inspired auxiliary losses that promote kinematic consistency and reliable use of leg information. To reduce policy-specific overfitting and consequently improve sim-to-real transfer, simulation training incorporates policy randomization, followed by partial real-world fine-tuning of the temporal encoder and prediction head. Experiments across diverse indoor and outdoor terrains demonstrate consistent reductions in position drift compared with classical filtering-based, hybrid, and purely learning-based baselines. Ablation studies further validate the contributions of the proposed training objectives, policy randomization, and real-world fine-tuning, particularly under unreliable contacts and sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Flat Policies: Hierarchical Post-Training for Embodied Agents in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05999)
*He Kong,Zengjue Chen,Qi Wang,Qianli Xing,Runliang Niu,Peidong Liu,Jiawei Li,Shiqi Wang,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiRoC是一个分层后训练框架，将VLA模型解耦为高层任务规划器和低层动作执行器，通过强化学习持续改进子目标条件下的动作生成，并在规划与执行间进行对齐以缓解分布不匹配，在多种机器人操作基准上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA后训练方法主要将模型优化为扁平策略，难以显式建模任务进展和执行长程操作；虽然分层方法引入任务分解，但主要依赖离线演示的监督学习，无法通过在线交互有效提升执行效果。

Method: 提出HiRoC分层后训练框架：1) 规划器将复杂任务分解为可执行子目标，提供显式语义指导；2) 执行器通过强化学习持续改进子目标条件下的动作生成；3) 在强化学习前将执行器与规划器生成的子目标进行对齐，缓解规划与执行间的分布不匹配，促进两模块协作。

Result: 在多种机器人操作基准上的大量实验表明，HiRoC持续优于强基线；综合分析验证了分层后训练的有效性以及各关键组件（规划器、执行器、对齐机制）的贡献。

Conclusion: 分层后训练框架能有效解决VLA模型在长程操作中的局限性，通过规划-执行解耦和在线强化学习提升性能，为VLA模型的层次化后训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in robotic manipulation by leveraging pretrained vision-language models. However, existing post-training methods predominantly optimize VLA models as flat policies, making it difficult to explicitly model task progression and perform robust long-horizon manipulation. Although hierarchical approaches introduce task decomposition, they mainly rely on supervised learning from offline demonstrations and cannot effectively improve execution through online interaction. To address this limitation, we propose Hierarchical Robotic Control (HiRoC), a hierarchical post-training framework that decouples high-level task planning from low-level action execution. The planner decomposes complex tasks into executable subgoals to provide explicit semantic guidance, while the executor continuously improves subgoal-conditioned action generation through reinforcement learning. To enable effective collaboration between the two modules, we further align the executor with planner-generated subgoals before reinforcement learning, mitigating the distribution misalignment between planning and execution. Extensive experiments across diverse robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that HiRoC consistently outperforms strong baselines. Comprehensive analyses further validate the effectiveness of hierarchical post-training and the contribution of each key component.

</details>


### [51] [Adaptive-WAM: Quality-Guided Early-Exit Planning from Intermediate Video-Diffusion Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06008)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Adaptive-WAM，一种基于Wan2.2-5B骨干网络的智能多出口规划器。通过受控实验发现规划性能对视频降噪时间步不敏感，且可从中间层解码出可靠轨迹。该模型在DiT块上附加轨迹扩散头，并用轻量轨迹质量评分器动态决定推理深度，避免了迭代式无分类器引导去噪和VAE解码。在NAVSIM上取得90.8 PDMS，迁移至nuScenes无需微调即可获得低L2误差和碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频扩散模型为自动驾驶提供了丰富的时空先验，但现有的世界-动作模型通常继承了迭代式未来视频生成的巨大计算成本，而实际部署仅需要一条自车轨迹。作者提出一个更基本的问题：要做出可靠的驾驶决策，必须执行视频扩散模型的多少部分？这推动了对模型计算效率与规划性能之间关系的研究。

Method: 方法包括三个关键部分：(1) 对视频去噪时间步和DiT深度进行受控研究，发现规划性能对视频噪声水平不敏感，而中间层即可解码出优质轨迹；(2) 基于此观察，将轨迹扩散头附加到选定的DiT块上，构建多出口架构；(3) 使用轻量级轨迹质量评分器，一旦解码出的最佳轨迹满足质量阈值即终止推理，否则从缓存的隐藏状态继续到更深的出口，实现自适应深度分配。

Result: 在NAVSIM上，自适应单轨迹规划器达到90.8 PDMS，固定出口变体以64个候选达到92.6 PDMS；在NAVSIM v2上获得89.9 EPDMS，为所比较的前视视频世界模型规划器中最佳。无需目标域微调即可迁移至nuScenes（0.88m平均L2误差，0.08%碰撞率）。在A100上，自适应路由使PDMS从90.62提升至90.79，平均端到端规划延迟170ms，比固定block-15规划器低约10%，比固定全深度规划器低47%。

Conclusion: Adaptive-WAM证明了视频扩散模型无需完整执行即可实现高质量轨迹规划。通过质量感知的自适应多出口推理，在大幅降低计算成本（延迟降低47%）的同时，甚至在规划性能上略有提升。这为将大规模视频扩散模型应用于实时自动驾驶规划提供了一条高效路径，且具备良好的跨数据集迁移能力。

Abstract: Large video diffusion models provide rich spatiotemporal priors for autonomous driving, but existing world-action models often inherit the cost of iterative future-video generation even though deployment only requires an ego trajectory. We ask a more basic question: how much of a video diffusion model must be executed to make a reliable driving decision? Through a controlled study of video denoising timesteps and Diffusion Transformer (DiT) depth, we find that planning performance is largely insensitive to the tested video-noise levels, whereas strong trajectories can already be decoded from intermediate layers. Based on this observation, we introduce Adaptive-WAM, a quality-aware multi-exit planner built on a Wan2.2-5B backbone. Trajectory diffusion heads are attached to selected DiT blocks, and a lightweight trajectory-quality scorer terminates inference once the best trajectory decoded so far satisfies a quality threshold; otherwise, computation continues from the cached hidden state to a deeper exit. The deployed planner therefore avoids the iterative classifier-free denoising loop and VAE decoding required for future-video synthesis, while dynamically allocating backbone depth according to trajectory quality. On NAVSIM, the adaptive single-trajectory planner achieves 90.8 PDMS; a separate fixed-exit variant reaches 92.6 PDMS with 64 proposals. It further obtains 89.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, yielding the best reported results among the compared front-view video world-model planners. Without target-domain fine-tuning, Adaptive-WAM transfers to nuScenes with 0.88 m average L2 error and a 0.08\% collision rate. On an A100, adaptive routing improves PDMS from 90.62 to 90.79 while averaging 170 ms end-to-end planning latency, approximately 10\% below the 190 ms fixed block-15 planner and 47\% below the 320 ms fixed full-depth planner. Code will be released.

</details>


### [52] [Topometric Autonomous Vehicle Localization by Combining Visual Embeddings and Feed-Forward 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06021)
*Eulogio Quemada-Torres,Alberto Jaenal,Francisco-Angel Moreno,Javier Gonzalez-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将视觉位置识别（VPR）与基于前馈神经3D几何（FF3D）的度量位姿估计相结合的自顶向下定位框架。通过离线自动地图构建工具建模场景中各部分的位姿-外观交互，并利用在线粒子滤波融合里程计和地点信念进行FF3D推理，在三个基准数据集上显著优于现有基于外观的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉定位（VL）需要紧凑、鲁棒且精确的环境地图。VPR通过低维图像嵌入实现紧凑性和外观鲁棒性，但度量精度不足；而基于局部特征或神经表示的VL度量精度高但扩展性差。作者希望融合两者的优势，克服VPR低度量精度的局限。

Method: 提出一个自顶向下（topometric）顺序外观定位框架：（1）离线自动映射工具建模场景不同区域中位姿-外观的概率交互；（2）在线粒子滤波结合里程计和地点信念，为FF3D推理提供度量位姿估计；（3）模块化设计使描述子提取器和FF3D模型可互换。

Result: 在三个已知基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，该框架相比现有基于外观的定位方法有显著改进；进一步分析显示顺序信念（sequential belief）能够在感知别名（perceptual aliasing）情况下缓解严重失败。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将神经度量估计融入概率式基于外观的定位中，验证了VPR与FF3D互补集成的有效性，同时保持各模块的互换性，为鲁棒且可扩展的视觉定位提供了新思路。

Abstract: Effective Visual Localization (VL) requires a map of the environment that combines compactness for efficient scalability with robustness against visual appearance changes and metric precision. Through low-dimensional image embeddings, Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is able to successfully meet the first two requirements, but its low metric accuracy makes it less suitable than standard VL approaches based on local features or neural representations. This limitation can be overcome by integrating VPR with the accurate local trajectory estimates produced by feed-forward neural 3D geometry (FF3D) models. In this paper, we address sequential appearance-based localization through a topometric framework that iteratively combines probabilistic VPR with FF3D metric pose estimation in controlled image sets. Our approach proposes an automatic offline mapping tool that models the topometric pose-appearance interaction in the different parts of the scene. This map is later employed by an online particle filter that estimates the pose from odometry and belief over places for FF3D inference, successfully incorporating neural metric estimation into probabilistic appearance-based localization. We extensively evaluate the framework on three known benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over existing appearance-based methods. The modularity of our approach allows the descriptor extractor and FF3D model to remain interchangeable, and a focused analysis further shows that sequential belief can mitigate severe failures under perceptual aliasing.

</details>


### [53] [IcFuzz: Fuzzing Isaac Sim with Semantic Stage Guidance and Multi-level Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06088)
*Zhixiang Chen,Zhuangbin Chen,Ruoxi Jia,Zeqin Liao,Wei Li,Jinyang Liu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出IcFuzz，这是首个针对NVIDIA Isaac Sim模拟器的模糊测试方法。IcFuzz通过LLM语义阶段分割、多级别变异算子和多臂老虎机调度算法，有效测试Isaac Sim，相比基线方法在代码覆盖率和缺陷检测上均显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Isaac Sim作为机器人模拟器基础设施用途广泛，但其内部结构复杂，存在软件缺陷影响仿真可靠性。现有模糊测试方法因缺乏上下文感知的对象语义、无法处理层级化仿真控制以及面对巨大仿真状态空间而难以有效测试Isaac Sim。

Method: IcFuzz包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于LLM的语义阶段分割，将仿真程序分解为结构化阶段以捕获上下文感知的对象语义；(2) 多级别变异算子设计，在层级化粒度上系统性测试模拟器；(3) 基于多臂老虎机算法自适应调度变异算子，高效探索仿真状态空间。

Result: 实验表明IcFuzz在代码覆盖率和缺陷检测方面均优于基线方法：代码覆盖率约为基线的190%-205%；三轮12小时测试中平均检测到3.7个独特崩溃而基线未检测到任何崩溃；在约四个月内发现11个bug，其中9个已被开发者确认或修复。

Conclusion: IcFuzz作为首个针对Isaac Sim的模糊测试方法，有效解决了上下文语义、层级控制和状态空间三大挑战，证明了LLM引导、多级别变异和自适应调度相结合在复杂机器人模拟器测试中的有效性。

Abstract: Robotics simulators serve as a foundational infrastructure for embodied AI, facilitating safe and scalable robotic system development. NVIDIA Isaac Sim has emerged as one of the most popular simulators, distinguished by its GPU-accelerated physics engine and photorealistic rendering, which enable high-fidelity modeling of complex environments. However, its inherent complexity inevitably introduces software bugs that can compromise simulation reliability. Existing fuzzing approaches struggle to test Isaac Sim effectively due to challenges of context-aware object semantics, hierarchical simulation control, and a vast simulation state space.
  In this paper, we propose IcFuzz, the first fuzzing approach for Isaac Sim. IcFuzz first performs an LLM-based semantic stage segmentation, decomposing simulation programs into structured stages that capture context-aware object semantics. Guided by this information, IcFuzz designs multi-level mutation operators to systematically exercise the simulator across hierarchical granularities. To efficiently navigate the vast simulation state space, IcFuzz employs a multi-armed bandit algorithm to adaptively schedule mutation operators. Experimental results show that IcFuzz outperforms the baselines in terms of both code coverage and bug detection. Specifically, IcFuzz achieves approximately 190\%--205\% of the code coverage of the baselines and detects an average of 3.7 unique crashes over three rounds of 12-hour tests, while no crashes are detected by the baselines. Moreover, IcFuzz has uncovered 11 bugs over approximately four months, 9 of which have been confirmed or fixed by the developers.

</details>


### [54] [Visual Grounding in Zero-Shot Vision-Language Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06154)
*J. de Curtò,Dayani Plasencia,Diego Sánchez,I. de Zarzà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统性地评估了视觉语言模型（VLMs）作为零样本控制器的实际感知能力。通过输入消融测试（盲图、重复输入、镜像反射等）发现，直接控制模型大多对视觉输入不敏感，甚至恒定策略优于脚本化控制器，没有任何本地VLM同时满足纵向和横向接地标准。然而，纯图像确定性控制证实视觉刺激携带足够信息，问题出在模块而非能力本身。作者提出泄漏控制的对称一致性守护机制，在272个保留帧上达到0.954平衡准确率，最终支持VLMs作为有界、选择性的危险辅助工具而非零样本控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型被广泛用作零样本控制器，但成功的轨迹可能仅源于模拟器动力学和保守动作先验，而非对视觉输入的有意义理解。作者旨在严格检验VLM决策是否真正基于视觉输入，以揭示其作为控制器的真实能力边界。

Method: 设计输入消融电池测试：（1）盲图控制、重复相同输入、车道轴反射、非视觉基线、流水线完整性检查；（2）在9个直接动作模型、6个结构化本地VLM和VLM-MPC层级上，覆盖两个载体和三个模拟器，分析32,874次评分调用；（3）提出后验、泄漏控制的对称一致性守护机制，从16个校准帧选择模型并冻结2/4危险投票，在原始与反射视图上验证。

Result: 直接控制结果总体负面：恒定SLOW策略优于脚本化几何控制器，多个模型对图像不变或几乎恒定，识别纵向危险的模型在反射下仍无法变换左右方向。无本地VLM满足联合接地标准。但图像确定性控制达到0.090m MAE的间距估计与精确镜像等变性。对称一致性守护在272个保留帧上达0.954平衡准确率（CI [0.895,0.990]），12折LOEO全部恢复相同模型对；弃权提升至0.934提交平衡准确率，覆盖率0.824，动作一致性0.934且镜像等变精确。

Conclusion: 当前VLMs应被视为有界、选择性的危险辅助工具，而非全能的零样本控制器。感知失败是模块化的而非普遍性的，通过模块组合与对称一致性验证可构建更可靠的VLM辅助驾驶框架。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as zero-shot controllers, but successful trajectories do not necessarily show that decisions are grounded in visual input: simulator dynamics and conservative action priors can produce favourable scores without meaningful perception. We investigate this with an input-ablation battery: blind-image controls, repeated identical inputs, lane-axis reflection, non-visual baselines, and pipeline-integrity checks. Across nine direct-action models, six structured local VLMs, and an exploratory VLM-MPC hierarchy, we analyse 32,874 scored calls over two embodiments and three simulators. The direct-control results are largely negative: a constant-SLOW policy outperforms a scripted geometric controller, several models are image-invariant or nearly constant, and models that recognize longitudinal hazards still fail to transform LEFT and RIGHT under reflection. No local VLM meets the joint longitudinal and lateral grounding criteria. However, an image-only deterministic positive control estimates the lead gap with 0.090 m MAE and exact mirror equivariance, confirming the stimuli carry sufficient visual information; the failures are modular, not universal. A post-hoc, leakage-controlled symmetry-consensus guardian selects two models from 16 calibration frames and freezes a 2-of-4 hazard vote across original and reflected views. On 272 held-out frames it reaches 0.954 balanced accuracy (episode-cluster bootstrap 95% CI [0.895,0.990]); nested leave-one-episode-out recovers the same pair and threshold in all 12 folds. Abstaining on ties raises committed balanced accuracy to 0.973 at 0.824 coverage. With deterministic perception retaining lateral authority, offline modular replay achieves 0.934 action agreement and exact mirror equivariance. These results support current VLMs as bounded, selective hazard assistants, not monolithic zero-shot controllers.

</details>


### [55] [Prior-SG: Task and Prior Driven Region Segmentation for Scene Graphs in Arbitrarily-Structured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06170)
*Giorgio Tonetti,Laurent Kneip,Abel Gawel,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Prior-SG 是一种任务与先验驱动的层次化 3D 场景图生成框架，将场景图生成问题建模为概率对齐问题，利用大语言模型动态合成先验图，并通过马尔可夫随机场优化融合视觉、几何和离散对象的异构专家信息，实现开放平面环境中的零样本空间语义分区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景图提取框架依赖纯局部视觉聚类或严格几何启发式（如墙体分隔的房间），在开放平面或任意结构环境中失效。

Method: 将场景图生成建模为概率对齐问题；通过多尺度、开放词汇特征融合将 RGB-D 传感器流聚合为物理实例图；利用大语言模型动态合成先验图（逻辑环境结构及任务相关词汇）；通过 MAP 估计在马尔可夫随机场中优化先验图拓扑先验与视觉/几何/离散对象异构专家的融合，解决局部感知歧义。

Result: 在多样化的模拟住宅数据集和大型开放平面真实环境中验证，语义区域分割精度达到 SOTA，在无物理墙壁的情况下能稳健划定远距离功能边界，且具备零样本本体灵活性。

Conclusion: Prior-SG 通过概率先验驱动的方式突破了传统几何启发式的局限，使机器人能够根据高层任务动态重构空间分区，为层次化空间推理提供了一种更灵活、可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs are a promising representation for high-level spatial reasoning in autonomous mobile platforms. However, existing extraction frameworks typically rely on purely local visual clustering or strict geometric heuristics, such as wall-separated rooms, which fail in open-plan or arbitrarily-structured environments. We propose Prior-SG, a task- and prior-driven framework that casts scene graph generation fundamentally as a probabilistic alignment problem. As the robot explores, it continuously aggregates an incoming RGB-D sensor stream into a physically grounded Instance Graph utilizing a multi-scale, open-vocabulary feature fusion strategy. The system then infers the high-level functional semantics of this map through a Maximum A Posteriori (MAP) estimate, guided by a Prior Graph-a logical expectation of the environment's structure and task-relevant vocabulary synthesized dynamically by a Large Language Model. By optimizing a Markov Random Field that fuses heterogeneous experts (visual, geometric, and discrete objects) with these topological priors, the system resolves local perceptual ambiguities. We validate this approach across diverse simulated residential datasets and large, open-plan real-world environments. Prior-SG achieves state-of-the-art semantic region segmentation accuracy compared to recent baselines, robustly delineates distant functional boundaries in the absence of physical walls, and uniquely provides zero-shot ontological flexibility, enabling the robot to entirely restructure its spatial partitioning based on a given high-level task.

</details>


### [56] [ErgoSurf: Ergodic Control for the Coverage of Unknown Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06208)
*Stefan Schneyer,Timo Bachmann,Maged Iskandar,Korbinian Nottensteiner,Alin Albu-Schäffer,Freek Stulp,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的在线遍历控制框架，通过触觉感知在未知表面上同时实现系统性覆盖和几何重建。该框架利用高斯过程隐式表面（GPIS）模型结合局部切平面点云近似来学习表面几何，并通过热扩散类比计算势场以引导遍历探索，在仿真和真实机器人实验中验证了其有效性和高重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的遍历控制方法依赖预先已知的表面几何信息或需要视觉传感器预先扫描几何形状，这限制了其在未知或动态环境中的实际应用。对于接触类任务（如检测、清洁、打磨和抛光），机器人需要在保持稳定接触的同时系统地覆盖表面，因此亟需一种不需要先验几何知识即可在线完成表面覆盖和几何学习的控制方法。

Method: 本文采用高斯过程隐式表面（GPIS）模型，通过执行过程中内在的触觉传感来学习全局表面几何。为高效在线规划，利用接触点处切平面采样的点云局部近似表面，并迭代拟合到高斯过程中。该近似同时作为目标分布和覆盖分布的采样域。采用热扩散类比计算势场以引导遍历探索，将空间覆盖目标转化为平滑的机器人轨迹。

Result: 通过仿真和真实机器人实验验证了该框架的有效性，实现了同步的遍历覆盖和在线表面几何学习，重建误差接近真实值（ground truth）。

Conclusion: 该在线遍历控制框架能够在无需先验几何信息的情况下，同时完成未知表面的系统覆盖和几何重建，为接触类任务在未知或动态环境中的实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Contact-centric tasks on surfaces, ranging from inspection and cleaning to sanding and polishing, require robots to systematically cover the surface while maintaining stable contact. Ergodic control generates trajectories that spend time at a location proportional to a desired, task-specific spatial distribution, enabling efficient information gathering and coverage. However, traditional ergodic control methods rely on prior knowledge of surface geometry or require a vision sensory input to scan the geometry beforehand, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios with unknown or dynamic environments. This paper introduces a novel online ergodic control framework that achieves systematic surface coverage while simultaneously reconstructing unknown surface geometry. We employ a Gaussian Process Implicit Surface (GPIS) model that learns global surface geometry from intrinsic tactile sensing during execution. For efficient online planning, we approximate the surface locally using point clouds sampled from tangent planes at observed contact points and iteratively fit them to the Gaussian Process. This approximation simultaneously serves as the sampling domain for both the target and the coverage distributions. We employ a heat-diffusion analogy to compute potential fields that guide ergodic exploration, translating spatial coverage objectives into smooth robot trajectories. We demonstrate our framework through simulation and real-robot experiments, validating simultaneous ergodic coverage and online surface geometry learning with reconstruction errors approaching the ground truth.

</details>


### [57] [VIDP: Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy for Compliant Robot Manipulation from Diverse Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06210)
*Hisham Khalil,Neil Fernandes,Thomas M. Kwok,Hsiu-Chin Lin,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出变阻抗扩散策略（VIDP），一种无需力传感器的模仿学习框架，通过任务参数化的方向感知混合模型（TP-DAMM）从多样化演示中提取物理一致的轨迹分布并将其映射为刚度曲线，实现姿态动作与任务顺应性的联合预测，在真实实验中显著优于固定阻抗基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要精确跟踪与机械顺应性，变阻抗控制能适应变化的接触约束，但传统静态顺应性无法适应不同接触条件。现有的变阻抗技能学习方法从力无关的动力学数据中推断顺应性存在歧义——轨迹变化可能反映几何适应而非有意顺应。因此需要一种能从演示中提取物理一致的轨迹分布并映射为顺应性（刚度）的方法，且无需力传感器。

Method: 提出VIDP框架：1）利用任务参数化的方向感知混合模型（TP-DAMM）从多样化演示中提取物理一致的轨迹分布，该模型能够区分由于空间布局变化引起的几何适应与有意的顺应性变化；2）将提取的轨迹分布映射为刚度曲线（stiffness profiles）；3）通过扩散策略联合预测姿态动作与任务顺应性，全程无需力传感器。

Result: 真实机器人实验中，VIDP在任务成功率上显著优于固定阻抗基线；与高刚度控制器相比降低了交互力，与低刚度基线相比降低了跟踪误差，即实现了高成功率、低交互力和低跟踪误差的平衡。

Conclusion: VIDP有效解决了从力无关数据中区分几何适应与有意顺应性的难题，通过任务参数化的分布建模实现了无需力传感器的变阻抗技能学习，在保证任务成功率的同时兼顾了交互力与跟踪精度的平衡，验证了该方法的实用性和优越性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires precise tracking and mechanical compliance, where variable impedance control can improve robustness in task success, whereas static compliance cannot adapt to varying contact constraints. Variable impedance skills can be learned from demonstrations, avoiding complex modeling, but compliance is a hidden variable in force-agnostic kinematic data. While existing methods infer compliance from trajectory variations, these variations may reflect geometric adaptation and not intentional compliance when subject to changing spatial layouts. Therefore, this letter introduces Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy (VIDP), an imitation learning-based variable impedance control framework leveraging a Task-Parameterized Directionality-Aware Mixture Model (TP-DAMM) to extract physically consistent trajectory distributions from diverse demonstrations. By mapping distributions to stiffness profiles, VIDP jointly predicts pose actions and task compliance without force sensors. Real-world experiments show that VIDP significantly outperforms fixed-impedance baselines in task success rate while reducing interaction forces with respect to high stiffness controllers and tracking errors with respect to low stiffness baselines.

</details>


### [58] [Design and Evaluation of a Touchscreen-Based Teleoperation Interface for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06219)
*Juan José García Cárdenas,Alperen Kenan,Hamidreza Raei,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani,Arash Ajoudani,Adriana Tapus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究设计并评估了一种新颖的触屏遥操作界面，用于核工业场景下的表面接触任务（如擦拭取样）。与传统摇杆相比，该触屏界面将连续手指运动直接映射到机械臂运动，实现更精细的速度控制和控制-可视化集成。20名参与者的对比用户研究表明，触屏界面在完成时间（中位数2.50 vs 5.38分钟，减少53.5%）、路径覆盖率和超调量方面显著优于摇杆，同时降低了认知负荷（NASA-TLX从52降至43）。单次点击自主模式的认知负荷最低（31分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核工业中的表面接触任务（如擦拭取样）需要精确的路径和力跟踪、避障及持续操作员注意力，传统摇杆界面难以有效支持这些需求，亟需更直观、精确和自然的遥操作交互方式。

Method: 设计了一种将连续手指运动直接映射到机械臂运动的触屏遥操作界面，提供更精细的速度控制并集成控制与可视化。采用跨国产的Franka Emika Panda机械臂进行对比用户研究，20名受试者在触屏、传统摇杆和单次点击自主模式三种条件下执行仿真表面操作任务，采集运动学、生理学和行为学数据评估任务性能、认知负荷（NASA-TLX）和操作员信任。

Result: 触屏界面使完成时间减少53.5%（中位数2.50 vs 5.38分钟），正弦路径覆盖率达90.7%（摇杆为84.1%），两种路径几何下的超调量均更低。NASA-TLX分数从摇杆的52降至触屏的43（-17.3%），自主模式最低为31（vs摇杆-40.4%，vs触屏-27.9%）。

Conclusion: 所提出的触屏遥操作界面易于实施，在遥操作表面任务中显著提升了操作效率与精确度，同时降低了操作员认知负荷，为核工业等挑战性环境中的遥操作提供了有前景的替代交互方案。

Abstract: Intuitive teleoperation interfaces are crucial for the safe and effective operation of robotic manipulators in challenging environments. In the nuclear industry, surface contact tasks such as swab sampling require precise path and force tracking, obstacle avoidance, and sustained operator attention, which conventional joystick interfaces struggle to support effectively.
  This study designs and evaluates a novel touchscreen teleoperation interface that maps continuous finger movements directly to robotic manipulator motions, provides finer velocity control, and integrates control with visualization, enabling more natural, precise, and intuitive surface interaction than conventional controllers. A comparative user study with 20 participants evaluated task performance and workload using the proposed touchscreen, a conventional joystick, and a single-click autonomous mode. Tasks simulated realistic surface manipulation using a Franka Emika Panda arm, remotely controlled from another country. Kinematic, physiological, and behavioral data were recorded to comprehensively assess task performance, cognitive load, and operator trust across each control condition.
  Participants completed teleoperation tasks more efficiently and accurately with the touchscreen interface, achieving a 53.5% reduction in completion time (median: 2.50 vs. 5.38 min), higher in-area coverage on the sinusoidal path (90.7% vs. 84.1%), and lower overshoot on both path geometries compared with the joystick. Cognitive load, quantified via NASA-TLX (0-100), decreased from joystick to touchscreen (mean TLX 52 to 43; -9 points, -17.3%) and was lowest under the autonomous one-click mode (31; -21 points vs. joystick, -40.4%; -12 vs. touchscreen, -27.9%). This research presents an easy-to-implement touchscreen interface that improves performance in teleoperated surface tasks while reducing cognitive load.

</details>


### [59] [Robot Learning from Human Demonstrations: Handwritten Alphabet Trajectories and Human-Likeness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06221)
*Alperen Kenan,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从示教学习中生成类人机器人运动的框架，涵盖数据采集、概率轨迹学习和感知用户评估。通过收集3,142条笔迹演示数据（覆盖52种拉丁字母大小写组合），在GMM/GMR方法基础上引入力和归一化时间维度，并适配非连续的多元轨迹。用户研究（21人）显示生成轨迹的类人评分为71.50（SD=22.56），多数轨迹被感知为更接近人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类与机器人交互中，类人运动被认为是建立信任和实现自然协作的关键。然而，传统编程方式难以赋予机器人类人运动特征，亟需一种从人类示教中学习运动技能并生成类人轨迹的框架。

Method: 框架包括三部分：(1)通过触摸屏遥操作界面从22名参与者收集3,142条笔迹演示（涵盖52种拉丁字母大小写组合），记录平面位置、接触力和时间信息；(2)在标准GMM/GMR基础上扩展，引入力和归一化时间维度以更丰富地表征人类动力学，并改进以处理非连续、多段轨迹以实现跨演示泛化；(3)通过21名参与者的用户研究，使用0-100连续评分量表（50为中性中点）评估生成轨迹的类人程度。

Result: 生成轨迹的整体类人评分为71.50（SD=22.56），表明大部分轨迹被感知为更类人。参与者认为几何定位和轨迹序列是最具影响力的感知因素，并对类人机器人行为持积极态度。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了从示教数据中学习并生成被感知为类人的机器人运动，数据集以开源形式发布，为后续类人机器人运动方法的开发和评估提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) provides a developmental framework through which robots can develop motor skills by observing and imitating human dynamics, reducing reliance on explicit programming to teach a skill to a robot. The resulting human-like robot motion is recognised as a key factor in building trust and enabling natural collaboration in human-robot interaction. This paper presents a framework for learning human-like robot motion from demonstration, including data collection, probabilistic trajectory learning, and perceptual user evaluation. A dataset of 3,142 handwriting demonstrations was collected from 22 participants across all 52 Latin alphabet character-case combinations via a touchscreen teleoperation interface, capturing planar position, contact force, and timing. Building on the widely used Gaussian Mixture Model and Gaussian Mixture Regression approach for learning from demonstration, the framework is extended in this work by incorporating force and normalised time dimensions to enable richer representation of human dynamics, and adapting it to handle non-continuous, multi-segment trajectories, enabling generalisation across demonstrations. A user study with 21 participants evaluated the perceived human-likeness of the generated trajectories using a continuous scale anchored between robotic and human-like motion, normalised to 0-100 where 50 represents the neutral midpoint. The generated trajectories achieved an overall human-likeness score of 71.50 (SD=22.56), indicating that the majority of trajectories were perceived as more human-like. Participants identified geometric positioning and trajectory sequence as the most influential perceptual factors, and reported positive attitudes toward human-like robot behaviour. The datasets are released as open-source, providing a reproducible benchmark for developing and evaluating human-like robot motion methods.

</details>


### [60] [GeniWorld: A Generalizable Interactive World Model for Robotic Manipulation via Visual Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06332)
*Chenghao Gu,Hanyang Yu,Jingbo Zhang,Haitao Lin,Wenyao Zhang,Jinghe Wang,Hanglei Jin,Shuzhao Xie,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeniWorld是一个基于预训练视频生成模型的交互式世界模型，通过URDF渲染将数值动作转化为视觉动作表征，解耦本体运动学与环境动力学，实现跨未见场景的零样本泛化，并可作为策略评估器和数据生成器提升下游策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在复杂未见环境中鲁棒性有限，而真实世界的大规模数据采集与评估成本高昂。现有的动作条件世界模型存在动作可控性差和对分布外场景泛化能力弱的问题。

Method: 1) 基于预训练视频生成模型构建；2) 使用URDF-based渲染将数值动作转换为视觉动作表征，实现空间有根的动作控制；3) 显式解耦本体运动学与环境动力学，缓解场景过拟合；4) 构建自回归视频预测模型并集成高频机器人运动学控制，支持闭环控制、与策略及人机遥操作交互。

Result: 即使在仅限固定场景数据训练的情况下，模型在域内任务上表现优异，并对高度随机化的未见环境实现鲁棒的零样本泛化。作为策略评估器在环境扰动下保持可靠；在有限真实演示下能生成多样化操作轨迹，提升下游策略在复杂环境中的性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GeniWorld通过解耦动作表征与环境建模，成功突破了世界模型在动作可控性和OOD泛化上的瓶颈，为机器人策略评估与数据扩展提供了可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies exhibit strong capabilities, but their robustness in complex and unseen environments remains limited. Scaling robot learning and evaluation in diverse real-world environments remains costly and challenging. Action-conditioned world models offer a promising alternative, but they often suffer from limited action controllability and poor generalization to out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To this end, we present GeniWorld, an interactive world model for robots that generalizes robustly across unseen scenarios. Building on pretrained video generative models, we use URDF-based rendering to transform numerical actions into visual action representations, enabling spatially grounded action control. By explicitly decoupling embodiment kinematics from environmental dynamics, our model mitigates scene overfitting and facilitates modeling of robot-environment interactions. To achieve closed-loop control, we construct an autoregressive video prediction model integrated with high-frequency robot kinematic control, enabling interaction with both robot policies and human teleoperators. In our experiments, even when trained solely on limited fixed-scene data, our model achieves superior in-domain performance and robust zero-shot generalization to highly randomized, unseen environments. For downstream applications, GeniWorld serves as a scalable policy evaluator that remains reliable under environmental perturbations. Furthermore, even with limited real-world demonstrations, GeniWorld generates diverse manipulation trajectories within the world model, improving downstream policy performance and robustness in complex environments.

</details>


### [61] [A Master-Salve Robot Manipulator for Needle-Based Teleoperation in MRI Chamber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06354)
*Omar Curiel,Jing-Yuan Huang,Po-Chih Chen,Ji Ma,Qing Dai,Wenqi Zhou,David Lu,Holden H. Wu,Tsu-Chin Tsao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种MRI安全的、主从式机器人操作臂，用于MRI腔室内的腹部介入手术。通过流体传动实现2+1自由度主从运动与力传递，结合数字主控制器实现多模态控制（手动、数字、混合、协作），并在初步MR扫描实验（活体猪实验）中验证了床边手动控制模式下的功能可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI引导的介入手术因成像环境对电磁兼容性要求严格，传统电驱动机器人难以进入MR腔室。现有主从或混合人机控制方案多为分裂轴或模式切换式，缺乏灵活的多模态控制能力。因此需要一种MR安全的流体驱动操作臂，能够在MRI实时引导下提供多种控制模式，以满足不同介入任务需求。

Method: 采用主从式液压传动架构：操作者通过2+1自由度主控制器将运动和力经流体管线传递至2+1自由度从端机械臂。其中，高输入阻抗、低泄漏的弹性体流体致动器负责远程角度控制；低摩擦石墨活塞气缸负责穿刺针插入轴远程驱动，满足亚牛顿级力透明度和亚毫米级运动传递精度。同时集成数字主控制器，可切换手动、数字、混合及协作四种控制模式，协作任务包括辅助组织穿刺、故障驱动虚拟约束和运动补偿。

Result: 初步MR扫描机测试结果表明，该操作臂在床边手动控制模式下，成功完成了活体猪实验，验证了系统的功能可行性与MR环境兼容性。

Conclusion: 该流体传动主从操作臂为MRI引导的腹部介入手术提供了安全、灵活的多模态人机控制方案，初步实验证明了其临床应用潜力，为未来在实时MRI引导下开展更广泛的介入手术奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a MR safe, master-slave robot manipulator for abdominal interventions in the MRI chamber. A human operated 2+1-DoF master controller manipulator transmits motion and force to a 2+1-DoF slave manipulator via fluid transmission. Jointly, a digital master controller provides multimodal control capability beyond common split axis or mode switchable hybrid human-digital controller configurations found in previous studies. High input impedance, low-leakage, elastomeric fluid actuators are delegated to remote angulation control. Low-friction graphite piston cylinders are delegated to needle insertion axis remote actuation given the sub-newton force transparency and sub-millimeter motion transmission over bedside fluid piping lengths. The device enables real-time MRI guided interventions allowing manual, digital, hybrid, and collaborative control modes. Collaborative tasks such as assisted tissue penetration, fault-driven virtual fixture, and motion compensation through feedback control are presented in this paper. Preliminary MR scanner results demonstrate manipulator functional viability for an in-vivo pig experiment in bedside, manual control mode configuration.

</details>


### [62] [DyPES-VLA: Learning Shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific Control for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06374)
*Junfeng Li,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,Yangyang Zheng,Pingyue Sheng,Jiayu Dong,Ruixin Li,Haodong Yan,Jiaguan Zhu,Tianran Zhang,Runze Yu,Wen Chen,Liuqing Yang,Yuxiang Gao,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DyPES-VLA，一种跨具身（cross-embodiment）的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过学习共享动力学先验（dynamics priors）和具身特定控制来解决异构机器人具身下的通用策略训练问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨具身VLA方法存在两大局限：1）未能充分利用跨异构视觉与交互数据中共享的动力学先验，限制了跨具身迁移；2）需要大量人工预处理将不同具身的动作转换为统一格式，成本高且不灵活。

Method: DyPES-VLA包含两大核心组件：1）共享动力学先验学习：在跨具身数据上通过未来预测（future-prediction）目标训练VLM，使共享查询表示能够捕捉物体运动、接触和交互引发的场景变化；2）具身特定MoE（Mixture-of-Experts）动作头：直接在每个具身的原生动作空间中生成可执行控制，无需手动预对齐异构动作。该动作头共享注意力层以捕捉通用时间动作结构，同时通过具身特定的前馈专家网络解决不同具身的运动学约束和控制语义差异。

Result: 作为通用策略，DyPES-VLA在仿真和真实世界评测中均达到SOTA：LIBERO上达到98.0%成功率，RoboCasa-GR1上达到59.25%，RoboTwin 2.0上达到89.02%。

Conclusion: 通过分离共享动力学先验与具身特定控制，DyPES-VLA有效解决了跨具身VLA训练中动力学利用不足和动作格式不统一的问题，证明了无需手动动作对齐即可实现高效的跨具身泛化。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a powerful paradigm for robot manipulation, but training a single generalist policy for heterogeneous robot embodiments remains an open problem. Existing methods have two main limitations. First, they underuse dynamics priors shared across diverse visual and interaction data, limiting cross-embodiment transfer. Second, they require extensive manual preprocessing to convert embodiment-specific actions into a common format. To overcome these limitations, we propose DyPES-VLA, a cross-embodiment VLA that learns shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific control. First, we learn shared dynamics priors by training the vision-language model (VLM) with a future-prediction objective on cross-embodiment data, driving the shared query representation to capture object motion, contact, and interaction-induced scene changes. Second, an embodiment-specific Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) action head translates these shared dynamics priors into executable controls directly in each embodiment's native action space, without manually pre-aligning heterogeneous actions into a common format. This head shares attention layers to capture common temporal action structures, while its embodiment-specific feed-forward experts resolve the unique kinematic constraints and control semantics of distinct embodiments. As a generalist policy, our \ourmethod achieves state-of-the-art performance across simulation and real-world evaluations, reaching 98.0% success on LIBERO, 59.25% on RoboCasa-GR1, and 89.02% on RoboTwin~2.0.

</details>


### [63] [$ω$-0: A Latent Predictive World Action Model for Concurrent Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06375)
*Zhe Li,Zhenzhe Zhang,Yangyang Wei,Wenjie Zhang,Xichen Yuan,Peiyuan Zhi,Gen Li,Xinying Guo,Fengjie Gao,Jianfei Yang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ω-0，一个用于真实世界人形机器人同时操作与移动（loco-manipulation）的潜在预测全身世界-动作模型。该模型通过语言指令、视觉观测和本体感受状态直接预测控制器兼容的全身动作潜在变量，利用轻量级未来观测嵌入作为预测目标，耦合潜在视觉前瞻与基于扩散的全身动作生成，并在11项家庭任务的真实实验中超越多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的家庭任务通常需要同时进行移动操作（loco-manipulation），要求机器人协调运动、姿态调整、平衡维持和物体操纵。然而现有的人形机器人策略通常将运动与操作分解处理，而近期世界-动作模型要么以手臂为中心，要么以视频为中心，无法满足全身协调的并发移动操作需求。

Method: ω-0是一个潜在预测全身世界-动作模型。其核心方法包括：(1) 基于语言指令、当前视觉观测和本体感受状态，直接预测控制器兼容的全身动作潜在变量可执行于真实机器人；(2) 不重建未来视频，而是学习紧凑的未来观测嵌入作为轻量级预测目标，将潜在视觉前瞻与基于扩散的全身动作生成相结合；(3) 支持第一视角RGB、外视角RGB和外视角深度输入；(4) 利用基于控制器的仿真回放将人类/公共视觉-动作先验落地为机器人可执行的动作潜在变量；(5) 收集ω-HOME数据集，包含40+小时的真实家庭人形机器人数据，具有多视角观测、全身SMPL动作、机器人状态和动作潜在变量的同步标注。

Result: 在11项家庭任务上的真实世界实验中，单一的ω-0模型能够产生平滑的"边操作边移动"行为，并一致性地优于代表性模仿学习、VLA（视觉-语言-动作）、人形机器人和世界-动作模型基线方法。

Conclusion: ω-0证明了通过潜在预测的方式进行视觉前瞻并结合扩散式全身动作生成，可以在真实世界人形机器人上实现统一的并发移动操作行为，其单模型多任务泛化能力优于传统的分解式或视频中心式方法，为真实世界人形机器人全身协调操作提供了有效的范式。

Abstract: Humanoid household tasks often require concurrent loco-manipulation, where the robot must move, adjust posture, maintain balance, and manipulate objects as a single coordinated behavior. Yet existing humanoid policies typically decompose locomotion and manipulation, while recent world-action models remain either arm-centric or video-centered. We present $ω$-0, a latent predictive whole-body world-action model for real-world humanoid concurrent loco-manipulation. Given a language instruction, current visual observation, and robot proprioceptive state, $ω$-0 directly predicts controller-compatible whole-body action latents for real-robot execution. Rather than reconstructing future videos, $ω$-0 learns compact future observation embeddings as a lightweight predictive objective, coupling latent visual foresight with diffusion-based whole-body action generation. The model supports egocentric RGB, exocentric RGB, and exocentric depth inputs, and leverages controller-based simulation replay to ground human/public visual-motion priors into robot-executable action latents. We further collect $ω$-HOME, a 40+ hour real-world household humanoid dataset with synchronized multi-view observations, whole-body SMPL motions, robot states, and action latents. Real-world experiments on 11 household tasks demonstrate that a single $ω$-0 model can produce smooth manipulate-while-moving behaviors and consistently outperform representative imitation learning, VLA, humanoid, and WAM baselines.

</details>

### [64] [Simple evolution drives direct reciprocity to maximum payoff in social dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06147)
*Martin A Nowak*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于突变和成对比较的简单进化过程，该过程能够在大群体、中等至高突变率和中等至强选择强度下，将直接互惠策略驱动至囚徒困境、雪堆、猎鹿和和谐博弈四种社会困境中的最大收益。研究发现记忆-2策略可在所有博弈中稳定达到最大收益，而记忆-1策略虽有能力解决四种困境，但通常被雪堆博弈中的"HOLD-trap"策略击败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接互惠是进化合作的重要机制，但合作是否占优势取决于进化动力学的细节。作者希望找到一种简单的进化过程，能够可靠地将直接互惠驱动至最优合作水平。

Method: 提出基于突变和成对比较的进化过程；突变用于在策略空间边界附近采样新策略，成对比较包含选择强度参数；在四种社会困境（囚徒困境、雪堆、猎鹿、和谐博弈）中系统研究该过程对大群体、不同突变率和选择强度的行为。

Result: 在大群体、中等至高突变率和中等至强选择强度条件下，该过程在四种博弈中均使策略群体达到最大收益。记忆-2策略在四种博弈中都稳定实现最大收益；记忆-1策略虽能潜在解决四种困境，但在雪堆博弈中通常被"HOLD-trap"策略所击败。

Conclusion: 研究表明，简单的基于突变成对比较的进化过程具有惊人的能力，能将直接互惠推向所有研究的社会困境中的最大收益，而策略记忆长度（记忆-1与记忆-2）对最终合作结果有显著影响，特别是记忆-1在雪堆博弈中的局限性。

Abstract: Direct reciprocity is a mechanism for evolution of cooperation based on repeated interactions between the same individuals. Direct reciprocity can help natural selection to favor cooperators over defectors, but whether or not cooperation prevails depends on the details of the evolutionary dynamics. We describe simple processes of evolution that have the astonishing ability of driving direct reciprocity to maximum payoff in all social dilemmas which we study. The basic process is based on mutation and pairwise comparison. Mutation samples strategies near the boundary of the strategy space. Pairwise comparison includes a parameter for intensity of selection. For large population sizes, intermediate to high mutation rates and intermediate to strong intensities of selection, we find that the process leads to communities of strategies that reach maximum payoff in Prisoner's Dilemma, Snowdrift, Stag Hunt and Harmony games. Maximum payoff in all four games is consistently achieved if players have access to memory-2 strategies. Memory-1 strategies have the capacity to resolve all four social dilemmas, but they are usually defeated by a ``Hold-trap'' in Snowdrift games.

</details>

### [65] [NP-Hardness of Non-Crossing Hamiltonian Path and Cycle in Non-Planar Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06255)
*Randal Tuggle,Jack Snoeyink*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文通过从3-SAT直接归约到非平面图上的非交叉哈密顿路径/回路问题，将Hamilton路径的困难性与非交叉约束的困难性分离开来，避免了先前通过平面图归约所导致的混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的困难性证明通常通过归约到平面图来完成，而在平面图中所有路径都自动是非交叉的，这混淆了哈密顿路径问题本身的困难性与非交叉约束带来的困难性，无法清晰地说明为什么仅禁止路径交叉就会使问题变得困难。

Method: 从3-SAT到非平面图上的非交叉哈密顿路径和回路问题进行直接归约，不使用平面图构造，将困难性明确放在非交叉约束上。

Result: 给出了一个更透明的证明，该证明不依赖平面结构的gadget构造，并将难题的根源明确归结于非交叉约束本身。

Conclusion: 该直接归约方法比以往的方法更清晰，且可能更容易推广到相关的其他问题。

Abstract: We seek to disentangle the hardness of finding a Hamiltonian path or cycle from the hardness of finding a non-crossing path or cycle by giving a direct reduction from 3-SAT to the non-crossing Hamiltonian path and cycle problems on non-planar graphs. Prior hardness proofs proceed by reduction to planar graphs, where every path is automatically non-crossing; this conflates the two sources of difficulty and leaves unclear why forbidding crossings on the path alone makes the problem hard. Our reduction places the difficulty squarely in the non-crossing constraint, avoids planar gadget constructions, and yields a more transparent proof that may be easier to extend to related problems.

</details>

### [66] [Rigidity of Averages over the Two Largest Prime Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05191)
*Dijia Chen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Alladi-Johnson猜想是否定的：不存在有界函数f使得其对最大素因子P₁(n)和第二大素因子P₂(n)的平均值收敛到不同极限。具体地，P₁(n)平均值的收敛会强制P₂(n)平均值以相同极限收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alladi和Johnson提出疑问：是否存在有界函数f作用于素数上，使得n的最大素因子P₁(n)和次大素因子P₂(n)的函数平均（在[2,x]范围内除以x）分别收敛到两个不同的极限κ₁≠κ₂。这个问题的本质是两种不同素因子统计量是否可能表现出不同的平均行为，如果存在，将揭示数论中不同阶素因子分布的深刻差异。

Method: 本文采用解析数论方法。首先在log-log尺度上，将两个平均值表示为与显式Dickman核的卷积。关键步骤是证明与P₁-平均值相关的Dickman核的傅里叶变换在实数轴上没有零点。利用Wiener的Tauber型定理，可以推出与加权素数和相关的平移测度的vague收敛性，由此进而导出第二个平均值（P₂平均值）的收敛性。

Result: 证明了对任意有界函数f，若P₁(n)的函数平均值收敛，则P₂(n)的函数平均值必然收敛到相同极限。因此Alladi-Johnson问题的答案是：不存在这样的有界函数f使得κ₁≠κ₂。

Conclusion: 该结果否定了Alladi和Johnson的猜想可能性，表明最大素因子和第二大素因子的平均行为在极限意义下本质上是一致的，即P₂分布并不比P₁表现出独立的行为。这也展示了Dickman核与Wiener Tauber型定理在数论中处理此类分布问题的有效性。

Abstract: Let \(P_1(n)\) and \(P_2(n)\) be the largest and second-largest distinct prime factors of \(n\), respectively. Alladi and Johnson asked whether there exists a bounded function \(f\) on the primes for which both limits \(\frac{1}{x}\sum_{2\le n\le x} f(P_1(n)) \longrightarrow κ_1\) and \(\frac{1}{x}\sum_{2\le n\le x} f(P_2(n)) \longrightarrow κ_2\) exist with \(κ_1\neqκ_2\), where we set \(f(P_2(n))=0\) when \(n\) is a prime power. We prove that this is impossible: convergence of the first average forces convergence of the second to the same limit.
  On the \(\log\log\)-scale, the two averages are expressed as convolutions with explicit Dickman kernels. The Fourier transform of the Dickman kernel associated with the \(P_1\)-average has no real zeros. Wiener's Tauberian theorem then yields vague convergence of the translated measures associated with the weighted prime sums. The convergence of the second average follows.

</details>


### [67] [Modularity of Point Counts for the Curves $X^a=Y^b$: New Rogers--Ramanujan Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05480)
*Kenny Lau,Ken Ono*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Huang–Jiang–Oblomkov 关于互素对 (a,b) 的连通幂零矩阵对 X^a=Y^b 的点计数猜想中 a=3 的全部层级：建立了新的无限族 Rogers–Ramanujan 恒等式，并给出了 Warnaar 乘积的几何起源。这些恒等式还通过了 Lean 形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Huang、Jiang 和 Oblomkov 提出猜想：对于互素 1<a<b，有限域上满足 X^a=Y^b 的连通幂零矩阵对的点计数本质上是一个模函数（即 Γ(a+b) 上的模函数），等价地其生成函数等于涉及 Jacobi theta 函数和 Dedekind eta 函数的显式乘积。a=2 的情况涵盖了经典结果如 Rogers–Ramanujan 和 Andrews–Gordon 恒等式，但 a≥3 时此前没有任何结果。本文旨在解决 a=3 这一完整层级。

Method: 作者为 a=3 层级构造了新的无限族 Rogers–Ramanujan 型恒等式，并揭示了 Warnaar 乘积的几何来源——即这些乘积正是与 a=3 情形下幂零矩阵对点计数相关的组合对象。证明采用组合与几何相结合的方法，并借助 AxiomProver 在 Lean 中将新恒等式基于已有文献进行了机器验证。

Result: 成功证明了 a=3 层级的全部内容：包括新发现的无限族 Rogers–Ramanujan 恒等式，以及 Warnaar 乘积的几何起源。所有新恒等式均已在 Lean 中通过 AxiomProver 验证（假设现有文献成立）。

Conclusion: 本文为 Huang–Jiang–Oblomkov 猜想在 a=3 的情形提供了完整证明，将点计数问题、q-级数恒等式与模形式理论联系起来，并展示了形式化验证在当代组合数论研究中的应用价值。这为 a≥4 的更高层级研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: For coprime $1<a<b$, let $M_n^{a,b}(\mathbb{F}_q)$ be the set of commuting pairs of nilpotent $n\times n$ matrices over $\mathbb{F}_q$ with $X^a=Y^b$. Huang, Jiang, and Oblomkov assembled their orders as an Eulerian $q$-series
  $Z_{a,b}(q)$. They conjectured that it is an explicit product $P_{a,b}(q)$ involving Jacobi's theta function and Dedekind's eta-function, implying the threefold equality $$\underbrace{\prod_{n\ge1}(1-q^n)\cdot\Biggl(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{|M_n^{a,b}(\mathbb{F}_q)|}{|\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{F}_q)|}\Biggr)\Biggr|_{q\mapsto q^{-1}}}_{\text{point count}}\;=\;\underbrace{Z_{a,b}(q)}_{q\text{-series}}\;=\;\underbrace{P_{a,b}(q)}_{\text{theta quotient}}$$ If true, the point count on $X^a=Y^b$ is essentially a modular function on $Γ(a+b)$. The conjecture is layered in $a$, with an identity for each $b$. The $a=2$ layer is classical, including identities of Rogers--Ramanujan and Andrews--Gordon. For $a\geq3,$ nothing was known. We prove the $a=3$ layer in full: a new infinite family of Rogers--Ramanujan identities, and a geometric origin for Warnaar's products. AxiomProver verified these new identities in Lean assuming existing literature.

</details>


### [68] [Explicit Algebraic Numbers all whose Integer Parts of Powers are Composite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05309)
*Suyeong Hahn,Dan Ismailescu,Ganghun Kim,Minseung Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了几类明确的代数整数α>1，使得对于所有但有限个正整数n，⌊α^n⌋均为合数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究形如⌊α^n⌋的序列是否为合数的问题，构造具有该性质的代数整数类。

Method: 通过构造几类明确的代数整数α>1，证明其整数部分序列⌊α^n⌋在除有限个例外后均为合数。

Result: 给出了几类显式的代数整数α>1，满足⌊α^n⌋对于几乎所有的n都是合数。

Conclusion: 成功构造了满足所要求性质的代数整数类，推进了关于组合序列素性与合性分布的研究。

Abstract: We present several explicit classes of algebraic integers $α>1$ such that $\lfloor α^n\rfloor$ is composite for all but finitely many $n$.

</details>


### [69] [The sesquicentennial of the prime number $2^{127} - 1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05319)
*Matthew Baker*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文回顾了1876年数学家Édouard Lucas证明39位数字2^127-1（M_127）为素数的历史事件，该素数保持了75年最大已知素数的纪录，也是最后一个无需机械辅助发现和验证的最大素数。文章还给出了与之密切相关的Lucas-Lehmer检验的现代证明，该检验至今仍是验证大素数的主要工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纪念2026年即将到来的M_127素数证明150周年，回顾这一数学史上标志性成就的历史背景，并重新审视Lucas-Lehmer检验这一至今仍在大素数认证中发挥核心作用的经典算法，为读者提供历史与现代证明相结合的综合视角。

Method: 文章采用历史文献综述与现代数学证明相结合的方式：首先梳理1876年Lucas证明M_127为素数的历史脉络与背景，然后给出Lucas-Lehmer检验的现代、严格化的数学证明，将经典结果纳入当代数论框架。

Result: 该素数在75年间保持最大已知素数的纪录，至今仍是唯一一个在不借助机械设备条件下发现并验证的最大素数。Lucas-Lehmer检验至今仍是大素数认证的主要引擎，其现代证明已被系统化呈现。

Conclusion: Lucas的成果不仅是数学史上的一座里程碑，其思想所催生的Lucas-Lehmer检验在现代计算数论中依然发挥着不可替代的作用，展示了经典数学成果的持久生命力。2026年的150周年纪念既是对历史的致敬，也是对现代素数研究连续性的强调。

Abstract: The year 2026 marks the 150th anniversary of a remarkable achievement in mathematics: in 1876, the French mathematician Édouard Lucas showed that the 39-digit number $M_{127} := 2^{127} -1$ is prime. This stood for 75 years as the largest known prime, and it remains the largest prime number discovered and certified without the aid of a mechanical device. We review the history behind this discovery and give a modern proof of the closely related Lucas-Lehmer test, which is still the main engine behind the certification of large prime numbers.

</details>


### [70] [Trianguline representations and locally analytic principal series of ${\rm GL}_2({\mathbb Q}_p)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05396)
*Matthias Strauch,Zichuan Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了p进Langlands对应中与绝对不可约2维p进Galois表示ρ相关的酉Banach空间表示Π(ρ)，若其局部解析表示Π(ρ)^la具有一个子商，同构于某个局部解析主序列表示的非零子商，则ρ必为trianguline表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解p进Langlands对应中表示Π(ρ)的局部解析向量结构如何反映底层Galois表示ρ的性质，特别是建立局部解析表示子商结构与ρ是否为trianguline之间的内在联系。

Method: 利用Colmez、Dospinescu、Paškūnas、Emerton等人的已有结果，通过分析局部解析表示Π(ρ)^la的子商结构，特别是其与局部解析主序列表示子商之间的同构关系，推出ρ的trianguline性质。

Result: 证明了若Π(ρ)^la存在一个子商同构于局部解析主序列表示G的非零子商，则ρ是trianguline表示。这一结果刻画了局部解析表示结构与Galois表示trianguline性之间的充分条件关系。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了p进Langlands对应中局部解析表示的子商性质与Galois表示的trianguline性之间存在密切联系，为深入理解p进Langlands对应的结构提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Let $ρ$ be an absolutely irreducible 2-dimensional $p$-adic representation of the absolute Galois group of ${\mathbb Q}_p$, and let $Π(ρ)$ be the unitary Banach space representation of $G = {\rm GL}_2({\mathbb Q}_p)$ associated to $ρ$ by the $p$-adic Langlands correspondence. We deduce from results due to Colmez, Dospinescu, Paškūnas, Emerton, and others that if the locally analytic representation $Π(ρ)^\rm{la}$ has a subquotient isomorphic to a non-zero subquotient of a locally analytic principal series representation of $G$, then $ρ$ is trianguline.

</details>


### [71] [Asymptotic uncorrelations between functions with squarefull kernel and functions of invariant average](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05470)
*Xiang Su,Biao Wang,Shaoyun Yi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了平方full核算术函数（s-函数）及其移位卷积和在与素数Omega函数轨道相关的唯一遍历动力系统中的渐近不相关性，从而得到了素数定理的精化版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ivić和Tenenbaum在1986年引入了s-函数，Erdős和Ivić给出了其移位卷积和的渐近估计。近年来，Bergelson和Richter在唯一遍历拓扑动力系统中研究了素数Omega函数的轨道，建立了素数定理的新动力学推广。本文旨在将s-函数及其移位卷积纳入这一动力学框架，探索它们与素数Omega函数轨道之间的相关性，并借此精化素数定理。

Method: 作者利用唯一遍历拓扑动力系统中沿素数Omega函数轨道的遍历平均方法，结合s-函数的算术性质及其移位卷积的渐近分析，证明了s-函数及其移位卷积与该动力系统中的轨道渐近不相关，从而建立了与素数Omega函数轨道之间的独立性结果。

Result: 本文证明了s-函数及其移位卷积在唯一遍历系统中与沿素数Omega函数的轨道渐近不相关。作为推论，通过s-函数的局部分布获得了素数定理的一个精化。此外，还建立了这些结果的若干变体。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将s-函数的算术分析与唯一遍历动力系统的框架相结合，证明了s-函数与素数Omega函数轨道的渐近不相关性，并以此精化了素数定理，拓展了该方向的研究成果。

Abstract: In 1986, Ivić and Tenenbaum introduced arithmetic functions with squarefull kernel, which are also called $s$-functions. Later, Erdős and Ivić gave an asymptotic estimate on the shifted convolution sums of $s$-functions. Recently, Bergelson and Richter studied the orbits along the prime Omega function in a uniquely ergodic topological dynamical system and established a new dynamical generalization of the prime number theorem (PNT). These orbits can be viewed as functions of invariant average under multiplications. In this paper, we show that both $s$-functions and their shifted convolutions are asymptotically uncorrelated to the orbits along the prime Omega function in a uniquely ergodic system. As a consequence, we obtain a refinement of the PNT via the local distribution of $s$-functions. Furthermore, several variants of these results are established as well.

</details>


### [72] [Iwasawa invariants and exceptional pairs of certain abelian fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05517)
*Kazato Nada,Haruma Sasaki,Hiroki Sumida-Takahashi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究二次域Q(√d)与p次分圆域复合后的Iwasawa不变量及例外对(p,χω_p^k)。通过模素理想计算特殊算术元素，确定了Iwasawa模的Λ-同构类，并在|d|<200、p<2,000,000（以及|d|<10、p<30,000,000）范围内发现了15个新的例外对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Iwasawa理论中例外对（exceptional pairs）的结构差异，理解χω_p^k部分的Iwasawa不变量的行为，特别是λ不变量等于2的例外情形。

Method: 对奇素数p和p次本原单位根ζ_p，通过模素理想计算特殊算术元素（special arithmetic elements），研究Q(√d,ζ_p)的Iwasawa不变量；利用Dirichlet特征χ（对应二次域Q(√d)）和Teichmüller特征ω_p，分析Λ-模的同构类，并区分例外对与非例外对的非分歧扩张（p外）结构差异。

Result: 确定了Iwasawa模的Λ-同构类，描述了例外与非例外对在p外非分歧扩张方面的结构差异；在指定范围内报告了15个新的例外对，其中包含(p,k,d)=(28,679,999, 2,284,521, -8)，其χω_p^{1-k}部分的Iwasawa λ不变量等于2。

Conclusion: 通过算术元素的计算方法成功发现了新的例外对并刻画了Iwasawa模的同构结构，为理解Iwasawa理论中例外情形的算术意义提供了新的数据与结构性认识。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime number and $ζ_p$ a primitive $p$-th root of unity. By computing special arithmetic elements modulo prime ideals, we have investigated the Iwasawa invariants and exceptional pairs $(p,χω_p^k)$ of $\mathbf{Q}(\sqrt{d},ζ_p)$. Here $χ$ denotes the Dirichlet character associated to $\mathbf{Q}(\sqrt{d})$, and $ω_p$ the Teichmüller character at $p$. First, we determine $\varLambda$-isomorphism classes of Iwasawa modules, and describe structural differences between exceptional and non-exceptional pairs in terms of unramified extensions outside $p$. Next, we report 15 new exceptional pairs in the range $|d|<200$ (resp.~$|d|<10$) and $p <2,\!000,\!000$ (resp.~$p <30,\!000,\!000$), including $(p,k,d)=(28,\!679,\!999,\;2,\!284,\!521,-8)$ for which the $χω_p^{1-k}$-part of the Iwasawa $λ$-invariant is equal to two.

</details>


### [73] [Generalised Gauss--Kuzmin Distribution for Klein sails in $\mathbb R^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05853)
*Gaurav Aggarwal,Konstantin Andritsch*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了三维Klein帆中局部面结构等分布的三维类比结果，推广了经典Gauss-Kuzmin分布（二维情形描述连分数展开数字的渐近分布）。作者通过齐次动力学方法，在SL₃(R)/SL₃(Z)上为对角流构造了一个横截面，使得对横截面的访问精确编码三维帆的几何信息，并证明了横截面测度的有限性，从而推导出极限面统计量，同时解决了Karpenkov提出的若干开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Gauss-Kuzmin分布描述连分数数字的渐近分布，在几何上可解释为R²中Klein帆面的等分布。本文的目标是将此现象推广到三维情形，即证明R³中一般Klein帆的局部面结构的等分布，从而获得Gauss-Kuzmin分布的高维推广，并解决Karpenkov提出的开放问题。

Method: 基于齐次动力学方法，受Kontsevich和Suhov工作的启发。具体地，在SL₃(R)/SL₃(Z)上为对角流构造一个横截面，使得动力系统对该横截面的访问精确编码三维帆的局部几何结构。核心技术贡献是证明该横截面测度是有限的，这是推导极限面统计量的关键。

Result: 证明了三维Klein帆中一般（generic）帆的局部面结构的等分布性质，建立了Gauss-Kuzmin分布的三维类比；同时解决了Karpenkov提出的多个开放问题。

Conclusion: 该工作通过齐次动力学框架成功将Gauss-Kuzmin分布的几何解释从二维推广到三维，证明了横截面测度的有限性，为更高维度的推广提供了方法论基础和技术储备。

Abstract: The classical Gauss--Kuzmin distribution describes the asymptotic distribution of continued fraction digits. Geometrically, this may be interpreted as the equidistribution of faces of Klein sails in $\mathbb R^2$.
  In this paper, we establish a three-dimensional analogue of this phenomenon. We prove the equidistribution of local face structures in generic Klein sails in $\mathbb R^3$, thereby obtaining a higher-dimensional generalization of the Gauss--Kuzmin distribution. In addition, we resolve several open questions posed by Karpenkov~\cite{Ka17}.
  Our approach is based on homogeneous dynamics and is motivated from the work of Kontsevich and Suhov~\cite{KS99}. More precisely, we construct a cross-section for the diagonal flow on $\operatorname{SL}_3(\mathbb R)/\operatorname{SL}_3(\mathbb Z)$, such that visits to the cross-section encode the geometry of three-dimensional sails. The principal technical contribution is the proof that the associated cross-sectional measure is finite, which enables us to derive the limiting face statistics.

</details>


### [74] [Hearing Tamagawa Factors modulo $q - 1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05868)
*Patrick Erik Bradley*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文发现阿贝尔簇的Tamagawa因子与p-adic Laplace积分算子在Néron模型的有理点上的小波特征值模q-1同余，通过将典范测度下的体积与局部L-因子和Tamagawa因子联系起来，并利用Serre不变量及前期关于“听Serre不变量”的结果证明该同余。

Assuming the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis, we compute the one-level density of the low-lying zeros of this family for test functions whose Fourier transform is supported in $(-\frac35, \frac35)$. As a consequence, we obtain the upper bound $\frac{13}{6}$ for the average analytic rank $r(E_d)$ over the family. Under the additional assumption of a conjecture on the distribution of quartic Gauss sums at prime elements (a quartic analogue of Patterson's conjecture for cubic Gauss sums), we extend the admissible support to $(-1, 1)$ and improve the upper bound for the average analytic rank to $\frac32$. Method: (1) 提出观测-wise representer公式，将参数到观测映射的灵敏度与移动边界状态及伴随系统关联；(2) 在Gaussian先验的Cameron-Martin空间上推导约化的Levenberg-Marquardt方法用于MAP估计，并给出线性化MAP近似的显式有限秩协方差，使得LM更新和协方差组装仅依赖观测空间的线性代数运算；(3) 在1D中利用显式移动边界解建立后验适定性和MAP估计器存在性，推导闭式Fréchet导数与representers；(4) 在2D中引入非常弱混合公式耦合压力、界面速度和域演化，通过形式化单侧方向形状线性化获得线性化状态和伴随系统，每个观测通过独立的并行伴随问题计算representers。

Abstract: We develop an infinite-dimensional Bayesian framework for recovering spatially varying log-permeability from pressure observations in single-phase Darcy flow with a moving boundary, motivated by resin transfer moulding. The main contribution is an observation-wise representer formulation linking sensitivities of the parameter-to-observable map to the moving-boundary state and adjoint systems. This yields a reduced Levenberg--Marquardt method for maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimation on the Cameron--Martin space of a Gaussian prior and an explicit finite-rank covariance for the linearised MAP (LMAP) approximation. Once the representers are computed, the LM update and covariance assembly reduce to linear algebra in observation space, independently of the discretisation dimension.
  In 1D, the explicit moving-boundary solution is used to establish posterior well-posedness and existence of MAP estimators, and to derive closed-form Fréchet derivatives and representers. In 2D, we introduce a very weak mixed formulation coupling pressure, interface velocity and domain evolution. A formal one-sided directional shape linearisation yields the corresponding linearised state and adjoint systems, with observation-wise representers computed through independent, parallel adjoint problems.
  Numerical experiments show that the 1D LMAP approximation closely matches a reference MCMC posterior while requiring \(\mathcal{O}(10^1)\) rather than \(\mathcal{O}(10^5\text{--}10^6)\) forward simulations. In 2D, the method produces reconstructions and uncertainty estimates comparable to ensemble Kalman inversion with large ensembles, at substantially lower computational cost. The workflow is demonstrated across several geometries and experimental configurations without reformulating the inversion methodology.

</details>

A low-rank Tucker reconstruction framework is incorporated to efficiently capture the priors, resulting in coefficients for basis function estimation and a tensor optimization. In addition, we provide a theoretical analysis which establishes a non-asymptotic reconstruction error bound that characterizes the effects of sampling complexity, optimization convergence, and model mismatch. A consistency--stability argument is then used to derive second-order convergence in time together with optimal-order convergence in space. Abstract: Recently, novel integrability techniques, and most notably Birkhoff coordinates followed by an explicit formula, have been introduced to solve the Benjamin--Ono (BO) equation, leading to major theoretical and computational advances. We propose to perturb this explicit BO formula via a novel splitting method for numerically solving a class of PDEs, which includes the Intermediate Long Wave (ILW) equation, as well as other quasilinear equations that are not necessarily integrable. Using the Birkhoff coordinates of BO we establish first-order convergence in the $H^s$-norm ($s \ge 0$) while requiring only one additional derivative on the initial data $u_0 \in H^{s+1}_{0}$, significantly improving upon the regularity requirements of typical splitting methods for nonlinear dispersive equations. Furthermore, we prove that in the deep-water limit, the scheme for the ILW equation converges to the BO solution. Computational advantages of these schemes are shown in simulations: unlike classical splitting methods, our approach does not require a restrictive quadratic time step condition to nearly preserve energy over long time scales, thus rendering efficient and accurate long-time simulations feasible. As an application, we numerically explore the soliton resolution conjecture for both the ILW and a KdV--BO equation.

Abstract: We develop and analyze a family of divergence-free kernel interpolation methods for tangential vector fields on the unit sphere. Starting from scalar radial kernels in Euclidean space, we construct tangent-valued, surface divergence-free matrix kernels without repeatedly applying surface differential operators. The construction separates the geometric enforcement of the divergence-free constraint from the choice of scalar generator and admits lower-order variants with reduced regularity requirements compared with classical potential-based methods. Using vector spherical harmonics, we derive explicit kernel representations and characterize their Fourier multipliers. We also introduce an inverse Laplace-Beltrami construction that preserves the multipliers of the underlying scalar zonal kernel. For interpolation at scattered nodes, we establish a lower bound for the smallest eigenvalue of the interpolation matrix and derive pointwise and Sobolev error estimates, including superconvergence for targets smoother than the native space. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical convergence and stability results, while additional examples on nonspherical surfaces illustrate the applicability of the kernel formula beyond the sphere.

Abstract: The Craig-Bampton (CB) method is a widely used dynamic substructuring technique based on component mode synthesis (CMS). The higher-order Craig-Bampton (HCB) method augments the CB basis with residual modes from a Neumann series expansion of the residual flexibility matrix, where HCB-n retains terms up to the n-th order and is reduced back to the CB size via the System Equivalent Reduction Expansion Process (SEREP), achieving improved accuracy at the same model dimension. However, assessing the accuracy of a reduced model without solving the full-order problem remains a fundamental challenge: if the full-order solution is required to evaluate the error, the purpose of model reduction is defeated. Despite the demonstrated superiority of the HCB method, no a priori error estimator (one that predicts eigenvalue errors without solving the full-order eigenvalue problem) has been proposed for it. The present work addresses this gap with a hierarchical estimation framework that exploits the nested Ritz subspace structure of the HCB method, where each higher-order solution serves as a reference for estimating the error of the preceding order. The framework provides (i) a generalized CB error estimator derived from a Rayleigh quotient perturbation analysis, and (ii) a novel HCB-1 error estimator using the HCB-2 eigensolution as a reference. Numerical examples across models of varying geometric complexity validate both estimators.

</details>

Abstract: Quasi-static electroporoelasticity couples Maxwell's equations with Biot's poroelasticity through electrokinetic coupling between the electric field and the pressure gradient. By introducing the total pressure, the electroporoelasticity equations are reformulated as a five-field system to address poroelastic locking in the nearly incompressible regime. For the resulting five-field system, a monolithic weak formulation is derived, together with a continuous stability estimate. A second-order backward differentiation formula (BDF2) time discretization and a mixed finite- element spatial discretization are then introduced, yieling to a fully discrete monolithic scheme. Building on this scheme, we develop an iterative decoupling method that alternates between an electromagnetic subproblem and a poroelastic subproblem, and prove its geometric convergence to the monolithic solution with an explicit mesh-independent contraction factor under the physical coupling condition. An algebraically equivalent reduced form is also presented, in which the electromagnetic block is solved only once per time step, while the poroelastic block is solved iteratively with electric-field correction updates obtained from the pressure-gradient feedback. Numerical experiments verify the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the locking-free performance.

Abstract: This paper presents a novel higher-order multi-scale (HOMS) computational framework for efficient, high-accuracy, and low-cost simulation of nonlinear hygro-thermo-mechanical (H-T-M) coupling problems in heterogeneous structures. The inherent nonlinearity in the investigated model stems primarily from temperature- or moisture-dependent material properties, and this model also accounts for temperature-dependent internal heat source and moisture sink terms induced by exothermic, moisture-consuming chemical reactions (e.g., hydration). The main contributions of this work are as follows. First, a high-accuracy multi-scale asymptotic model incorporating higher-order correction terms is constructed for nonlinear H-T-M coupling problems in heterogeneous structures with highly spatial inhomogeneity, using the multi-scale asymptotic approach together with Taylor series expansions. Second, rigorous error estimates in both point-wise and integral senses are derived for the multi-scale asymptotic solutions, which theoretically demonstrate the necessity and superiority of the proposed HOMS method. Third, an efficient two-stage numerical algorithm with off-line and on-line stages is developed, based on finite difference and finite element methods, and its convergence is also proved rigorously. Finally, two- and three-dimensional numerical experiments are performed to assess the computational performance of the proposed HOMS approach, showing excellent numerical accuracy and robustness with low computational overhead.
