Abstract: In this work, we present two novel strategies to impose high-order boundary conditions on fixed and moving curved domains, approximated with piecewise affine triangulations. Achieving high-order accuracy on curved domains requires tackling both the PDE discretization error and the geometrical error simultaneously. While the former can be reduced by employing high-order numerical methods such as finite volume and discontinuous Galerkin, the latter demands either a high-order parametrization of the physical domain or a consistent approximation of the boundary conditions. Minimization-based approaches like the Reconstruction for Off-site Data (ROD) method allow one to skip the construction of high-order curvilinear meshes by defining high-order consistent boundary conditions on a computational boundary that does not match the physical one. The ROD approach mitigates the second-order geometrical error by retrieving a modified polynomial in each boundary cell, which enforces the boundary conditions exactly on the physical boundary. However, the standard ROD method requires the inversion of a local linear system, whose cost grows with the polynomial degree and mesh refinement. Inspired by a recent one-dimensional analysis, we show that ROD-type approaches can be recast as simple polynomial corrections, applicable without any linear system inversion. This greatly simplifies the development of minimization-based boundary treatments and reduces the associated computational cost. To prove the wide applicability of our strategy, we develop it within a Runge-Kutta discontinuous Galerkin framework and an ADER arbitrary-Lagrangian-Eulerian finite volume framework for compressible flows on fixed and moving domains. Several numerical experiments with Dirichlet and slip-wall boundary conditions are presented, with convergence analysis up to fifth order in both 2D and 3D.

</details>

Abstract: We derive an explicit one-step kernel formula for the kinetic Fokker-Planck equation. The resulting kernel formula provides an explicit approximation of the density evolution. In a weighted phase-space setting, we establish local weak consistency and first-order finite-time weak convergence under suitable regularity and moment assumptions. We also give a formal variational interpretation of the formula in terms of a regularized Wasserstein-type proximal operator and present analogous constructions for a broader class of parabolic equations. Numerical experiments illustrate the accuracy of the kernel approximation and its application to deterministic particle schemes for kinetic sampling.

</details>

Abstract: Deep neural networks have shown great empirical success in the solution of a wide variety of ill-posed inverse problems in imaging. Yet, very few works have studied their behavior in the limit that turns the discretized ill-conditioned problems into truly ill-posed ones, i.e., for an increasing resolution of the discretization. In this work, we review common approaches to neural operator learning in architectures that resemble a U-Net, one of the most common classical architectures for inverse imaging problems. We discuss advantages and drawbacks of the respective approaches, consider a 1D toy example for improved interpretability, and present extensive numerical experiments on how different types of neural operator U-Nets can improve a first (crude) limited angle CT-reconstruction. In particular, we study how well networks trained for a certain resolution of the discretization generalize to other resolutions. Our finding is that while U-shaped neural operator architectures are by design resolution-invariant, the classical U-Net architecture seems to be more robust with respect to resolution changes than expected.

<details>

Abstract: We study a fully implicit Rothe time discretization for a cutoff first-order formulation of the Westervelt equation. The key ingredients are the enthalpy variable and the primitive mobility variable, which turn each nonlinear time step into a uniformly monotone elliptic problem and avoid higher-order energy estimates and inverse inequalities. For every time step, the discrete problem reduces to a monotone elliptic equation, which yields well-posedness of the Rothe scheme by the Browder-Minty theorem. We derive a discrete energy inequality, establish compactness for the transformed variable by an Alt-Luckhaus type argument, and pass to the limit to obtain existence of weak solutions for the cutoff first-order system. We prove a weak-strong uniqueness principle for the cutoff problem and formulate a conditional a posteriori criterion under which the cutoff is inactive. For sufficiently regular solutions of the forced cutoff problem, we establish consistency estimates which identify the temporal residuals produced by the Rothe approximation in the natural discrete-in-time weak norms. These estimates provide a rigorous consistency basis for the observed temporal behavior. Finally, numerical experiments illustrate the stability of the scheme, its observed near first-order temporal behavior, and inactivity of the cutoff along the computed discrete trajectories in the tested regimes.

</details>

Abstract: Reduced Basis (RB) methods provide low-dimensional approximations of parametrized partial differential equations with controllable accuracy. We discuss the construction of RB approximations for transient thermal simulation of an induction motor and compare their performance to Finite Element (FE) models and Lumped Parameter Thermal Networks (LPTNs) after calibration to measurements. Numerical results demonstrate that RB models can achieve FE accuracy, allow for a fully automatic construction, and offer computational efficiency comparable to traditional LPTNs.

Abstract: This work develops a thermodynamically consistent phase-field model for tumor growth based on the energetic variational framework. The proposed scheme is rigorously proved to be unconditionally energy stable and mass conservative. Furthermore, optimal first-order temporal error estimates are established for the tumor phase-field variable, the nutrient concentration, and the fluid velocity.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Mechanistic chromatography models are valuable for process development, but gradient-based parameter estimation and optimization can be hindered by computational cost and the effort of deriving objective-function gradients. To address this concern, a fully differentiable Julia chromatography solver, ChromOps.jl, is presented that combines high-order spatial discretization with discrete forward sensitivity analysis (DFSA). Two high-order spatial discretizations, finite difference summation-by-parts (FD-SBP) and the discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method (DG-SEM), are compared on a 6-component ion-exchange chromatography problem with steric mass action kinetics. Both attain their theoretical convergence rates on manufactured problems, while FD-SBP shows favourable work-precision performance for both forward (primal) and dual-valued simulations and is conceptually simpler to implement. DFSA scales as tDFSA approx 1.4(1+Np)tfwd for up to Np = 24 parameters propagated in a single ForwardDiff.jl chunk, i.e. about 1.4 forward solves per additional parameter. Because the primal and dual-valued solves share the same code path, DFSA provides accurate gradients of user-defined objectives without any hand-derived chain-rule code, enabling accessible gradient-based parameter estimation and optimization.

</details>

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimising an energy over an unknown manifold that is given implicitly by a point cloud. For a known manifold one can define a gradient descent scheme analogously to the classical construction in Euclidean spaces. However, when the manifold is not known one has to simultaneously estimate the manifold whilst minimising the energy. We define a gradient descent scheme which remains in a neighbourhood of the manifold and, under suitable stability and sampling assumptions, converges to a neighbourhood of a local minimiser whose size vanishes as the time step and sampling errors vanish. As an example we show the application of the methodology to learning operator corrections in inverse problems.

</details>

### [97] [Soprano voices in opera seria: a corpus-based inquiry into eighteenth-century vocal types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05257)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 18世纪意大利正歌剧中女高音占据主导地位，存在四种跨性别扮演配置（男女歌手皆可扮演男女角色）。作者假设作曲家会根据首演歌手和角色性别进行针对性的音乐写作，因此需要通过统计方法系统地探究声乐类型、角色性别与音乐风格选择之间的关系。

Method: 构建约1,700首咏叹调的数据集，采用统计模型分析声乐类型、角色性别与音乐风格特征之间的关系。对比多种监督学习方法，最终选择岭回归逻辑回归作为主要模型，因其在性能上与最佳替代方法相当，同时允许对系数的解释和不确定性量化。

Result: 研究发现一种不对称性：角色性别在音乐写作中被持续编码，而演唱者性别几乎无法从音乐中恢复。男性角色咏叹调具有大音程跳跃、音程变异性和更宽的音域；女性角色咏叹调则偏好更高的最高音、小音程和旋律稳定性。女歌手演唱音高高于男高音歌手，但这与角色塑造的相关性高于生理差异。

Conclusion: 作曲家创作时更多依据角色的性别而非演唱者的生理性别来调整音乐写作风格，表明性别表达在歌剧音乐中是通过角色塑造来实现的，而非由演唱者的身体特征直接决定。

Abstract: Eighteenth-century Italian opera seria was dominated by soprano voices. Although masculine roles were typically performed by castrati and feminine roles by women, cross-casting was common, producing four soprano configurations in which performers of either sex could portray characters of either gender. We hypothesize that composers tailored their writing both to the singers premiering their arias and to the characters' gender. Using statistical models built on approximately 1,700 arias, we analyze relationships between vocal typology, characters' gender, and stylistic choices, comparing supervised learning methods and selecting ridge logistic regression as our primary model, whose performance is competitive with that of the best-performing alternative while allowing for interpretation and uncertainty quantification of its coefficients. Results reveal an asymmetry: character gender is consistently encoded in the writing, while the singer's sex is barely recoverable. Masculine-character arias feature large melodic leaps, intervallic variability, and wider ranges; feminine-character arias favor higher maximum pitches, minor intervals, and melodic stability. Female singers sang higher pitches than male sopranos, but this correlates more with character portrayal than with physiology.

</details>


### [98] [Conditional multivariate functional PCA for the reconstruction of temperature and salinity profiles partially sampled by deep-diving marine mammals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05376)
*Nadège Fonvieille,Christophe Guinet,Baptiste Picard,David Nerini*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多元函数主成分分析（multivariate functional PCA）的统计方法，利用雌性南象海豹生物记录仪获取的部分采样温度/盐度剖面，重建南印度洋海域的垂直温盐条件。通过对完整双变量剖面的协方差结构进行参数估计并构建特征函数基，结合条件估计和地理协变量，可将截断至250 m的模拟剖面扩展到500 m深度域，温度与盐度重建精度分别提升30%和33%，并成功重建约9万个不完整剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南象海豹携带的生物记录仪虽能提供前所未有的时空覆盖，但其最大记录深度随动物行为而变化，导致温盐剖面仅能部分采样，无法获得完整的垂直环境视图。需要一种统计方法来重建缺失的深层温盐数据。

Method: 采用多元函数主成分分析（multivariate functional PCA），从一组完整的双变量（温度+盐度）剖面中参数化估计协方差结构和均值函数，构建特征函数基。考虑测量误差方差后，通过条件估计将部分采样的温盐剖面投影到完整剖面的特征空间中，从而在定义域上进行重建。同时引入地理协变量以提升重建精度。

Result: 在模拟实验中，截断至250 m的剖面重建到[20,500] m范围时，加入地理协变量使温度重建精度提高30%，盐度提高33%。实际应用中，基于约1万个到达500 m的完整剖面，成功重建了约9万个不完整剖面，覆盖法国亚南极群岛周边约300万平方公里区域。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效利用部分采样的生物记录仪数据进行垂直温盐场重建，显著扩展了数据覆盖的深度范围，为南大洋海洋环境监测提供了一种可行的统计重建框架。

Abstract: We present a statistical method to reconstruct the vertical thermohaline conditions in the Indian Sector of the Southern Ocean, where temperature and salinity profiles are partially sampled by female southern elephant seals. Datasets collected by biologgers provide unprecedented spatial and temporal coverage of ocean conditions. However, the maximum recorded depth varies with the animals' behaviour, offering only a partial view of the vertical environment. Using multivariate functional Principal Component Analysis (PCA), a parametric estimation of the covariance structure and mean function from a set of complete bivariate profiles allows the construction of an eigenfunction basis. By accounting for measurement error variance, partially sampled temperature and salinity profiles can be projected into the eigenspace of the complete profiles through conditional estimation of their functional principal coordinates and then reconstructed over the defined domain. For simulated snippet profiles truncated at depth $z_{\max} = 250$ m and reconstructed over $\mathcal{Z} = [20,500]$ m, reconstruction accuracy increases by 30 % for temperature and 33 % for salinity when incorporating geographical covariates. We then reconstruct ~90,000 incomplete profiles from the multivariate functional PCA of ~10,000 profiles reaching 500 m, covering approximately 3 million km$^2$ around the French subantarctic islands.

</details>


### [99] [Modeling E-Bike Route Choice in Washington, DC: A Path Size Logit Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05449)
*Yiheng Qian,Zehui Yin,Darren M. Scott,Xiang Yan*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究利用华盛顿特区Capital Bikeshare的GPS轨迹数据，构建包含观测路线与最短路径的混合选择集，估计Path Size Logit模型，结合GIS基础设施变量与街景图像（SVI）视觉特征，揭示电动自行车骑行者的路线选择偏好。发现骑行者倾向避开与机动车和行人的冲突、保持行程连续性；自行车设施在主干道上的影响远大于支路；长距离出行对骑行基础设施偏好更强；街景视觉特征（尤其是树木）能提升模型表现但影响小于道路基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动自行车（e-bike）路线选择行为对骑行基础设施规划至关重要，但现有实证证据十分有限，需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 使用Capital Bikeshare系统的GPS轨迹数据，构建由观测路线和对应最短路径组成的混合选择集，估计Path Size Logit模型，并将GIS基础设施变量与从街景图像（SVI）中通过计算机视觉提取的街景视觉特征相结合。

Result: 电动自行车骑行者倾向于选择与机动车和行人冲突最小且保持行程连续性的路线；道路层级显著调节自行车设施的影响，主干道上自行车设施的作用远大于支路；长距离出行对骑行基础设施偏好更强；街景视觉特征可提升模型表现但效果小于道路基础设施，其中树木是唯一始终呈现正效应的绿化元素。

Conclusion: 研究提供了电动自行车时代骑行基础设施规划的实际证据，标准化效应量可识别最具行为重要性的路线属性，为相关政策制定和设施规划提供科学依据。

Abstract: Understanding e-bike route choice is essential for developing effective cycling infrastructure, yet empirical evidence remains limited. This study investigates shared e-bike route choice in Washington, DC, using Global Positioning System (GPS) trajectory data from the Capital Bikeshare system. A Path Size Logit model is estimated using a hybrid choice set consisting of observed routes and corresponding shortest paths, integrating Geographic Information System (GIS)-based infrastructure variables with computer vision-derived street-level visual features extracted from Street View images (SVI). The results indicate that e-bike riders tend to choose routes that minimize conflicts with both motor vehicles and pedestrians while maintaining travel continuity. Roadway hierarchy substantially moderates the influence of bicycle facilities, with the presence of bicycle facilities having a much greater impact on route choice along major roads than along minor roads. Longer trips also exhibit stronger preferences for cycling infrastructure. Incorporating street-level visual features improves model performance, although their effects are generally smaller than those of road infrastructure, with trees being the only greenery component showing a consistently positive effect. Standardized effect sizes further identify the most behaviorally important route attributes. These findings provide practical evidence for cycling infrastructure planning in the e-bike era.

</details>


### [100] [How Infrastructure and Streetscape Shape E-Scooter Route Choice: Evidence from Washington, DC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05465)
*Yiheng Qian,Duanya Lyu,Wenwen Zhang,Steve Hankey,Meng Qi,Xiang Yan*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究利用华盛顿特区的GPS轨迹数据和Path Size Logit模型，结合计算机视觉提取的Google Street View街道景观特征，分析了电动滑板车的路线选择行为。研究发现自行车道设施的效果取决于道路环境：主干道仅有物理隔离的自行车道能提升吸引力，而次干道中指定车道和保护车道均有增益；人行道是最频繁的骑行环境，但只有沥青铺设的人行道才带来正效用；树冠覆盖、建筑和墙体覆盖与路线选择正相关。道路基础设施对路线选择的影响强于视觉街道景观特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动滑板车已成为重要的微出行模式，但关于其路线选择行为的实证证据仍然有限。理解骑行者的路线偏好对于优化基础设施投资至关重要。

Method: 采用Path Size Logit模型分析GPS轨迹数据，结合道路和基础设施特征，并利用计算机视觉技术从Google Street View图像中提取视觉街道景观特征纳入模型。

Result: 自行车设施效果依赖道路环境：主干道仅受保护自行车道有效，次干道中指定车道和保护车道均有增益；人行道仅在沥青铺设时带来正效用；树冠覆盖、建筑和墙体覆盖与路线选择正相关。道路基础设施影响强于视觉特征，但后者提供额外解释力。

Conclusion: 研究支持针对性的基础设施投资，并将街道景观改善作为增强微出行路线吸引力的补充策略。

Abstract: E-scooters have emerged as an important micromobility mode for short urban trips, yet evidence on route choice behavior remains limited. This study examines e-scooter route choice in Washington, DC using GPS trajectory data and a Path Size Logit model. In addition to roadway and infrastructure characteristics, the model incorporates visual streetscape features extracted from Google Street View imagery using computer vision techniques. The results show that the effectiveness of cycling infrastructure depends strongly on roadway context. On major roads, only protected bicycle facilities significantly increase route attractiveness, whereas on minor roads both protected and designated lanes provide utility gains. Sidewalks constitute the most frequently used riding environment, yet only asphalt-paved sidewalks are associated with positive utility, suggesting that sidewalk riding may reflect the absence of attractive on-street alternatives rather than a preference for pedestrian infrastructure. Tree coverage, particularly during summer, as well as building and wall coverage, are positively associated with route choice. Likelihood ratio tests and value-of-distance analysis indicate that roadway infrastructure exerts a stronger influence on route choice than visual streetscape features, although the latter provide additional explanatory power. These findings support targeted infrastructure investment and the integration of streetscape improvements as a complementary strategy for enhancing micromobility route attractiveness.

</details>


### [101] [Long-Term Mortality Following STN-DBS in Parkinson's Disease: A Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05609)
*Malinda Iluppangama,Dilmi Abeywardana*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究对214名接受丘脑底核脑深部电刺激（STN-DBS）的帕金森病患者进行回顾性生存分析，发现中位生存期约134个月，5/10/15年死亡率分别为8.0%/42.5%/56.0%。年龄≥60岁植入显著增加死亡风险（HR=4.689），而术后至少一次翻修手术降低死亡风险（HR=0.296），患者满意度高达97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STN-DBS是帕金森病（PD）成熟的治疗方法，能改善运动症状和生活质量，但关于其长期生存率和死亡风险因素的研究尚不充分，需要更系统的量化评估为临床决策提供依据。

Method: 对214例接受STN-DBS的PD患者进行回顾性生存分析：采用Kaplan-Meier方法估计生存概率和中位生存期，用log-rank检验比较不同性别和植入年龄组的生存差异；使用Cox比例风险（Cox-PH）模型在单因素和多因素分析中识别死亡相关因素，并用加速失效时间（AFT）模型进行验证。

Result: 死亡率随时间递增，5年、10年和15年死亡率分别为8.0%、42.5%和56.0%，中位生存期约134个月。性别间生存无显著差异（p=0.63），但植入时年龄≥60岁的患者生存显著更低（p<0.0001）。术后至少一次翻修手术降低死亡风险（HR=0.296, p<0.001），植入年龄≥60岁增加死亡风险（HR=4.689, p<0.001），两者在多因素调整后仍显著并由AFT模型确认。52名受访患者中97%表示满意，96%愿意推荐STN-DBS。

Conclusion: STN-DBS与PD患者的长期生存和高患者满意度相关。高龄植入增加死亡风险，而翻修手术与死亡率的关系可能反映适应症混杂而非直接因果效应。研究支持按年龄分层的患者筛选策略，并强调STN-DBS术后长期监测的重要性。

Abstract: Background: Deep brain stimulation of the subthalamic nucleus (STN-DBS) is an established PD treatment that improves motor symptoms and quality of life, but long-term survival and mortality risk remain poorly characterized. This study evaluated long-term survival and mortality-associated factors after STN-DBS.
  Methods: A retrospective survival analysis of 214 PD patients who underwent STN-DBS used Kaplan-Meier methods to estimate survival probabilities and median survival, with log-rank tests comparing survival by gender and age at implantation. Cox proportional hazards (Cox-PH) models identified mortality-associated factors in both univariate and multivariate analyses, and were validated using accelerated failure time (AFT) models.
  Results: Mortality rose progressively, with 8.0%, 42.5%, and 56.0% of patients dying within 5, 10, and 15 years, respectively; median survival was approximately 134 months. Survival did not differ by gender (p = 0.63) but was significantly lower for patients aged 60 years or older at implantation (p < 0.0001). At least one post-implantation revision reduced mortality risk (HR = 0.296, p < 0.001), while implantation at age 60 years or older increased it (HR = 4.689, p < 0.001); both remained significant after adjustment and were confirmed by AFT models. Patient satisfaction was high, with 97% of 52 surveyed patients reporting satisfaction and 96% would recommend STN-DBS.
  Conclusion: STN-DBS is linked to long-term survival and high patient satisfaction in individuals with PD. Older age at implantation increased mortality risk, whereas revision surgery and mortality likely reflect confounding by indication rather than a direct causal effect. These findings support age-stratified patient selection and highlight the importance of continued long-term monitoring following STN-DBS.

</details>


### [102] [Information leakage from data revisions in retrospective forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05883)
*Johannes Bracher,Sebastian Funk*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文指出Aygün等人声称其ERA系统在2024/25季度新冠住院预测中大幅超越CDC集成模型，但作者证明这一性能提升源于回顾性预测设置中的信息泄漏——即未考虑数据修订问题，并警示类似机制在其他预测领域同样存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示AI驱动预测系统中因回顾性设置导致的信息泄漏问题，纠正可能误导的结论，并对整个AI辅助预测建模领域提出普遍性警示。

Method: 对Aygün等人的ERA系统预测结果进行复现与审查，识别出他们未纳入数据修订（data revisions）这一关键因素，从而证明性能优势来自信息泄漏而非模型本身的真实预测能力。

Result: 证明ERA系统观察到的性能增益是回顾性预测设置中数据修订被忽略所导致的虚假优势，并非真正的预测能力提升。

Conclusion: 信息泄漏机制在许多预测领域普遍存在，本警示不仅适用于流行病预测，对整个新兴的AI辅助预测建模领域都具有重要借鉴意义。

Abstract: Aygün et al (2026, https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-026-10658-6) claim that their AI-driven Empirical Research Assistance (ERA) system produces COVID-19 hospitalisation forecasts which outperform the state-of-the-art CDC ensemble by a considerable margin for the 2024/25 season. We demonstrate that the observed performance gain is attributable to information leakage in the retrospective forecasting setup, which resulted because data revisions were not taken into account. As similar mechanisms are at play in many other forecasting fields, our cautionary tale applies not just to epidemic forecasting, but is relevant to the entire emerging field of AI-assisted predictive modelling.

</details>


### [103] [Evaluating the influence of treatment-effect heterogeneity on discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06002)
*Florie Bouvier,Etienne Peyrot,Francois Petit,Raphaël Porcher*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了在20种不同分布下，oracle条件平均处理效应（CATE）模型可实现的最佳判别能力，并评估了三种判别指标（c-statistic for benefit、concentration of benefit和PAPE）的行为差异。研究发现三种指标对处理效应异质性的敏感度不同，其中concentration of benefit即使在异质性极低的情况下也能达到完美判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化医疗中需要识别哪些患者能从特定治疗中获益，而条件平均处理效应模型的判别能力对指导治疗决策至关重要。然而，关于根据人群治疗效应的潜在分布能够实现多大判别能力的研究甚少，缺乏对判别上限的理论认知。

Method: 在20种具有不同平均处理效应和异质性水平的人群分布下，计算oracle CATE（即真实理想CATE模型）可达到的判别效果。使用三种判别指标进行评估：c-statistic for benefit、concentration of benefit和population average prescriptive effect（PAPE）。

Result: 三种判别指标对治疗效应异质性的要求不同：实现高c-statistic for benefit和PAPE值需要更大的处理效应异质性，而concentration of benefit在极低异质性下即可达到高判别结果。三个指标在不同分布下的行为差异显著，例如concentration of benefit在治疗效应异质性可忽略的设置中也能指示完全判别。

Conclusion: 不同判别指标对处理效应异质性水平的敏感度存在本质差异，研究者在评估CATE模型的判别能力时需谨慎选择指标，并理解其与潜在治疗效应分布之间的关系。

Abstract: Analyzing the heterogeneity of treatment effects is crucial in personalized medicine to identify which patients will benefit from specific treatments. The performance of a conditional average treatment effects model to guide treatment decisions can be assessed in different ways, with an important one being the model's ability to effectively discriminate between individuals who benefit from the treatment and those who do not. While many methods and algorithms have been proposed to develop conditional average treatment effects models and individualized treatment rules, little is known about the discriminative ability that can be achieved according to the population's underlying distribution of treatment effects. In this work, we computed the discrimination that can be achieved under oracle CATE for a panel of 20 distributions with varying average treatment effects and levels of heterogeneity. The assessment included the following discrimination metrics: the c-statistic for benefit, the concentration of benefit, and the population average prescriptive effect (PAPE). Results showed that the three metrics employed in this study did not require the same levels of treatment effect heterogeneity to lead to high discrimination results. Notably, achieving high c-statistic for benefit and PAPE values required greater heterogeneity than obtaining high concentration of benefit values. The three metrics considered behave very differently across the distributions. For instance, the concentration of benefit can indicate perfect discrimination in settings with negligible treatment-effects heterogeneity.

</details>


### [104] [Longitudinal wearable monitoring and polygenic risk for incident major depressive disorder in the All of Us Research Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06063)
*Yuezhou Zhang,Amos A. Folarin,Rongrong Zhong,Hyunju Kim,Shaoxiong Sun,Callum Stewart,Richard JB Dobson*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究整合基因组、电子健康记录和纵向Fitbit可穿戴设备数据，探讨MDD多基因风险评分与行为特征的交互作用对重度抑郁症（MDD）发病风险的影响，发现PRS与可穿戴行为特征存在交互效应，整合后模型区分度显著提升（C-index从0.637增至0.705）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重度抑郁症（MDD）风险既受稳定的遗传易感影响，也受动态行为模式影响，但以往研究很少在真实世界环境中使用长期客观数据同时考察这两个维度。作者旨在弥合遗传易感性与实时行为监测之间的鸿沟，探索两者对MDD发病的联合预测价值。

Method: 研究纳入All of Us研究计划中3,030名欧洲遗传背景成人（其中284例在180天基线期后发展为EHR记录的MDD）。使用时变Cox模型分析MDD PRS、月度可穿戴设备衍生的体力活动和睡眠特征，以及可穿戴特征与MDD PRS的交互项与MDD发病的关联。模型顺序整合PRS、基线可穿戴特征、月度可穿戴特征及交互项，评估C-index提升。

Result: 较高的MDD PRS、较低的每日步数、较低的中低强度和高强度体力活动、较低的睡眠效率以及较大的睡眠时长变异性均与MDD发病风险增加相关。久坐时间和睡眠时长变异性与MDD的关联在不同PRS水平下有差异，高PRS参与者中风险关联更强。PRS分层风险比曲线显示，高PRS参与者需要更有利的行为水平（如更高步数、更稳定的睡眠）才能达到与低PRS参与者相当的估计风险。逐步整合各项预测因子使C-index从0.637提升至0.705。

Conclusion: 研究支持遗传易感性与纵向真实世界行为监测对MDD发病风险表征的互补价值，为未来遗传信息指导的数字表型分析用于个性化风险监测和预防提供了基础。

Abstract: Major depressive disorder (MDD) risk reflects both stable inherited liability and dynamic behavioral patterns, yet these dimensions are rarely examined together using long-term objective data in real-world settings. Here, we integrated genomic, electronic health record (EHR), and longitudinal Fitbit wearable data from 3,030 adults of genetically inferred European ancestry in the All of Us Research Program, 284 of whom developed EHR-recorded incident MDD after a 180-day baseline period. Time-varying Cox models examined associations of MDD polygenic risk scores (PRS), monthly wearable-derived physical activity and sleep features, and interactions between wearable features and MDD PRS with incident MDD. Higher MDD PRS, lower daily steps, lower light and vigorous physical activity, lower sleep efficiency, and greater sleep duration variability were associated with higher risk of EHR-recorded incident MDD. The associations of sedentary time and sleep duration variability with incident MDD differed across MDD PRS levels, with stronger risk associations among participants with higher MDD PRS. PRS-stratified hazard ratio curves further indicated that comparable estimated risk corresponded to more favorable behavioral levels (such as higher daily step counts and more stable sleep) among participants with higher MDD PRS than among those with lower MDD PRS. Sequentially integrating MDD PRS, baseline wearable features, monthly wearable features, and selected interactions between wearable features and MDD PRS increased model discrimination, with the C-index increasing from 0.637 to 0.705. These findings support the complementary value of inherited liability and longitudinal real-world behavioral monitoring for incident MDD risk characterization and may inform future work on genetically informed digital phenotyping for personalized risk monitoring and prevention.

</details>

### [105] [Contour integral methods and model order reduction for parametric linear control systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05363)
*Serkan Gugercin,Mattia Manucci*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轮廓积分方法（CIM），用于高效计算控制形式参数线性系统在指定时间区间内、达到用户预设精度的输出。CIM通过沿修正积分轮廓的求积规则近似逆拉普拉斯变换，并与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）有效结合，其中贪心算法依据推导的误差估计构建投影空间。该框架天然满足Hermite插值条件，大幅降低了参数域上输入-输出关系的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数线性系统的输出计算在控制工程中广泛存在，但直接求逆拉普拉斯变换或在高维参数空间中进行仿真计算代价高昂。现有投影MOR方法在处理参数化系统的精度与效率平衡上仍有不足，因此需要一种既能保障精度又能降低成本的数值方法。

Method: 1) 采用轮廓积分方法，沿修正积分轮廓应用求积规则来逼近逆拉普拉斯变换；2) 针对参数化系统，将CIM与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）相结合，利用贪心算法构建投影空间；3) 推导了该设定下的误差估计以指导贪心选择；4) 证明了该投影框架天然满足Hermite插值条件，从而增强插值精度。

Result: 在基准非参数与参数控制系统中验证了方法的精度和效率，与最先进的基于投影的MOR方法相比，CIM结合贪心投影MOR在降低参数域上输入-输出评估成本的同时保持了预设精度。

Conclusion: CIM与投影MOR的结合为参数线性控制系统的输出计算提供了一种高效且精确的框架，其Hermite插值性质进一步增强了性能，适用于广泛的输入函数和由低维子空间良好捕捉的初始条件。

Abstract: This paper introduces a contour integral method (CIM) for efficiently computing outputs of parametric linear systems in control form over specified time intervals and to a user-prescribed accuracy. The CIM approximates the inverse Laplace transform via a quadrature rule applied along a modified integration contour. For parametric systems, we show how CIM integrates effectively with projection-based model order reduction (MOR) where a greedy algorithm builds the projection spaces following an error estimate we derive for this setting. We additionally demonstrate that the developed projection framework naturally enforces Hermite interpolation conditions. This combination substantially lowers the cost of evaluating the input-output relations across the parameter domain, for a wide range of input functions, and for initial conditions well captured by a low-dimensional subspace.. We demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the approach on benchmark non-parametric and parametric control systems, comparing against state-of-the-art projection-based MOR methods.

</details>


### [106] [An Exact Solution of the Two-Ball Multi-Look Search Game with Three Boxes and Heterogeneous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05406)
*Igor Kleiner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出了异质多查找搜索成本博弈的精确解：游戏价值是三个显式有理函数的最大值；最优Hider策略是限制在三种、五种或全部六种摆放位置上的乘积型分布；非浪费型确定性Searcher策略与72棵基本决策树支付等价，仅诱导42种不同的开箱次数分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异质多查找搜索成本博弈（Hider将两个相同球分布在三个盒子中，Searcher自适应开箱寻找两球）的精确解问题，该问题此前未有完整精确解决方案。

Method: 通过三个参数区域分别求解：第一个区域用解析论证；第二个区域用两个精确Searcher混合策略；第三个区域用四个精确Searcher混合策略。使用有理Bernstein基证书验证参数化混合策略在整个成本区域的可行性，并通过独立实现交叉验证策略集、零和线性规划价值和154个dyadic节点。

Result: 游戏价值是三个显式有理函数的最大值；最优Hider策略为限制在三种、五种或全部六种摆放位置的乘积型分布；非浪费型确定性Searcher策略支付等价于72棵基本决策树，产生42种不同的开箱次数分布；所有参数化混合策略的可行性均通过精确有理Bernstein基证书得到验证，独立实现结果完全一致。

Conclusion: 该异质多查找搜索成本博弈已被完全解决，精确的价值函数、双方最优策略均已给出，并通过多种独立验证手段确保了正确性。

Abstract: A Hider distributes two identical balls among three boxes whose search costs satisfy $a\ge b\ge c>0$. A Searcher opens boxes adaptively until both balls are found; every opening incurs the corresponding box cost and recovers at most one ball. We give an exact solution of this heterogeneous multi-look search-cost game. The value is the maximum of three explicit rational functions. In the three parameter regimes, an optimal Hider strategy is the product-form distribution restricted respectively to three, five, or all six placements. Every non-wasteful deterministic Searcher policy is payoff-equivalent to one of $72$ elementary decision trees, which induce only $42$ distinct opening-count profiles. An analytic argument settles the first regime. Two exact Searcher mixtures settle the second, and four settle the third. Feasibility of the parameterized mixtures over the full cost region is established by exact rational Bernstein-basis certificates. Independent implementations reproduce the policy set, the zero-sum linear-program values, and all $154$ dyadic nodes examined by the Bernstein verifier.

</details>


### [107] [A Relaxation-Based Decomposition Approach for Solving a Supported-Evacuation Problem in Wildfires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05413)
*Shahryar Moradi,Antoine Sauré,Jonathan Patrick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对火灾期间医院患者和长期护理居民等弱势群体的疏散问题，提出了一种两阶段随机优化模型，同时优化设施选址、车队规模和车辆路径（含严格时间窗约束）。为解决NP难复杂度，作者提出了基于逻辑Benders分解的创新求解方法，结合组合Benders割和逻辑不等式。通过在科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区火灾演练的真实数据上进行的数值实验，证明了该方法相比替代策略能显著提升避难所选址、车辆利用率和整体疏散效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火灾疏散研究中，针对弱势群体（如医院患者、长期护理居民）的有支持疏散（supported evacuation）这一关键领域尚未得到充分研究，现有方法难以在不确定性下同时优化设施选址、车队规模和路径规划，且问题固有的NP难特性带来了巨大求解挑战。

Method: 构建两阶段随机优化模型，整合设施选址、车队规模确定和车辆路径规划，并施加严格时间窗约束以保障弱势群体疏散的时效性。为应对NP难复杂度，提出基于逻辑Benders分解（Logic Based Benders Decomposition）的创新求解框架，其中引入组合Benders割（Combinatorial Benders Cuts）与逻辑不等式（logic based inequalities）来加速主问题与子问题的收敛。

Result: 利用科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区火灾演练的真实数据进行大规模数值实验，结果表明所提方法能够获得高质量解，相较于替代策略在避难所布局、车辆利用率和整体疏散效率方面均有显著改善。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了弱势群体火灾支持疏散优化领域的空白，所提出的基于逻辑Benders分解的求解方法在保证解质量的同时有效应对了问题的计算复杂性，为社区火灾疏散规划提供了具有实际应用价值的决策支持工具。

Abstract: This study addresses the critical yet under researched area of supported evacuation for vulnerable populations during wildfires, such as hospital patients and long term care residents, by developing a two stage stochastic optimization model that optimizes facility location, fleet sizing, and vehicle routing under strict time windows. To overcome the problem NP hard complexity, the authors propose an innovative solution methodology leveraging Logic Based Benders Decomposition, featuring Combinatorial Benders Cuts and logic based inequalities. Extensive numerical experiments and real world data from a community wildfire drill in Roxborough Park, Colorado, demonstrate that the proposed approach yields high quality solutions, significantly improving shelter placement, vehicle utilization, and overall evacuation efficiency compared to alternative policies.

</details>


### [108] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化期望成本（其被积函数可能非光滑且非凸）与下半连续、近端有界函数之和。在仅要求样本量序列非减且无界（无需指定增长率）的宽松条件下，建立了函数值序列几乎必然收敛和轨迹聚点稳定性的收敛保证，并利用Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz性质将子序列收敛升级为整条轨迹收敛到单一驻点，对指数型KL去奇异函数和多项式增长的样本量导出了显式多项式收敛速率（至多差一个对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机优化方法在处理期望成本与非光滑正则项之和的问题时，通常要求正则项的（弱）凸性或对随机预言机的方差有均匀上界，这限制了方法的适用范围。本文旨在放宽这些假设，提出一种能同时处理非光滑、非凸被积函数和一般形式正则项的更普适框架。

Method: 提出近端随机次梯度方法：每次迭代用逐步细化的样本平均替换期望成本，步长由Armijo型线搜索结合充分下降性质和样本近似诱导的随机误差来选取。被积函数需满足一个非光滑局部化的下降引理变体，该假设同时涵盖具有Lipschitz梯度的光滑损失及其与凸函数之差。理论分析不要求正则项的（弱）凸性，也不要求随机预言机方差的均匀有界。

Result: 在样本量序列仅需非减且无界的宽松条件下，建立了函数值序列的几乎必然收敛性以及每个轨迹聚点的平稳性；利用KL性质将子序列收敛升级为整条轨迹收敛到单一驻点；对指数型KL去奇异函数和多项式增长的样本量，导出了函数值和迭代点的显式多项式收敛速率（至多差一个对数因子）。即使在光滑设定下这些收敛保证也是新的。

Conclusion: 该框架比现有方法适用于更一般的问题表述，放宽了对正则项凸性和随机预言机方差均匀界的假设，其收敛性分析在光滑情形下也具有新颖性，为处理非光滑非凸随机优化问题提供了更普适且理论上完备的求解方案。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

</details>


### [109] [An Inertial Block Proximal Linearized Method with Adaptive Momentum for Nonconvex and Nonsmooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05502)
*Weifeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带两阶段自适应动量的惯性块近端线性化方法(IBPL^+-TP)，用于求解一大类多块非凸非光滑优化问题。该方法引入了两阶段自适应动量策略和两个独立的加速外推点，在保证目标函数单调收敛和序列全局收敛到临界点的同时，显著提升了收敛速度。数值实验表明，该方法在稀疏非负矩阵分解和稀疏非负CP分解（均带ℓ0约束）等机器学习问题上优于多种最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多块非凸非光滑优化问题广泛存在于地震前兆分析和机器学习等实际应用中，而现有方法在加速收敛方面存在外推参数难以有效设置、约束过多等局限，需要一种既能有效加速收敛又能保证理论性质收敛的新方法。

Method: 提出惯性块近端线性化方法(IBPL^+-TP)，其核心创新包括：(1) 采用两阶段自适应动量策略动态更新外推参数；(2) 在迭代中允许使用两个不同的外推点以加速收敛；(3) 这两个外推点的外推参数彼此独立，且不受其他任何参数的约束。

Result: 理论方面证明了目标函数的单调收敛性、生成序列全局收敛到临界点，并建立了收敛速率；数值实验方面应用于带ℓ0约束的稀疏非负矩阵分解和稀疏非负CP分解问题，结果优于多个最新方法。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持理论收敛保证的同时，通过灵活的外推策略显著提升了优化效率，在非凸非光滑多块优化问题上具有明显的实际优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of multiblock nonconvex nonsmooth optimization problems, which covers many applications such as the analysis of pre-earthquake anomalies and machine learning. To solve this class of problems, we propose the inertial block proximal linearized method with two-phase adaptive momentum (IBPL$^+$-TP). Compared to the current methods, our method possesses three main advantages: (1) it introduces a two-phase adaptive momentum strategy to effectively update the extrapolation parameters, (2) it allows using two different extrapolation points to accelerate the convergence, (3) it allows the extrapolation parameters of these two extrapolation points to be independent of and unconstrained by all other parameters. While maintaining the above advantages, we prove that our method ensures the monotonic convergence of the objective function of this class of problems, and we also prove that the sequence generated by our method globally converges to a critical point, as well as establish the convergence rate of our method. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, we apply it to solve two nonconvex and nonsmooth machine learning problems, namely sparse nonnegative matrix factorization with $\ell_0$-constraints and sparse nonnegative CP decomposition with $\ell_0$-constraints. The numerical experimental results on solving these problems show that our method outperforms several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [110] [A Unified Framework for Iterate Convergence of Bregman Proximal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05536)
*He Chen,Jiaming Fan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的框架来证明Bregman近端方法（BPMs）的迭代收敛性，适用于广泛的核函数和复合目标函数。通过扩展缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质，建立了离散时间（BPM）和连续时间（mirror flow）轨迹收敛的统一理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bregman近端方法（BPMs）的迭代收敛性问题长期未解，尤其对非凸目标函数。已有工作仅对Shannon熵核和线性约束问题建立了收敛性，缺乏适用于广泛核函数和复合目标的统一理论框架。

Method: 1) 扩展了现有工作中的SKŁ性质，引入核相关的参数化函数；2) 证明当核具有闭定义域时，扩展的SKŁ性质对所有连续子解析函数成立；3) 验证标准BPMs在温和正则条件下满足框架假设；4) 基于参数化函数分析连续时间BPM（mirror flow）的收敛性。

Result: 1) 建立了适用于广泛核函数和复合目标函数的统一迭代收敛框架；2) 证明了扩展SKŁ性质对连续子解析函数成立（核具有闭定义域时）；3) 在温和正则条件下验证了标准BPMs的迭代收敛性；4) 首次在没有凸性假设或平稳点孤立假设的条件下，证明了mirror flow对o-minimal可定义目标函数收敛到平稳点。

Conclusion: 该工作为BPMs提供了离散时间和连续时间相统一的轨迹收敛理论，显著推广了已有结果，填补了非凸BPM收敛性分析的重要空白。

Abstract: Iterate convergence of Bregman proximal methods (BPMs) has long remained open, especially for nonconvex objectives. Recently, \citet{chen2026skl} made progress by establishing iterate convergence for a BPM via the so-called scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (SKŁ) property, but only for the Shannon entropy kernel and linearly constrained problems. In this paper, we develop a unified iterate convergence framework that applies to a broad group of kernels and composite objective functions. Our approach extends the analytical tools in \cite{chen2026skl}, in particular the SKŁ property, which plays a central role in ensuring convergence of the generated sequences. By introducing kernel-dependent parameterization functions, we show that the extended SKŁ property holds for all continuous subanalytic functions, particularly when the kernel has a closed domain. We then verify that the assumptions of the framework are satisfied by standard BPMs under mild regularity conditions, thereby establishing their iterate convergence for a wide range of objective functions. Furthermore, based on the parameterization functions, we show that the continuous-time BPM (mirror flow) converges to a stationary point for o-minimal definable objective functions, yielding the first trajectory convergence result for mirror flow without imposing convexity assumptions on the objective function or isolation assumptions on stationary points. Taken together, these discrete- and continuous-time convergence results provide a unified trajectory convergence theory for BPMs.

</details>


### [111] [A Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter for Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05559)
*Lei Ma,Yuzhong Hu,Xiaoming Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种重定域Yau-Yau滤波器（RD-YYF），将非线性目标跟踪中的前向Kolmogorov方程（FKE）求解限制在固定大小的、以最新状态估计为中心的局部窗口中。离线阶段利用物理信息神经网络（PINN）生成FKE解快照，并通过主成分分析构建密度演化的低维表示；在线阶段由预训练代理模型预测窗口内密度演化，再进行观测更新与状态估计。在两个几何约束目标跟踪实验中，RD-YYF的跟踪误差低于EKF和粒子滤波，同时保持高效的逐时间步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau-Yau滤波器将非线性状态估计转化为由前向Kolmogorov方程（FKE）控制的概率密度传播问题，但将其应用于目标跟踪时，需要在有限计算域上高效近似FKE，这是一个关键的技术挑战。

Method: 提出重定域Yau-Yau滤波器（RD-YYF），核心思想是在以最新状态估计为中心的固定大小局部窗口内求解FKE。离线阶段：使用PINN生成FKE解快照，通过PCA构建密度演化的低维表示，并训练一个轻量级残差代理模型，将初始条件系数和域中心映射到终端解系数。在线阶段：预训练代理模型预测重定域窗口内的密度演化，随后执行观测更新和状态估计。

Result: 在两个几何约束目标跟踪实验中，RD-YYF取得了低于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）和粒子滤波（PF）的跟踪误差，同时保持了高效的逐时间步推理。消融实验表明，域重定心技术改善了高概率区域的密度近似精度，并加速了离线PINN的收敛速度。

Conclusion: RD-YYF通过域重定心与代理模型加速相结合，在保证非线性目标跟踪精度（优于EKF和PF）的同时维持推理效率，展现了该方法在高效非线性目标跟踪中的潜力。

Abstract: The Yau-Yau filter reformulates nonlinear state estimation as probability-density propagation governed by the Forward Kolmogorov equation (FKE). Applying it to target tracking, however, requires efficient FKE approximation on a finite computational domain. This paper proposes a Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter (RD-YYF), which solves the FKE within a fixed-size local window centered at the latest state estimate. This design concentrates numerical resolution near the dominant posterior density. Offline, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) generate FKE solution snapshots, while principal component analysis constructs a low-dimensional representation of density evolution. A lightweight residual surrogate maps the initial-condition coefficients and domain center to the terminal-solution coefficients. Online, the pretrained surrogate predicts density evolution within the recentered window, followed by observation update and state estimation. Experiments on two geometrically constrained target-tracking examples show that RD-YYF achieves lower tracking errors than the extended Kalman filter (EKF) and particle filter (PF), while retaining efficient per-timestep inference. Ablation results indicate that domain recentering improves density approximation in high-probability regions and accelerates offline PINN convergence. These results demonstrate the potential of RD-YYF for efficient nonlinear target tracking

</details>


### [112] [SDDmiP.jl: A Software Package with a Provably Convergent Benders Algorithm for Multi-Stage Stochastic Mixed-Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05567)
*Akul Bansal,Simge Küçükyavuz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源软件包，实现了针对多阶段随机整数规划的贝叶斯（Benders）型分解算法，该算法具有可证明的收敛性。除了标准割族（Benders、强化Benders和Lagrangian割），该算法还整合了ReLU割，为一般的混合整数状态变量提供收敛保证。由于对偶问题常有多重最优解，文章提出基于归一化和正则化的割选择策略来强化割的质量，并引入交替割准则以在便宜与昂贵的割之间动态选择。在四类多阶段随机整数规划上的实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机整数规划的可扩展求解仍具挑战。标准Benders分解对离散状态变量缺乏收敛保证，而ReLU割虽能保证收敛，但其生成所依赖的对偶问题常有多重最优解，不同最优解产生的割在逼近子问题成本的质量上差异显著。因此需要有效的割选择策略来强化割的质量并提升算法实际性能。

Method: 该软件包实现了一个可证明收敛的Benders型分解算法，集成了Benders割、强化Benders割、Lagrangian割和ReLU割等多种割族。针对对偶问题多重最优解问题，提出了两种割选择策略：（1）基于对偶问题归一化的策略；（2）基于对偶问题正则化的策略。此外，还引入了交替割准则，在Benders割有效时使用廉价割，仅在必要时调用更昂贵但更紧的割。通过在四类多阶段随机整数规划基准问题上进行实验来评估这些方法。

Result: 计算实验在四类多阶段随机整数规划问题上验证了所提出的割选择策略和交替割准则的实用性能，揭示了问题结构对其实际表现的影响规律，表明所提出的割强化策略能够有效提升算法性能。

Conclusion: 该开源软件包为多阶段随机整数规划提供了一种可证明收敛的实用求解工具，通过割选择策略和交替割准则在计算效率与割质量之间取得了平衡，并为问题结构如何影响算法性能提供了实践洞见。

Abstract: We present an open-source software package that implements a provably convergent Benders-type decomposition algorithm for multistage stochastic integer programs. In addition to standard cut families, such as Benders, strengthened Benders, and Lagrangian cuts, the algorithm incorporates rectified linear unit (ReLU) cuts, which provide convergence guarantees for general mixed-integer state variables. However, the dual problems used to generate these cuts often admit multiple optimal solutions. Although each solution yields a valid cut that separates the incumbent, the resulting cuts can differ in how well they approximate the subproblem cost. To strengthen these cuts, our package implements and evaluates two cut-selection strategies based on normalization and regularization of the dual problem. We also incorporate an alternating-cut criterion that uses cheaper Benders cuts when they are effective and invokes more expensive tight cuts only when necessary. Computational experiments on four classes of multistage stochastic integer programs benchmark these methods and provide insights on how problem structure affects their practical performance.

</details>


### [113] [Convergence Rates for Variational Inequality Projection Neural Networks with a State-Dependent Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05574)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究闭凸集上变分不等式问题的连续时间投影神经网络，提出了一种新的状态依赖矩阵度量（其逆既非Hessian生成也非标量度量），证明了投影在参数和度量上的联合正则性，并在谱界、Lipschitz强单调算子等条件下给出局部和全局指数收敛的显式速率与半径，同时通过环形周期轨道算例和确定性计算验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对连续时间投影神经网络的收敛性分析仅覆盖两类度量：Hessian生成的逆度量（可用Bregman距离消除度量导数项）和状态依赖标量度量。现实中存在既非这两种类型的矩阵度量，其收敛性分析是空白，需要新的理论框架来刻画此类系统的动力学行为与收敛保证。

Method: 本文首先证明投影算子在自变量与度量两个参数上的联合正则性；在常见谱界条件下，将欧几里得Lipschitz估计从平方界改进到界本身。针对Lipschitz强单调算子和Lipschitz度量，采用Lyapunov方法证明局部指数收敛并给出显式收敛速率与收敛半径；在紧可行集上引入显式度量变化界，导出全局指数收敛。此外构造一个二次连续可微且一致正定的度量与强单调线性算子，证明存在周期轨道环带，并验证其逆度量在整个环带上违反Hessian可积性，最后通过确定性数值计算验证解析公式并量化收敛证书中的松弛量。

Result: 获得了投影映射在参数和度量联合变量下的正则性定理；欧几里得Lipschitz常数从O(L²)改进为O(L)；建立了局部指数收敛的显式速率和半径公式；在紧可行集上得到全局指数收敛；发现并证明了逆度量违反Hessian可积性的周期轨道环带的存在性；数值实验证实了解析公式并量化了收敛证书中的松弛度。

Conclusion: 本文填补了投影神经网络在非Hessian型、非标量矩阵度量下的收敛性理论空白，证明该类系统在Lipschitz强单调条件下仍具有指数收敛性，且收敛保证是显式和可计算的；周期轨道环带的发现揭示了此类度量系统的丰富动力学行为，并为后续研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We study continuous-time projection neural networks for variational inequalities on closed convex sets. A positive definite matrix that depends on the state preconditions the operator, and its inverse defines the projection metric. Existing convergence analyses of this flow cover Hessian-generated inverse metrics and state-dependent scalar metrics. In the first case, a Bregman distance eliminates metric-derivative terms. We treat a matrix metric whose inverse is of neither kind. We prove joint regularity of the projection in its argument and metric. Under common spectral bounds, the Euclidean Lipschitz estimate improves from the squared bound to the bound itself. For Lipschitz strongly monotone operators and Lipschitz metrics, we prove local exponential convergence with explicit rate and radius. On compact feasible sets, an explicit metric-variation bound yields global exponential convergence. A twice continuously differentiable, uniformly positive definite metric and a strongly monotone linear operator produce an annulus of periodic orbits. The inverse metric violates Hessian integrability throughout the annulus. Deterministic computations confirm the analytic formulas and quantify slack in both convergence certificates.

</details>


### [114] [An inverse mixed-integer optimization framework for learning interpretable models of expert decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05585)
*Anurag Holani,Rishabh Gupta,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种逆向优化方法，从专家的历史决策中联合学习其偏好（感知成本）和决策规则，通过三个案例研究（排班问题、生产计划问题、真实路由问题）验证了该方法在捕捉和复现专家决策方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家的决策策略经过多年经验积累，往往隐含且难以显式表达，因此需要通过观察其决策来学习可解释的决策模型，以用于新手培训、人机系统性能提升和全自主系统开发。

Method: 开发一种逆向优化（inverse optimization）方法，同时学习决策者的感知成本（偏好）和支配其选择的决策规则（约束），共同构建可解释的专家决策模型。

Result: 通过三个案例研究验证：轮班分配问题、生产计划问题和真实路由问题。结果显示，同时建模感知成本和决策规则相较于仅建模单一因素能产生更好的预测效果，证明了所提框架的有效性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 同时学习感知成本和决策规则的逆向优化框架能够更好地捕捉和复现专家决策行为，为构建可解释的专家决策模型提供了更灵活且有效的途径。

Abstract: Understanding how experts make decisions and being able to transfer that knowledge is important, especially in complex engineering applications. It is highly valuable for training novices, improving the performance of human-machine systems, and potentially enabling fully autonomous systems that perform as well as human experts. However, an expert's decision-making strategy, developed through years of experience, is often not directly accessible, since the implicit preferences and decision rules involved can be difficult to specify explicitly. This has motivated the use of observed decisions made by the expert to learn an interpretable model that captures the expert's decision-making process. In this work, we develop an inverse optimization approach to jointly learn the decision-maker's preferences (or perceived costs) and the decision rules governing their choices. We demonstrate the general applicability of our approach using three case studies that consider a shift assignment problem, a production planning problem, and a real-world routing problem, respectively. Across these case studies, modeling both perceived costs and decision rules leads to better predictions, highlighting the value of the proposed framework and its greater flexibility in capturing and replicating expert decision making.

</details>


### [115] [Fast Gradient Algorithm with Dry-like Friction and Nonmonotone Line Search for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05653)
*Lien T. Nguyen,Andrew Eberhard,Xinghuo Yu,Chaojie Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在Hilbert空间中求解可微（可能非凸）函数极小化问题的快速梯度算法，通过将凸函数的干摩擦性质推广为非凸设定下的“类干摩擦”性质，并结合线搜索技术自适应更新参数。算法在特定参数下可子序列收敛到临界点，或在Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz（KL）性质下完全序列收敛到临界点；该方法还可推广至弱凸非光滑优化问题，并适用于更广泛的KL函数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的惯性梯度算法在非凸优化中面临着收敛性分析和参数选择困难的问题，而干摩擦性质此前主要适用于凸函数。作者希望将干摩擦思想推广到非凸设定，并结合线搜索技术自适应调整参数，从而在保证收敛性的同时获得更灵活、更高效的算法框架。

Method: 1) 将凸函数的干摩擦性质扩展为非凸设定下的“类干摩擦”性质；2) 采用线搜索技术每次迭代自适应更新参数；3) 基于Moreau包络的变分性质，将算法适配到弱凸非光滑优化问题；4) 在KL框架下建立完全序列收敛性分析，并将KL指数的结果扩展到既非凸也非连续的更广泛KL函数类。

Result: 算法在参数不同选择下可实现子序列收敛到临界点或完全序列收敛到“近似”临界点；在目标函数的merit函数满足KL性质时，可建立完全序列收敛到临界点的结果。仿真结果表明算法效率高，并展示了类干摩擦与外推法及线搜索技术结合的优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的快速梯度算法由于参数的灵活性，可退化为多种现有的带Hessian阻尼和干摩擦的惯性梯度算法，同时通过线搜索和类干摩擦性质提升了收敛性能，并成功扩展到弱凸非光滑优化场景，具有广泛的理论和应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a fast gradient algorithm for the problem of minimizing a differentiable (possibly nonconvex) function in Hilbert spaces. We first extend the dry friction property for convex functions to what we call the dry-like friction property in a nonconvex setting, and then employ a line search technique to adaptively update parameters at each iteration. Depending on the choice of parameters, the proposed algorithm exhibits subsequential convergence to a critical point or full sequential convergence to an ``approximate'' critical point of the objective function. We also establish the full sequential convergence to a critical point under the Kurdyka--Lojasiewicz (KL) property of a merit function. Thanks to the parameters' flexibility, our algorithm can reduce to a number of existing inertial gradient algorithms with Hessian damping and dry friction. By exploiting variational properties of the Moreau envelope, the proposed algorithm is adapted to address weakly convex nonsmooth optimization problems. In particular, we extend the result on KL exponent for the Moreau envelope of a convex KL function to a broad class of KL functions that are not necessarily convex nor continuous. Simulation results illustrate the efficiency of our algorithm and demonstrate the potential advantages of combining dry-like friction with extrapolation and line search techniques.

</details>


### [116] [Curvature Residual Geometry in Bregman Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05680)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过最小化Bregman损失拟合线性回归模型时，即使生成势函数φ强凸，回归目标函数在参数θ上可能仍然非凸的问题。通过将Hessian表示为依赖于残差的加权Gram矩阵，推导了局部强凸性、光滑性和梯度下降条件线性收敛的条件，并针对二次-四次势函数给出了精确的标量凸性条件及负曲率区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Bregman回归假设生成势函数凸则目标函数凸，但实际中即使φ强凸，回归目标在θ上仍可能非凸，这造成了生成势凸性与模型优化几何之间的脱节。作者希望弥合这一gap，理解残差依赖的曲率如何与生成势及设计矩阵相互作用。

Method: 将回归目标的Hessian表示为加权Gram矩阵，其权重依赖于势函数的导数及当前残差。基于此表示，推导局部强凸性、光滑性和梯度下降条件线性收敛的充分条件。对二次-四次势函数（quartic potential）推导精确的标量凸性条件，识别负曲率区间，并给出正曲率的局部和全局充分条件。通过数值实验验证标量条件在Hessian仍正定时可能失效，并观察步长收敛范围随四次项参数增大而缩小。

Result: 获得了Hessian的加权Gram矩阵表示及相应的局部强凸性/光滑性/线性收敛条件。对二次-四次势给出了精确的标量凸性条件及负曲率区间。数值实验表明：标量条件在Hessian实际仍正定时可能过于保守地失效；梯度下降收敛的步长范围随四次参数增大而缩小。

Conclusion: 反向Bregman回归中，残差依赖的曲率与生成势及设计矩阵的交互是决定优化几何的关键因素。本文提供的条件为理解并刻画此类非凸Bregman回归的收敛行为奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider linear regression models fitted by minimizing Bregman losses of the form \[ \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^n \left[ φ(y_i)-φ(x_i^\topθ) -φ'(x_i^\topθ) \bigl(y_i-x_i^\topθ\bigr) \right], \] where \(y_i\) is the observed response and \(x_i^\topθ\) is the linear prediction. Even when the generating potential \(φ\) is strongly convex, the resulting regression objective may be nonconvex in \(θ\). This creates a gap between the convexity of the generating potential and the optimization geometry of the fitted model.
  The Hessian can be written as a weighted Gram matrix whose weights depend on the derivatives of the potential and the current residuals. This representation gives simple conditions for local strong convexity, smoothness, and conditional linear convergence of gradient descent.
  For the quadratic--quartic potential, we derive an exact scalar convexity condition, identify the interval of negative curvature, and obtain local and global sufficient conditions for positive curvature. Numerical experiments indicate that these scalar conditions may fail while the full Hessian remains positive definite at the evaluated points. They also indicate that the range of tested gradient-descent step sizes leading to convergence decreases as the quartic parameter grows.
  These results characterize how residual-dependent curvature interacts with the generating potential and the design matrix in reverse Bregman regression.

</details>


### [117] [A Damped Subspace Splitting Algorithm for Constrained Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05682)
*Yuanming Su,Yukuan Hu,Xin Liu,Guanghui Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于子空间分裂重构和单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法（DASSP）的约束密度泛函理论（CDFT）求解算法，该算法是首个具有严格收敛保证的CDFT计算算法，通过谱最小化、投影梯度和阻尼对偶上升三个高效步骤，在合成和真实问题上实现了高可行性和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDFT计算的离散优化问题同时包含Stiefel流形约束和非凸二次约束，现有算法要么无法高精度满足二次约束，要么因双循环迭代结构面临收敛问题，亟需一种既能严格满足约束又具有收敛保证的高效算法。

Method: 首先利用旋转不变性并引入非线性子空间对齐约束，推导出子空间分裂（subspace-splitting）重构，将两组约束解耦；基于该重构提出单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法DASSP，每轮迭代包含谱最小化步骤、投影梯度步骤和阻尼对偶上升步骤，三步均可高效实现。

Result: 在合成和真实CDFT问题上的数值结果表明，DASSP达到了高可行精度，在保持鲁棒性的同时展现出良好的计算效率。

Conclusion: DASSP作为首个具有严格收敛保证的CDFT算法，有望为可靠高效的大规模CDFT应用铺平道路。

Abstract: Constrained density functional theory (CDFT) provides a powerful framework for describing electronically excited and charge-localized states, which underlie a broad range of physical and chemical phenomena. However, the discretized optimization problems arising from CDFT calculations remain challenging, owing to the presence of both the Stiefel manifold constraint and additional nonconvex quadratic constraints. Existing algorithms either fail to enforce the quadratic constraints with high accuracy or face convergence issues due to double-loop iterative structures. In this paper, we first derive a subspace-splitting reformulation that decouples the two groups of constraints, by exploiting the inherent rotation invariance and introducing a nonlinear subspace alignment constraint. Based on this reformulation, we propose a single-loop damped alternating direction method of multipliers, called DASSP. To the best of our knowledge, DASSP is the first algorithm for CDFT calculations with rigorous convergence guarantees. Each iteration of DASSP comprises a spectral minimization step, a projected gradient step, and a damped dual ascent step, all of which admit efficient implementations. Numerical results on synthetic and realistic CDFT problems demonstrate that DASSP attains high feasibility accuracy and exhibits favorable efficiency without compromising robustness. We expect that this work will pave the way toward reliable and efficient large-scale CDFT applications.

</details>


### [118] [Quadratic Degree Sequence Optimization and the Critical Roots of a Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05827)
*Frédéric Meunier,Shmuel Onn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了度序列优化问题在二次函数情形下的复杂度，引入了图的临界根概念，并证明最优值作为二次根的函数据此呈凸分段仿射性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度序列优化问题是图论中的一个基础组合优化问题，涉及在给定图的子图中最大化顶点度数为变量的函数之和。理解其计算复杂度以及最优解的结构性质，对于图论和组合优化的理论与实践都具有重要意义。

Method: 论文聚焦于二次函数情形下的度序列优化问题，通过引入图的"临界根"（critical roots）这一新概念，将最优值关于二次根的变化划分为若干区间，并分析其结构性质。

Result: 研究表明，最优值作为二次根的函数在这些由临界根定义的区间上是凸分段仿射的（convex piecewise affine），揭示了问题解结构的几何特征。

Conclusion: 该研究为度序列优化问题在二次函数情形下提供了新的结构洞察，临界根的引入为理解问题的复杂度与解的最优结构提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The degree sequence optimization problem is to find a subgraph of a given graph which maximizes the sum over all vertices of a given function evaluated at the subgraph degree of that vertex. Here we study this problem and its complexity for quadratic functions. In particular, we introduce the critical roots of a graph, and show they define intervals over which the optimal value of the problem, as the quadratic root varies, is convex piecewise affine.

</details>


### [119] [Robust priority-aware coverage optimization for aerial sensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05873)
*Vanshika Datta,C. Nahak,J. C. Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向空中传感器网络的优先级感知鲁棒覆盖优化框架，在传感器位置存在不确定性的情况下，通过RRF鲁棒性公式化建模，最大化加权覆盖目标，并设计了PAROO算法确定传感器朝向，实验证明其在机场监控场景中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中传感器网络中传感器位置存在不确定性，而现有覆盖优化方法未同时考虑区域优先级和位置扰动鲁棒性，难以在安全敏感场景下有效分配传感资源。

Method: 构建包含监控约束和RRF（鲁棒性公式化）的数学优化模型，提出优先级感知鲁棒朝向优化（PAROO）算法，以最大化加权覆盖为目标确定最优传感器朝向。

Result: 在机场启发式监控场景的实验中，该框架能有效引导传感资源投向高优先级区域，较代表性基线方法获得更高的加权覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该框架在安全敏感环境下具有实际应用价值，实现了鲁棒性和优先级感知能力的兼顾。

Abstract: This article presents a priority-aware robust coverage optimization framework for an aerial sensor network under sensor location uncertainty. Each region is assigned a priority weight, and the objective is to maximize the weighted coverage while maintaining robustness against positional perturbations. A mathematical optimization model is developed by incorporating surveillance constraints and an RRF-based robustness formulation into the proposed framework. An efficient priority-aware robust orientation optimization (PAROO) algorithm is then proposed to determine the sensor orientations that maximize the weighted coverage objective. Experimental results on an airport-inspired surveillance scenario demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively directs sensing resources toward high-priority regions and achieves higher weighted coverage than representative baseline approaches, highlighting its practical applicability in security-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [120] [Exact Anchoring and a Dualization-Based Matheuristic for Bi-Level Dual-Defense Network Interdiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05922)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为双层防御-攻击者模型（BDAM）提供精确最优解作为评估基准，证明其攻击者路径项可通过下层对偶化转化为精确的单层MILP，并提出与精确锚点结合的MILP-DA混合求解器，在18个配置中超过此前元启发式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层拦截模型常由元启发式方法求解，但在测试规模上缺乏精确最优解，导致无法评估解的质量。作者之前用混合元启发式求解BDAM，本工作旨在提供精确的最优解基准，以评估和改进此前方法。

Method: (1) 利用下层对偶化，将BDAM中占主导地位的攻击者路径项精确重构为单层混合整数规划（MILP），该约简适用于弧长是防御者二元决策的线性函数的情况；(2) 针对供应规则在平局（ties）下不适定问题，提出威胁走廊（threat-corridor）表述以保证平局不变性；(3) 提出MILP-DA，将精确锚点与走廊解码及可行性修复配对。

Result: 在先前基准的全部18个三行和五行配置上验证了最优性，其中17个在10秒内求解；在15*30网格上获得强未认证候选解。此前的平均结果比该前沿低3.55%，差距随规模增大。平局移动目标小于10^(-3)但实现的供应成本最多波动0.83单位。MILP-DA在18个认证配置中胜出13个，在全部18个更大配置上全部获胜，所有失败均发生在三行网格上。

Conclusion: 精确单层重构使BDAM在基准规模上可被证明最优，揭示了此前元启发式质量的系统性偏差并提供了修正框架；平局非适定性问题虽被目标权重视为量化上被抑制，但在方法上仍需要求走廊表述加以消除。

Abstract: Bi-level interdiction models are frequently solved by metaheuristics whose solution quality cannot be assessed, because exact optima are unavailable at the scales tested. We supply them for the bi-level dual-defense attacker model (BDAM), which couples node interdiction, edge destruction and capacitated supply support, and which we previously solved by a hybrid metaheuristic. First, BDAM's dominant attacker-path term admits an exact single-level reformulation by lower-level dualization, a reduction available whenever arc lengths are linear in the defender's binary decisions; the resulting mixed-integer program certifies optimality on all eighteen three-row and five-row configurations of our earlier benchmark, seventeen in under ten seconds, with strong uncertified incumbents out to 15*30 grids. Our published averages sit 3.55% below that frontier, and the gap widens with scale. Second, the supply rule, like any rule priced on a single attacker shortest path, is ill-posed under ties, and our threat-corridor formulation is tie-invariant by construction. The tie-break moves the objective by under 10^(-3) but the realized supply cost by up to 0.83 units, so the defect is suppressed by the objective weight rather than absent. Third, MILP-DA pairs the exact anchor with the corridor decode and a feasibility repair; compared against our published figures with no re-implementation on either side, it wins on thirteen of eighteen certified configurations and on all eighteen larger ones, every loss falling on a three-row grid.

</details>


### [121] [Stabilizer Design for Policy Iteration in Stochastic Linear Quadratic Control: A Spectrum-Assignment Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05953)
*Xinyu Cao,Bing-Chang Wang,Ying Cao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间不定随机线性二次控制问题，提出一种谱分配方法为无模型策略迭代（PI）寻找初始稳定控制器，并设计了不依赖初始稳定策略的无模型算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略迭代是求解最优控制问题的重要强化学习工具，但初始化阶段（寻找初始稳定控制器）通常依赖完整模型信息，这严重制约了无模型PI的应用。此外，对于含状态和控制依赖乘性噪声的随机系统，其稳定性由包含漂移项和扩散项的Lyapunov型不等式决定，而非确定性情形下的Hurwitz条件，这使得对应的无模型PI初始化问题更具挑战性。

Method: 提出一种新颖的谱分配方法：借助Lyapunov型算子的谱，通过调节累积因子使原系统从稳定辅助系统逐步逼近，从而获得稳定控制增益；进一步利用系统数据和累积因子的调节，设计了一个不依赖初始稳定策略的无模型算法。

Result: 通过仿真结果验证了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的谱分配方法和无模型算法为连续时间不定随机线性二次控制中的无模型策略迭代提供了可行的初始化方案，克服了对完整模型信息的依赖。

Abstract: Policy iteration (PI) is an important reinforcement learning tool for solving optimal control problems which includes an initialization stage, i.e., the search for an initial stabilizing controller. However, the initialization stage typically relies on complete model information, thereby imposing substantial constraints on the initialization of model-free PI. For stochastic systems with multiplicative noise dependent on state and control, the stability is not ensured by Hurwitz conditions as in the deterministic case, but rather by a Lyapunov-type inequality that incorporates both drift and diffusion terms. Therefore, the corresponding model-free PI initialization problem is more challenging. To this end, a novel spectrum assignment method is proposed to obtain an initial stabilizer for PI in continuous-time indefinite stochastic linear quadratic control. With the help of the Lyapunov-type operator's spectrum, the original system is gradually approximated from the stable auxiliary system by adjusting a cumulative factor, thereby obtaining a stabilizing control gain. Furthermore, by leveraging system data and adjusting the cumulative factor, we design a model-free algorithm that does not rely on an initial stabilizing policy and can achieve optimal control. Finally, simulation results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [122] [Distributed Fault Diagnosis in Discrete Event Systems with Transmission Delay Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06035)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究部分可观测离散事件系统中的分布式故障诊断问题，提出了一种新的分布式可诊断性条件，并设计了延迟记录器结构和新的诊断函数来验证该条件，能够应对传输延迟的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注分散式故障诊断，但在实际分布式系统中，代理之间传输信息时存在传输延迟，这会破坏原有的可诊断性保证。目前缺乏在传输延迟存在情况下验证分布式故障可诊断性的系统化方法，因此需要新的方法论来填补这一空白。

Method: 提出了一种新的分布式可诊断性条件，该条件扩展了现有文献中的分散式可诊断性条件。为了验证该条件，创新性地构建了一种名为"延迟记录器"（delay recorder）的结构，并设计了一个新的诊断函数（diagnosis function），通过这两者共同完成分布式可诊断性的验证。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的验证方法能够成功判定故障是否可以被诊断，即在存在传输延迟的情况下仍能正确判断系统是否满足分布式可诊断性。

Conclusion: 该工作为含传输延迟的部分可观测离散事件系统提供了一种有效的分布式故障诊断可行性验证框架，扩展了传统分散式可诊断性理论，使其能够适应更实际的通信约束场景。

Abstract: This note studies the distributed fault diagnosis problem in partially-observed discrete event systems, where the system is monitored by a group of agents to cooperatively diagnose faults within a finite number of steps. The novelty of this work is the creation of a methodology to verify when the faults can be diagnosed even in the presence of transmission delay impairments. To address this scenario, a new distributed diagnosability condition is proposed, which extends decentralized diagnosability conditions proposed in the literature. Such distributed diagnosability condition is then verified via a novel structure named delay recorder and a new diagnosis function. Theoretical analysis shows that the verification method can successfully determine whether the faults can be diagnosed.

</details>


### [123] [Muon on the Stiefel Manifold Admits an Exact Closed-Form Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06218)
*Mikhail Solonko,Molozhavenko Alexander,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon这一矩阵感知优化方法在Stiefel流形（由正交列矩阵构成）上的扩展。作者证明了该更新规则存在精确的闭式解，并据此开发了Skewon算法，同时为非凸光滑情形建立了保证一阶收敛的理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stiefel流形在机器学习和科学计算中广泛存在，但现有将Muon方法扩展到该流形的方案依赖于启发式、近似或迭代式更新，计算效率参差不齐。作者希望找到一种精确且高效的求解方式。

Method: 作者推导出Stiefel流形上Muon更新的精确闭式解，并基于此设计了Skewon算法，实现了针对正交约束优化问题的高效实现。同时在光滑非凸设定下提供了Skewon的一阶收敛性保证。

Result: 提出的Skewon算法具备高效的实现，避免了现有方法的启发式或近似缺陷，并获得了在光滑非凸情形下的首次一阶收敛性理论保证。

Conclusion: Skewon为Stiefel流形上的正交约束优化提供了一种精确、高效且具有理论收敛保证的新算法。

Abstract: We study Muon, a recently proposed matrix-aware optimization method, in the context of the Stiefel manifold. This manifold consists of matrices with orthonormal columns and is ubiquitous in machine learning and scientific computing. Existing extensions of Muon to this manifold rely on heuristic, approximate, or iterative updates with varying computational efficiency. We show that the corresponding Stiefel Muon update admits an exact closed-form solution and use this result to develop Skewon, a practical algorithm for orthogonality-constrained optimization with an efficient implementation. We further establish first-order convergence guarantees for Skewon in the smooth non-convex setting.

</details>


### [124] [New Lower Bounds for Weak Limited Augmented Zarankiewicz Numbers in the $m\times 3$ Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06050)
*Liqun Qi,Yannan Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文精确确定了弱限制增广Zarankiewicz数 z_{wL}(m,3) 在 m=5,6,7,8,9 时的值，均为 2m，并通过下界构造与上界有限搜索验证，强有力地支持了 z_{wL}(m,3)=2m（对所有 m≥5）的猜想，同时推导出 BSR(m,3)≥2m 的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了验证关于弱限制增广Zarankiewicz数 z_{wL}(m,3)=2m 的猜想，作者针对小规模情况（m=5到9）进行精确求解，为后续更大规模的证明奠定基础。

Method: 下界通过显式构造弱可容许（weak admissible）图获得；上界对于 m=7,8,9 则通过对典型的 C_4-free 基础图进行精确有限搜索来匹配下界。此外，利用 BSR(m,n)≥z_{wL}(m,n) 的不等式关系推导出 BSR 的下界。

Result: 得到 z_{wL}(5,3)=10, z_{wL}(6,3)=12, z_{wL}(7,3)=14, z_{wL}(8,3)=16, z_{wL}(9,3)=18，即 z_{wL}(m,3)=2m（5≤m≤9），并推出 BSR(m,3)≥2m。

Conclusion: 这些结果连同已知的小规模情形强有力地支持了 z_{wL}(m,3)=2m 对所有 m≥5 成立的猜想。

Abstract: We determine the exact weak limited augmented Zarankiewicz numbers $z_{wL}(m,3)$ for $m=5,6,7,8,9$. We establish \[ z_{wL}(5,3)=10,\quad z_{wL}(6,3)=12,\quad z_{wL}(7,3)=14,\quad z_{wL}(8,3)=16,\quad z_{wL}(9,3)=18. \] Thus $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ for all $5\le m\le 9$. The lower bounds are given by explicit weak admissible constructions, while the matching upper bounds for $m=7,8,9$ are obtained by exact finite searches over canonical $C_4$-free base graphs. Together with the known small cases, this strongly supports the conjecture that \[ z_{wL}(m,3)=2m \quad \text{for all } m\ge 5. \] Since BSR$(m,n)\ge z_{wL}(m,n)$, these constructions imply \[ {\rm BSR}(m,3)\ge 2m \quad \text{for } m=5,6,7,8,9. \]

</details>


### [125] [On Same-Sample and Independent-Sample Stochastic Extragradient for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06182)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了随机外梯度（SEG）方法在单调变分不等式问题（VIP）上的收敛性质，填补了同样本SEG（S-SEG）缺乏理论分析的空白，并揭示了S-SEG与独立样本SEG（I-SEG）在收敛性上的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管确定性外梯度算法在VIP上的收敛理论已很成熟，但其随机版本的收敛性理解仍不充分。现有研究大多局限于独立样本SEG（I-SEG），且需要紧致定义域或均匀有界方差的假设；而自然且具有本质上不同性质的同样本SEG（S-SEG）几乎未被关注。本文旨在填补这些理论空白。

Method: （1）证明S-SEG对样本Lipschitz参数敏感：仅有均值Lipschitz性和有界方差不足以保证收敛，即使在紧致集上也不收敛；（2）在放宽假设条件下，针对可能无界的定义域，为两种SEG变体建立高概率受限间隙（restricted-gap）收敛结果，并证明这些结果在一般情况下不能进一步改进；（3）展示一个已知对I-SEG保证几乎必然末次迭代收敛的非对称双步长选择，对S-SEG可能失效，构造了一个即使采用修正步长也几乎必然发散的随机单调VIP反例。

Result: （1）揭示了S-SEG与I-SEG在收敛性上的关键差异，明确了S-SEG收敛所需的更严格条件；（2）在松弛假设下为两种SEG变体建立了高概率受限间隙收敛定理；（3）构造了S-SEG在修正步长下仍几乎必然发散的随机单调VIP反例，证明了某些I-SEG的理论结果无法推广到S-SEG。

Conclusion: S-SEG和I-SEG在收敛性质上存在根本性差异，S-SEG需要比均值Lipschitz性和有界方差更强的条件才能保证收敛；而针对I-SEG设计的一些高效步长策略（如非对称双步长）不能直接迁移到S-SEG。这些发现有助于更深入理解随机VIP算法的收敛本质，并为后续算法设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: We study stochastic extragradient (SEG) methods for solving monotone variational inequality problems (VIPs) over a feasible set. Although extragradient is a foundational algorithm for VIPs and its deterministic convergence theory is well developed, its stochastic counterpart remains less understood. Most existing analyses focus on independent-sample SEG (I-SEG) and assume either that the domain is compact or that the variance of the stochastic operator is uniformly bounded. The behavior of same-sample SEG (S-SEG), a natural variant with materially different properties, has received far less attention. In this work, we address these gaps in the literature. We first show that S-SEG is sensitive to samplewise Lipschitz parameters: mean Lipschitzness and bounded variance alone do not ensure convergence, even on a compact set. Then, for possibly unbounded domains, we establish a high-probability restricted-gap convergence for each SEG variant under a relaxed set of assumptions, and show that certain fundamental improvements to these results are impossible in general. Finally, we show that a known asymmetric double step-size selection that guarantees almost sure last-iterate convergence for I-SEG can fail for S-SEG: there exists a stochastic monotone VIP for which S-SEG diverges almost surely even under the modified step-sizes.

</details>


### [126] [Characteristic Sensitivity Ensembles for Inference of Hidden Dynamics from Marginal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06190)
*Qi Wang,Gustaaf Jacobs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从观测到的边缘概率密度函数推断广义常微分方程组动力学参数的框架。通过将低维观测中的扩散等不可逆过程重铸为增强状态空间中确定性的可逆流动（满足Liouville方程），利用特征线表示和随机灵敏度传播，结合交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计器，实现随机梯度下降推断。四个实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际系统中的扩散及其他不可逆随机过程难以直接从低维观测中推断。传统方法需要求解高维Liouville方程，计算代价高昂。本文的核心动机是将随机/不可逆动力学的推断问题转化为确定性ODE的推断问题，从而绕过高维偏微分方程的求解困难，实现高效且可扩展的参数推断。

Method: 1）将噪声/不可逆过程通过状态空间扩展重铸为确定性可逆流，联合密度满足双曲Liouville方程，观测到的边缘分布是该双曲动力学在观测坐标上的投影；2）不直接求解高维Liouville方程，而是利用其特征线表示，从初始分布采样的粒子沿特征线输运；3）沿特征线传播欧拉灵敏度，并用交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计器，使随机梯度下降可行。

Result: 四个实验验证了方法：三模线性系统通过单模边缘观测恢复；非线性Gompertz生长模型含隐模式；双稳态系统的隐藏模式将单峰边缘分布变为双峰；cellular flow中粒子的Stokes-Oseen阻力定律恢复。所有设置下均对收敛行为进行了分析，验证了方法的有效性和收敛性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将不可逆/随机动力学的推断问题转化为确定性ODE的推断，通过特征线表示和基于交叉U统计量的无偏随机梯度方法克服了高维Liouville方程的计算瓶颈，在多种典型动力学系统上展示了鲁棒的参数恢复能力。

Abstract: A framework is developed for the inference of dynamics described by a generalized system of ordinary differential equations. A stochastic gradient method is coined that infers dynamics from observed marginal probability density functions using the joint probability density function of the observable and latent variables. Diffusion and other irreversible processes observed in a low-dimensional state can be recast as deterministic, reversible flows in a sufficiently augmented state space, where the joint density satisfies the hyperbolic Liouville equation. The marginal distribution observed is the projection of these hyperbolic dynamics onto the observed coordinates, with the latent components carrying the randomness and memory. This reframing allows inference for irreversible or stochastic dynamics into the recovery of a deterministic Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) from marginal observations. Instead of solving the high-dimensional Liouville equation for the joint density, the algorithm exploits its characteristic representation. Particles sampled from the initial distribution are transported along characteristic lines. The Eulerian sensitivity with respect to parameters is obtained by sensitivity propagation along the characteristic lines, with a crossed U-statistic producing an unbiased gradient estimator, which enables stochastic gradient descent. Four experiments validate the method: recovery of a three-mode linear system observed through the marginal of a single mode; a nonlinear Gompertz growth model with a hidden mode; a bistable system whose hidden mode turns a unimodal marginal bimodal; and Stokes--Oseen drag law recovery for particles in a cellular flow. Convergence behavior is analyzed across these settings.

</details>


### [127] [Localized Stabilization of Transport PDEs by Interior Flux Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06249)
*Constantinos Kitsos,Ian Manchester*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多维连续性方程（含源项）在有界域上的局部内部通量反馈镇定问题，提出有限时间特征阻尼不等式作为主要几何条件，建立了L2指数稳定性、Lyapunov型入口条件、输入到状态稳定性（ISS）估计，并扩展至非线性速度场情形，最后给出二维示例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多维连续性方程在有限区域上的稳定性控制问题，已有方法多依赖全局反馈，而局部内部反馈的设计与几何条件分析尚不充分。作者希望提供一种在局部区域施加通量反馈即可实现系统指数稳定的框架，并给出可验证的几何条件。

Method: 反馈通过通量的散度来施加，设计使得误差相对于参考廓型满足带局部阻尼的传输方程。提出有限时间特征阻尼不等式作为核心几何条件，要求相关特征在时间窗口内积累均匀数量的阻尼。结合增益条件（局部阻尼与压缩放大之间的权衡）推导L2指数稳定性；利用Lyapunov型入口条件保证支撑受限轨迹族上的特征阻尼；构建加权Lyapunov泛函获得微分Lyapunov判据和关于加性扰动的ISS估计。同时讨论通量反馈的椭圆右逆实现，并将特征阻尼论证推广到状态非线性依赖的速度场。

Result: 在增益条件满足时，误差在L2范数下指数稳定；加权Lyapunov泛函提供了微分形式的Lyapunov判据及ISS估计。二维算例验证了几何条件、增益条件和实现条件的可满足性。

Conclusion: 该研究为多维连续性方程提供了一套基于局部通量反馈的完整镇定理论，包含几何条件、Lyapunov判据、鲁棒性估计及非线性扩展，丰富了分布式参数系统反馈控制的工具箱。

Abstract: We study stabilization of multidimensional continu- ity equations with source terms on bounded domains by means of localized interior flux feedback. The feedback is prescribed through the divergence of the flux and is chosen so that the error with respect to a reference profile satisfies a transport equation with localized damping. The main geometric condition is a finite-time characteristic damping inequality, requiring relevant characteristics to accumulate a uniform amount of damping over a time horizon. This condition is shown to yield exponential stability in L2 of the error, under a gain condition relating localized damping to compressive amplification of the transport field. Lyapunov-type entrance conditions ensure characteris- tic damping on support-restricted families of trajectories. A weighted Lyapunov functional provides a differential Lyapunov criterion and an input-to-state (ISS) estimate with respect to additive perturbations. We also discuss elliptic right-inverse realizations of the feedback flux and extend the characteristic damping argument to velocity fields depending nonlinearly on the state. A two-dimensional example finally illustrates the geometric, gain, and realization conditions.

</details>


### [128] [Fault Diagnosis and Prognosis in Partially-Observed Discrete Event Systems with Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06295)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

TL;DR: 该论文研究了离散事件系统中观测延迟场景下的故障诊断与预测问题，提出了带延迟观测的诊断性和可预测性条件，并通过引入延迟观测器和新的验证函数进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际系统中，观测数据可能因通信延迟等原因无法即时获取，而现有的故障诊断与预测理论假设观测即时可用，导致现有方法在此场景下过于保守，需要修订。

Method: 提出了两个针对延迟观测的故障诊断与预测条件，指出并修订了现有可预测性（prognosability）概念以避免保守性；通过引入延迟观测器（delay observer）和新的验证函数来验证所提出的诊断性与可预测性条件。

Result: 理论分析表明所提出的验证方法在存在观测延迟的离散事件系统中对故障诊断与预测是有效的。

Conclusion: 该研究为延迟观测场景下的离散事件系统故障诊断与预测提供了完整的理论框架和验证方法，修正了现有可预测性概念的保守性缺陷。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis and prognosis in discrete event systems are studied in the scenario where the observations are possibly received with delay. To address this scenario, two conditions for diagnosis and prognosis with delayed observations are proposed, where we show that the state-of-the-art notion of prognosability must be revised to avoid conservativeness. Diagnosability and prognosability conditions are then verified by introducing a delay observer and a new verification function. Theoretical analysis indicates the effectiveness of the verification method for fault diagnosis and prognosis in the system.

</details>


### [129] [The Benefits of an Integrated Approach for Stability-Constrained Power System Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06349)
*Gereon Recht,Benedikt Jahn,Oussama Alaya,Karl-Kiên Cao,Hendrik Lens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究电力系统规划中的集成方法，将稳定措施（惯量和电压稳定约束）与发电、输电、储能扩建同时考虑，发现该方法可降低系统成本，且电网构成型电池储能系统的双重用途优于静态同步补偿器等稳定措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变器资源在电力系统中渗透率不断提高，需要替代同步发电机对动态电压稳定和惯性响应的贡献。然而传统的电力系统规划方法是顺序式的，稳定措施仅在规划后期才被考虑，这可能导致次优的规划结果。因此需要探索集成式规划方法的优势。

Method: 采用集成式电力系统规划方法，通过简化的稳定性约束（惯量约束和电压稳定约束），将稳定措施与发电、输电、储能系统的扩建同时进行优化，避免顺序式规划的局限性。

Result: 集成式规划方法能够降低系统成本；在各类稳定措施中，电网构成型电池储能系统的双重用途（同时提供惯量支持和电压稳定）相较静态同步补偿器等单一用途措施更具经济优势，因此更受青睐。

Conclusion: 集成式电力系统规划方法优于传统顺序式方法，且电网构成型电池储能系统的双重用途使其成为最具成本效益的稳定措施选择，为高比例逆变器资源电力系统的规划提供了新思路。

Abstract: Increasing penetration of inverter-based resources in today's power systems requires substitution of the contribution from synchronous generators to dynamic voltage stability and inertial response. However, established approaches for power system planning are sequential, as stabilising measures are only considered at a later stage of the planning process. We investigate the advantages of an integrated approach for power system planning, where stabilising measures are considered simultaneously with the expansion of generation, transmission, and storage systems via simplified stability constraints on inertia and voltage stability. We find that system costs are reduced with the integrated approach and that the dual-use option of grid-forming battery energy storage systems is favoured over other stabilising measures like static synchronous compensators.

</details>
