### [130] [Superconducting and charge-ordered phases from Dirac quantum spin liquids on the triangular lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05277)
*Andreas Feuerpfeil,Ronny Thomale,Subir Sachdev,Pietro M. Bonetti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用理论框架，解释三角晶格量子自旋液体绝缘体在压力或掺杂下转变为超导态，以及在中红外光驱动下太赫兹电导增强的现象。该框架基于U(1) Dirac自旋液体的费米子自旋子与规范场的耦合，通过自旋子电荷子的Higgs凝聚实现向超导和电荷有序相的转变，并产生d+id超导、电荷密度波、键密度波和配对密度波等丰富相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角晶格量子自旋液体绝缘体在实验上观察到压力/掺杂诱导的超导转变和中红外光驱动下的太赫兹电导增强，但缺乏统一的理论框架来解释这些现象。现有数值研究虽提供了自旋液体态的证据，但需要理论上的统一描述来阐明自旋液体与超导/电荷有序相之间的转变机制。

Method: 作者构建了从U(1) Dirac自旋液体（及其带隙的Z2和手性衍生态）出发的通用理论框架。核心方法包括：(1) 自旋液体相包含与涌现规范场耦合的分裂化Dirac自旋子；(2) 通过无自旋电荷e的玻色子chargons（doublons和holons）的Higgs凝聚驱动相变；(3) 利用Dirac自旋子的投影对称群唯一确定chargons的对称性和色散关系；(4) 构建chargon能带极小值附近的有效低能理论，并用规范不变的chargon Higgs场复合算符作为序参量描述各相。

Result: 该框架生成了一个丰富的相图，包含多种有序态：d+id超导、电荷密度波、键密度波和配对密度波等。这些有序相是常规相，既没有分裂化激发也没有涌现规范动力学，与自旋液体相形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 该理论框架统一解释了三角晶格量子自旋液体在压力、掺杂或光驱动下向超导/电荷有序相的转变，为理解相关实验现象提供了理论基础，并预测了丰富的竞争有序相。

Abstract: Triangular-lattice quantum spin-liquid insulators are observed to undergo transitions to superconductivity under pressure or doping, and exhibit enhanced terahertz conductivity when driven by mid-infrared light. We present a general theoretical framework for the emergence of superconducting and charge-ordered phases from a U(1) Dirac spin liquid with fermionic spinons, as well as from its gapped $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and chiral descendants. Numerical studies have provided substantial evidence for these spin-liquid states.
  The spin-liquid phase hosts fractionalized Dirac spinons coupled to an emergent gauge field, whereas the superconducting and charge-ordered phases are conventional, with neither fractionalized excitations nor emergent gauge dynamics. The transition between these phases is driven by the Higgs condensation of spinless charge-$e$ bosonic chargons (``doublons'' and ``holons''). We show that the projective symmetry group of the Dirac spinons uniquely determines the symmetry and dispersion of the chargons, allowing us to construct an effective low-energy theory near the chargon band minima. Gauge-invariant composites of the chargon Higgs fields provide the order parameters characterizing the phases. The resulting phase diagram contains a rich variety of ordered states, including $d+id$ superconductivity, charge-density waves, bond-density waves, and pair-density waves.

</details>


### [131] [Self-dual $S_3$ gauge theory in 2+1d: lattice model and topological phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05294)
*Da-Chuan Lu,Chong Wang,Ashvin Vishwanath*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构造了第一个非阿贝尔版本的电磁自对偶格点模型：在S₃量子双D(S₃)的张量积希尔伯特空间上，通过格点平移实现交换非阿贝尔电荷子C和磁通子F的Z₂ᵉᵐ任意子置换对称性。该模型的锯齿边界终止无微调地实现由四临界Ising CFT描述的无能隙临界边缘态。体态存在三个独立的保持Z₂ᵉᵐ的玻色子微扰，驱动D(S₃)进入不同的有能隙相，作者通过任意子凝聚、微观格点哈密顿量和Chern-Simons-Higgs理论三种独立方法分析这些相变并得到统一图景。该构造可推广到无穷族的二面体量子双D(D₂ₙ)，且每个模型无符号问题，可用于非阿贝尔Chern-Simons-Higgs理论的大规模数值研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Z₂规范理论的电磁自对偶（可通过半格点平移实现交换电荷和磁通）是精确格点实现的对偶对称性的标志性例子，但其非阿贝尔推广此前一直缺失。作者旨在构建第一个非阿贝尔格点模型，将这一任意子置换对称性以格点平移的形式精确实现，从而为研究非阿贝尔量子双重模型中的对偶对称性及其引发的相变提供微观基础。

Method: 作者在D(S₃)量子双模型上构造了一个以格点平移实现Z₂ᵉᵐ任意子置换对称性的非阿贝尔格点模型。通过三种独立方法——范畴论任意子凝聚、微观格点哈密顿量分析、Chern-Simons-Higgs理论——分析三个独立的Z₂ᵉᵐ保持玻色子微扰所驱动的相变。此外，构造可推广至无穷族的自对偶二面体量子双D(D₂ₙ)模型。

Result: 得到了三个核心结果：（1）锯齿边界终止自然实现四临界Ising CFT描述的无能隙临界边缘态；（2）三种方法（任意子凝聚、格点哈密顿量、CS-Higgs理论）对三个独立微扰驱动的相变给出完全一致的统一描述，支持了"最小凝聚原理"：增殖一个玻色子任意子通常驱动包含它的最小可凝聚代数的凝聚，而对称性相关的凝聚产物以简并真空出现并自发破缺任意子置换对称性；（3）构造推广到无穷族自对偶二面体量子双模型，且所有模型无符号问题。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了电磁自对偶从阿贝尔到非阿贝尔推广的空白，建立了任意子凝聚、格点微观哈密顿量与CS-Higgs理论之间的统一图景，并提出最小凝聚原理作为一般性指导原则。无符号问题的特性为将来大规模数值探索非阿贝尔Chern-Simons-Higgs理论的相图铺平了道路。

Abstract: Electric-magnetic self-duality of the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory, realized microscopically as a half-lattice-translation exchanging electric charge and magnetic flux, has been an influential example of a duality symmetry with an exact lattice realization. We construct the first non-Abelian generalization of this construction: a lattice model of the $S_3$ quantum double $\mathcal{D}(S_3)$ on a tensor product Hilbert space in which the $\mathbb{Z}^{\mathrm{em}}_2$ anyon-permutation symmetry, exchanging the non-Abelian chargeon $C$ and fluxon $F$, is realized via lattice translation. Consequently we find that the zigzag boundary termination of the model realizes, without fine-tuning, a gapless critical edge state described by the tetracritical Ising CFT. The bulk admits three independent $\mathbb{Z}_2^{\mathrm{em}}$-preserving bosonic perturbations, driving $\mathcal{D}(S_3)$ into distinct gapped phases. We analyze these transitions by three independent methods: category-theoretic anyon condensation, microscopic lattice Hamiltonians, and Chern-Simons-Higgs theory, which all agree, yielding a unified picture. These examples motivate a minimal-condensation principle: proliferating a bosonic anyon generically drives condensation of a minimal condensable algebra containing it, with symmetry-related condensates appearing as degenerate vacua that spontaneously break the anyon-permutation symmetry. Our model construction extends to an infinite family of self-dual dihedral quantum doubles $\mathcal{D}(D_{2n})$. Notably, each model is sign-problem-free, opening the door to large-scale numerical exploration of the phases of non-Abelian Chern-Simons-Higgs theories.

</details>


### [132] [Strongly Enhanced Charge-Density Waves and Correlated Insulating State in Atomically Thin 1$T$-TaS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05532)
*Gan Liu,Yulu Liu,Qiling Luo,Zhentao Huang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Meiyu Wang,Jinsheng Wen,Yi Lu,Xiaoxiang Xi*

TL;DR: 本文通过变温拉曼光谱和电输运测量研究了1T-TaS₂中依赖于厚度的电荷密度波（CDW）转变，发现三种CDW相可持续到单层极限，随厚度减薄转变温度升高、方阻增大、载流子局域长度缩短，且单层中独特的CCDW-NCCDW一阶滞回转变消失，计算表明增强的CDW源于面外屏蔽减弱导致的库仑相互作用增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解原子级薄层1T-TaS₂中CDW转变如何随厚度演化，以及电子关联、电子-声子相互作用和层间耦合在其中的协同作用，为二维CDW系统中关联相的工程调控提供依据。

Method: 采用变温拉曼光谱和电输运测量研究不同厚度1T-TaS₂的CDW转变，并结合理论计算分析CDW增强的机理。

Result: 三种CDW相（非公度、近公度、公度）在单层极限下仍然存在；随厚度减薄，转变温度升高，方阻提高多个数量级，载流子局域长度显著减小；单层中CCDW-NCCDW一阶滞回转变消失；理论计算显示CDW增强源于面外屏蔽减弱导致的库仑相互作用（尤其是非局域部分）增强。

Conclusion: 原子级薄1T-TaS₂中CDW的增强和转变动力学的改变源于电子关联、电子-声子相互作用与层间耦合的协同作用，为二维CDW系统中关联相的调控开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate thickness-dependent charge-density-wave (CDW) transitions in 1$T$-TaS$_2$ using temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy and electrical transport. Raman measurements show that the incommensurate, nearly commensurate, and commensurate CDW phases persist down to the monolayer limit. As the thickness is reduced, the transition temperatures increase, accompanied by an orders-of-magnitude rise in sheet resistance and a sharp reduction in the carrier localization length. The first-order hysteretic CCDW-NCCDW transition is uniquely absent in the monolayer. Calculations suggest that the enhanced CDW in thin layers originates from strengthened Coulomb interactions due to reduced out-of-plane screening, particularly in the nonlocal component. These findings highlight the cooperative roles of electron correlation, electron-phonon interaction, and interlayer coupling in shaping the ground state and transition dynamics of atomically thin 1$T$-TaS$_2$, opening pathways for engineering correlated phases in two-dimensional CDW systems.

</details>


### [133] [Local Spin Excitations Mediate Quasiparticle Breakdown in the Orbital-Selective Mott Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05556)
*Yuekun Niu,Yu Ni,Jia-Ming Wang,Zhong-Yi Lu,Yun Song,Shiping Feng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用动力学平均场理论（DMFT）研究双带Hubbard模型，揭示Hund耦合的自旋翻转和Ising分量产生局域自旋激发（LSEs），这些LSEs耦合不同轨道之间的电子，导致轨道选择Mott相（OSMP）中准粒子破坏，这是该现象的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道选择Mott相（OSMP）传统上被描述为有效解耦轨道内局域电子与巡游电子共存，但新出现的准粒子破坏证据表明存在超出这一图景的物理，其微观起源尚不清楚。

Method: 采用动力学平均场理论（DMFT）对双带Hubbard模型进行计算分析，通过对比移除Hund耦合中不同分量前后的行为，系统研究准粒子破坏的机制。

Result: 发现Hund耦合的自旋翻转和Ising分量产生局域自旋激发（LSEs），这些激发在轨道间耦合电子，重整化准粒子寿命和束缚能，从而破坏OSMP中良好定义的准粒子；移除这些分量后恢复相干准粒子行为并使两带电荷动力学完全解耦。

Conclusion: 电子与局域自旋激发的耦合是驱动OSMP内准粒子破坏的根本机制。

Abstract: The orbital-selective Mott phase (OSMP) is commonly described as a coexistence of localized and itinerant electrons within effectively decoupled orbitals, but emerging evidence for quasiparticle breakdown points to physics beyond this picture, whose microscopic origin remains unknown. Using dynamical mean-field theory for the two-band Hubbard model, we show that the spin-flip and Ising-type components of Hund's coupling generate local spin excitations (LSEs). These LSEs couple electrons between different orbitals, renormalize quasiparticle lifetimes and binding energies, and thereby destroy well-defined quasiparticles in the OSMP. Removing these two components of Hund's coupling restores coherent quasiparticle behavior and fully decouples the charge dynamics of the two bands. Our results therefore identify electronic coupling to LSEs as the fundamental mechanism driving quasiparticle breakdown within the OSMP.

</details>


### [134] [Helical-to-Fan Transitions under Magnetic Fields in the Noncentrosymmetric Tetragonal Magnet EuRhGe$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05709)
*Takeshi Matsumura,Hiroto Nitta,Hironori Nakao,Masashi Kakihana,Masato Hedo,Takao Nakama,Yoshichika Ōnuki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过共振X射线衍射研究了非中心对称体心四方磁体EuRhGe₃的磁结构。在TN=12 K以下，该材料呈现非公度螺旋磁有序（q=(0,0,0.809)），磁矩位于ab平面内，层间旋转角为145.8°。施加磁场后，材料依次经历圆形螺旋→螺旋磁孤子晶格→q=0.8的公度锁定→自旋翻转xyz-fan（椭圆锥形）→平面xy-fan的完整场致结构转变序列，为理论预测提供了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 螺旋磁体在场致结构转变中的完整演化序列（从圆形螺旋到自旋翻转xyz-fan再到平面xy-fan）虽然在理论上已被预测，但缺乏实验证据。EuRhGe₃作为非中心对称体心四方磁体，可能为验证这一理论预测提供理想平台。

Method: 采用共振X射线衍射技术，在磁场沿a轴方向施加的条件下，系统研究EuRhGe₃在不同温度和磁场下的磁结构演化，包括非公度传播矢量、二次谐波2q峰、场致锁定转变及不同磁相的结构特征。

Result: 在零场下观察到q=(0,0,0.809)的非公度螺旋磁序，层间旋转角为145.8°。施加磁场后：(1) 2 K、3.8 T发生q=0.8的公度锁定转变（伴随2q谐波出现，表明螺旋畸变为孤子晶格态）；(2) 5 T以上进入自旋翻转xyz-fan（椭圆锥形）态，磁矩主要沿b轴振荡并伴随微小c轴分量；(3) 更高场强下进入无c轴分量的平面xy-fan态。

Conclusion: EuRhGe₃首次完整呈现了理论预测的场致磁结构转变序列：圆形螺旋→螺旋磁孤子晶格→公度结构→自旋翻转xyz-fan（椭圆锥形）→平面xy-fan，是该理论框架下的典型实验范例。

Abstract: The magnetic structure of EuRhGe$_3$, a noncentrosymmetric body-centered tetragonal magnet with the space group $I4mm$, has been investigated by resonant X-ray diffraction. Below $T_{\text{N}}=12$ K, EuRhGe$_3$ undergoes a helical magnetic ordering with an incommensurate propagation vector $q=(0, 0, 0.809)$, in which the magnetic moments lie in the $ab$ plane and rotate by a constant turn angle of $145.8^{\circ}$ between adjacent layers. When a magnetic field is applied along the $a$ axis at 2 K, a second-harmonic $2q$ peak develops, indicating that the circular helix is gradually distorted into a helimagnetic soliton-lattice state, which eventually undergoes a lock-in transition to the commensurate structure with $q=0.8$ at 3.8 T. Above the subsequent phase boundary at 5 T, the helicity is lost, and a spin-flop $xyz$-fan (elliptic conical) state is realized, in which the moments oscillate predominantly along the $b$ axis but are accompanied by a small $c$-axis component. At higher fields, the system enters a conventional planar $xy$-fan phase without a $c$-axis component. EuRhGe$_3$ provides a prototypical example of a helimagnet that exhibits a full sequence of field-induced structures, evolving from a circular helix to a spin-flop $xyz$-fan (elliptic conical), and finally to a planar $xy$-fan structure, which has been theoretically predicted.

</details>


### [135] [Correlated topological-polarization surface states in the narrow-gap insulator FeSb2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05887)
*Takahiro Iwagaki,Hideki Matsuoka,Ginta Hoshino,Kanata Watanabe,Shungo Aoyagi,Shunsuke Kitou,Yuiga Nakamura,Motoaki Hirayama,Takashi Koretsune,Naoya Kanazawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究在窄带隙绝缘体FeSb2外延薄膜中证实了由拓扑极化起源的金属极性表面态，并揭示了强关联体相与表面态的体-边对应关系；通过静电门控可将该关联表面驱动至铁磁或可能的交错磁量子相变，确立拓扑极性作为关联拓扑相的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子关联与能带拓扑各自能产生丰富的量子相，但由于元素需求相互冲突，二者长期以来难以在同一材料中统一。作者希望通过拓扑极化这一途径将二者结合，在不依赖自旋轨道耦合的情况下，将能带拓扑扩展到关联的3d过渡金属化合物中。

Method: 制备FeSb2外延薄膜，观察其金属极性表面态（源自拓扑极化）；通过非互易表面输运测量探测体相Fe 3d轨道占据的关联驱动重构；利用静电门控调控关联表面态，驱动其穿过量子相变。

Result: 在FeSb2薄膜中观察到金属极性表面态，非互易表面输运仅在体相Fe 3d轨道占据发生关联驱动重构的起始温度以下出现，证明关联拓扑系统中的体-边对应关系；静电门控将关联表面驱动至铁磁或可能的交错磁态。

Conclusion: 拓扑极化可作为设计关联拓扑相的一般性设计原则，为在广泛材料中实现关联拓扑相提供了新途径。

Abstract: Strong electron correlations and band topology each generate rich quantum phases, but conflicting elemental requirements have largely kept them apart. Topological polarization offers a route to unite them, producing polar surface states from bonding charge without spin-orbit coupling and thereby extending band topology to correlated 3d transition-metal compounds. Here we demonstrate that epitaxial thin films of the narrow-gap insulator FeSb2 host metallic polar surface states of topological-polarization origin, governed by the strong correlations of the bulk. Nonreciprocal surface transport emerges only below the onset temperature of a correlation-driven reconstruction of the bulk Fe 3d orbital occupation, providing direct evidence of bulk-edge correspondence in a correlated topological system. Moreover, electrostatic gating drives this correlated surface across a quantum phase transition into a ferromagnetic or possibly altermagnetic state. Our results establish topological polarization as a design principle for correlated topological phases in a broad range of materials.

</details>


### [136] [Landscape of incompressible crystals of hard-core bosons on the square-kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05927)
*Pratyay Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文利用超越常规单胞描述的分层平均场理论研究方形kagome晶格上的硬核玻色子模型，揭示了常规单胞方法仅能捕捉5/6和2/3两种密度下的相，而通过扩大变分团簇还能发现包括ρ=3/4在内的额外不可压缩态。ρ=3/4相经Matsubara-Matsuda映射对应于自旋1/2 XXZ模型中的半磁化平台，且该方法在两种实验相关的方形kagome化合物上的计算结果与实验吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于最小单胞的描述无法完整刻画方形kagome晶格所支持的不可压缩态层次结构，需要发展超越常规单胞的方法来更全面地揭示该晶格体系中的物相。

Method: 采用超越常规单胞描述的分层平均场理论（hierarchical mean-field theory），通过扩大变分团簇（variational cluster）来研究方形kagome晶格上的硬核玻色子模型，并利用Matsubara-Matsuda映射将玻色子结果转换到自旋1/2 XXZ模型，结合第一性原理计算的交换参数应用于实验相关的材料。

Result: 常规单胞方法仅能捕捉密度为5/6和2/3的两个基于紧凑局域态的相；扩大变分团簇后发现了额外的不可压缩态，其中ρ=3/4相尤为稳健，对应自旋模型的半磁化平台。在实验材料中的应用与现有实验结果吻合，并预测了新的磁化平台结构。

Conclusion: 超越常规单胞的分层平均场方法能更完整地揭示方形kagome晶格体系中不可压缩态的层次结构，为理解相关自旋材料的磁化平台现象提供了更准确的理论框架，并具有实验预测能力。

Abstract: We investigate a hard-core boson model on the square-kagome lattice using hierarchical mean-field theory beyond the conventional unit-cell description. We show that the conventional description of the square-kagome lattice based on its smallest unit cell does not fully capture the hierarchy of incompressible states supported by the lattice, but only captures the two compact-localized-state-based phases at densities $5/6$ and $2/3$. Within our approach, we reproduce these previously established phases and uncover additional incompressible states enabled by enlarging the variational cluster. Among these, the $ρ=3/4$ phase is found to be particularly robust, which, through the Matsubara-Matsuda mapping, corresponds to a half-magnetization plateau in the spin-$1/2$ XXZ model. We further apply our approach to two experimentally relevant square-kagome compounds using exchange parameters obtained from first-principles calculations. The calculated magnetization processes are in good agreement with available experimental results and predict additional plateau structures in these materials.

</details>


### [137] [Dissipation-induced bulk and boundary criticality in the Haldane chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05984)
*Zhenjiu Wang,Manuel Weber*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟研究了与耗散欧姆热库耦合的Haldane链，发现了具有自发破缺SO(3)对称性、由动力学指数z≈2的相互作用不动点控制的二级量子相变，并推导出广义弦序参量证明SPT基态在弱耗散下稳定、在临界点具有非平凡标度维度。文章还发现拓扑边缘模表现出与平庸态相变不同的边界临界性，并通过耗散φ^4理论的ε展开进行了精确分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解对称性保护拓扑(SPT)相在开放量子系统中的稳定性与临界行为，探索耗散如何调控边界临界性，这是将拓扑物质概念推广到非平衡和耗散量子系统的重要一步。

Method: 大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟研究Haldane链与耗散欧姆热库的耦合系统；推导广义弦序参量刻画SPT态稳定性；对平庸态等价转变采用耗散φ^4理论结合ε展开进行解析研究。

Result: 发现一个由z≈2的相互作用不动点控制的二级量子相变，进入自发破缺SO(3)对称性的反铁磁态；SPT基态在弱耗散下稳定，临界点处弦序参量具有非平凡标度维度；拓扑边缘模展现与平庸态转变不同的独特边界临界性。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了临界但非共形的反铁磁体中的自旋杂质，为通过耗散连续可调边界临界性开辟了道路，展示了耗散作为控制量子临界和拓扑行为新维度的潜力。

Abstract: We study the Haldane chain coupled to a dissipative ohmic bath. Using large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we identify a second-order quantum phase transition into an antiferromagnetic state with spontaneously broken SO(3) symmetry that is governed by an interacting fixed point with dynamical exponent $z\approx 2$. We derive a generalized string order parameter which indicates that the symmetry-protected topological ground state of the Haldane chain is stable for weak dissipation and develops a nontrivial scaling dimension at criticality. In particular, its topological edge modes display nontrivial boundary criticality that is distinct from an equivalent transition out of a trivial state; for the latter, we perform an accurate $ε$ expansion of a dissipative $φ^4$ theory with ordinary boundary conditions. Our setup realizes a spin impurity in a critical yet nonconformal antiferromagnet and paves the way for continuously tunable boundary criticality controlled by dissipation.

</details>


### [138] [Resilient strange metal at an unconventional quantum critical point in $d=2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05988)
*Jérôme Leblanc,A. -M. S. Tremblay*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用改进的双粒子自洽（TPSC）方法研究二维Hubbard模型在量子临界点附近的奇异金属行为，发现即使低于Mott转变，在费米液体与非公度自旋密度波序转变的量子临界点上，由Kohn反常导致的非传统临界指数使得电阻率呈线性温度依赖，但该行为并不需要沿费米面的线性温度自能（与常规假设不同）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷原子实验和图示量子蒙特卡罗方法对具有最近邻跳跃的二维Hubbard模型进行了可控研究，因此有必要对奇异金属行为及其与量子临界性质的关系做出理论预测，以指导实验和计算。

Method: 采用非微扰改进的双粒子自洽（improved two-particle self-consistent, TPSC）方法，研究相互作用强度低于Mott转变时，费米液体与非公度自旋密度波序之间的量子临界点附近的物理性质。

Result: 在关联长度约1至100个晶格间距范围内，费米面上的Kohn反常导致非传统的临界指数；单粒子自能的温度依赖沿费米面具有强烈的动量依赖，且无清晰的Landau准粒子证据；电阻率随温度线性变化（T→0），但并未伴随沿费米面的线性温度自能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，线性温度依赖的电阻率并不一定要求沿费米面的自能呈线性温度依赖（这是以往常被假设的条件），为理解奇异金属行为提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Properties of the two-dimensional Hubbard model with nearest-neighbor hopping are under scrutiny in cold-atom experiments and diagrammatic quantum Monte Carlo. Given the controlled nature of these approaches, it is timely to make predictions about strange-metal behavior and its relation to quantum-critical properties. Even for interaction strengths below the Mott transition, several peculiarities occur at the quantum critical point separating Fermi liquid and incommensurate spin-density wave order. Here, we predict, using the non-perturbative improved two-particle self-consistent approach, that for correlation lengths ranging from about one to one hundred lattice spacings, Kohn anomalies on the underlying Fermi surface lead to unconventional critical exponents. The temperature dependence of the single-particle self-energy acquires strong momentum dependence along the Fermi surface with no clear evidence of Landau quasiparticles. Nevertheless, we observe strange-metal behavior, namely, resistivity that scales linearly with temperature as $T\rightarrow{0}$. Our work shows that a linear temperature dependence of resistivity arises without a linear-in-temperature self-energy along the Fermi surface, as is often assumed.

</details>


### [139] [Neural Flux Attachment: From Bose Condensates to Chiral Topological Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06168)
*Rudik Badalyan,Khachatur G. Nazaryan,Tigran A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出ChernFormer，一种结合费米子变压器与固定Chern-Simons相位的神经网络波函数，能够同时描述玻色凝聚体和手性拓扑液体。其固定相位改变统计但不改变概率，使得玻色子学习问题等价于费米子问题。在Kalmeyer-Laughlin基态及前两个手性边缘态的基准测试中表现优异，重叠曲线接近1，并能恢复Laughlin涡旋和集体边缘涡旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一神经波函数是否能同时描述玻色凝聚体与手性拓扑液体这一根本性问题。传统方法难以在统一的变分框架中处理常规玻色序和手性拓扑物质。

Method: ChernFormer将费米子变压器与固定的Chern-Simons相位相结合，为每对粒子附加一个统计涡旋。每个因子在交换下改变符号，二者乘积严格为玻色子。固定相位不改变概率，从而使玻色子学习问题在相同近似误差和重叠下等价于费米子问题。足够容量下可近似平面上任意固定粒子数的归一化玻色子波函数。

Result: 在Kalmeyer-Laughlin基态和两个手性边缘态的测试中，重叠曲线在所有最大采样尺寸下保持接近1。在N=20时，独立幅度和相位图恢复了局部Laughlin涡旋和集体边缘涡旋。而连续、非零的相同粒子因子乘积则遗漏了这些基本边缘扇区。

Conclusion: ChernFormer提供了一种统一的变分语言，能够同时描述常规玻色序和手性拓扑物质，填补了神经波函数在该领域的空白。

Abstract: Can one neural wave function describe both a Bose condensate and a chiral topological liquid? We introduce ChernFormer, which combines a fermionic transformer with a fixed Chern-Simons phase that attaches one statistical vortex to every particle pair. Each factor changes sign under exchange, so their product is exactly bosonic. The fixed phase changes statistics but not probability, making every bosonic learning problem equivalent to a fermionic one with the same approximation error and overlap. With enough capacity, ChernFormer can approximate any normalizable bosonic wave function on the plane at fixed particle number. A finite, smooth network still vanishes when particles meet, yet this contact hole can shrink while the wave function and condensate fraction approach those of a nodeless condensate. Following the needle-in-a-haystack target-reconstruction benchmark introduced in \cite{NazaryanGaggioliTengFu2025}, we test ChernFormer on the Kalmeyer--Laughlin ground state and its first two chiral edge states. The overlap curves stay close to unity through their largest sampled sizes, while independent amplitude and phase maps at $N=20$ for all three states recover both local Laughlin vortices and the collective edge vortex. By contrast, a continuous, nonzero product of identical particle-wise factors misses these elementary edge sectors. ChernFormer therefore provides one variational language for conventional bosonic order and chiral topological matter.

</details>


### [140] [Phonon spectral functions of low-density polaron metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06357)
*Luis Walther,Alberto Nocera,Mona Berciu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用DMRG计算一维自旋less Holstein模型在低但有限载流子浓度（x≤0.15）下的声子谱函数，发现声子谱权重从裸声子能量Ω向低频（低至ω=0）和高频两侧转移，与Migdal极限中Kohn反常的预期形成鲜明对比，且无2k_F扭结特征。RPA定性捕捉该行为，而“dressed RPA”方案（用动量平均近似重正化电子增量传播子）在几乎零额外计算成本下半定量复现结果；但加入最低阶顶点修正会产生非物理的负谱权重，表明在非微扰地dressing传播子时，顶点和传播子修正必须自洽处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低密度极化子金属中声子谱函数的研究此前仅局限于单极化子极限（x→0），而有限掺杂条件下（x≤0.15）的声子谱行为尚属空白。理解该区域的声子谱对揭示弱掺杂绝缘体的物理至关重要，且现有Migdal极限的Kohn反常预期可能不适用于该密度范围，因此需要系统地计算并发展高效近似方案。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）数值计算一维自旋less Holstein模型中有限低载流子浓度下的声子谱函数；随后基于随机相位近似（RPA）提出“dressed RPA”方案，将电子增量传播子用动量平均（MA）近似重正化，以半定量复现DMRG结果；并检验加入最低阶顶点修正后的效果。

Result: 发现声子谱权重显著转移到ω=0以下和裸声子能量Ω以上两个区域，完全不同于Migdal极限中谱权重集中于Ω附近且q=2k_F处出现扭结的Kohn反常图像；2k_F扭结特征完全缺失。RPA定性捕捉该行为，dressed RPA在几乎零额外计算成本下半定量吻合；而加入最低阶顶点修正导致非物理的负谱权重。

Conclusion: dressed RPA方案为低密度极化子金属的声子谱函数提供了高效且可靠的近似工具；研究还揭示了在非微扰dressing传播子的框架下，顶点修正与传播子修正必须一致处理，否则会产生非物理结果；该发现对弱掺杂绝缘体的声子性质研究具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: We use the density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) to compute the phonon spectral function of a one-dimensional spinless Holstein model doped with a low but finite carrier concentration, $x \leq 0.15$, as a function of the electron-phonon coupling $λ$. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first such results in this regime, complementing extensive prior work at the single-polaron level ($x\to 0$). We find that significant phonon spectral weight is transferred both below, all the way down to $ω=0$, and above the bare phonon energy $Ω$, in stark contrast with the Kohn-anomaly phenomenology expected in the Migdal limit, where weight remains centered near $Ω$ with a kink at $q=2k_F$. No signature of this $2k_F$ kink appears in our results. This behavior is captured qualitatively by the Random Phase Approximation (RPA), and semi-quantitatively, at negligible extra computational cost, by a ``dressed RPA'' scheme in which the electron addition propagator is renormalized using the Momentum Average (MA) approximation for the low-density electron-polaron. By contrast, adding the lowest-order vertex correction to this dressed scheme produces unphysical negative spectral weight, signaling that vertex and propagator dressings must be treated consistently once the propagators are dressed nonperturbatively. Our results provide an efficient approximation for the phonon spectral functions of low-density polaron metals, a regime relevant to weakly doped insulators.

</details>
